### [1] [Wake Up to the Past: Using Memory to Model Fluid Wake Effects on Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22472)
*Luca Vendruscolo,Eduardo Sebastián,Amanda Prorok,Ajay Shankar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了流体尾流效应对相邻机器人扰动的预测问题，通过实证分析发现支持历史状态输入和传输延迟预测能显著提高尾流效应预测器的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空中和水下机器人（如多旋翼飞行器和鱼雷）通过扰动介质产生尾流效应，这些尾流效应对相邻机器人构成扰动。由于流体的混沌时空动力学特性、机器人物理几何形状及其复杂运动模式的相互纠缠，尾流效应难以建模和预测。传统数据驱动方法通常学习将两个机器人当前状态映射到"受害"机器人所受力的无记忆函数，在敏捷场景中表现不佳，因为尾流效应具有有限传播时间。

Method: 提出了对尾流效应预测器所需特性的实证研究。探索了七种数据驱动模型，旨在捕捉四种不同介质中流体尾流效应的时空演化。开发了平面直线龙门架系统，用于两个旋转单旋翼飞行器的真实世界数据测试和反馈控制验证。

Result: 通过模型内省和分析，发现支持先前状态历史作为输入和传输延迟预测的特征能够显著提高预测器在不同预测器和流体中的准确性。实验验证表明，包含历史状态输入和考虑传输延迟的模型在尾流效应预测方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 尾流效应预测器的准确性关键在于支持历史状态输入和传输延迟预测。这些特性使模型能够更好地捕捉尾流效应的时空演化特性，从而在敏捷场景中提供更准确的扰动预测。

Abstract: Autonomous aerial and aquatic robots that attain mobility by perturbing their medium, such as multicopters and torpedoes, produce wake effects that act as disturbances for adjacent robots. Wake effects are hard to model and predict due to the chaotic spatio-temporal dynamics of the fluid, entangled with the physical geometry of the robots and their complex motion patterns. Data-driven approaches using neural networks typically learn a memory-less function that maps the current states of the two robots to a force observed by the "sufferer" robot. Such models often perform poorly in agile scenarios: since the wake effect has a finite propagation time, the disturbance observed by a sufferer robot is some function of relative states in the past. In this work, we present an empirical study of the properties a wake-effect predictor must satisfy to accurately model the interactions between two robots mediated by a fluid. We explore seven data-driven models designed to capture the spatio-temporal evolution of fluid wake effects in four different media. This allows us to introspect the models and analyze the reasons why certain features enable improved accuracy in prediction across predictors and fluids. As experimental validation, we develop a planar rectilinear gantry for two spinning monocopters to test in real-world data with feedback control. The conclusion is that support of history of previous states as input and transport delay prediction substantially helps to learn an accurate wake-effect predictor.

</details>


### [2] [Energy-Aware Collaborative Exploration for a UAV-UGV Team](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22507)
*Cahit Ikbal Er,Saikiran Juttu,Yasin Yazicioglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种考虑能量约束的无人机-地面机器人协同探索框架，通过能量受限的探索路径规划和移动充电站支持，在未知环境中实现高效协同探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境探索中，无人机受限于电池容量，飞行时间有限，需要与地面机器人协同工作。地面机器人既能探索地面区域，又能作为移动充电站支持无人机，从而提高整体探索效率。

Method: 1) 建立能量约束模型，将无人机飞行时间限制纳入考虑；2) 使用密度感知分层概率路线图构建稀疏耦合的空地路线图；3) 将路径选择问题建模为耦合定向问题，在满足会合约束下最大化信息增益；4) 在碰撞验证的路线上构建探索路径。

Result: 通过仿真研究、基准比较和真实世界实验验证了方法的有效性。该方法能够在能量约束下实现无人机和地面机器人的高效协同探索，确保在无人机飞行时间限制前完成会合。

Conclusion: 提出的能量感知协同探索框架成功解决了无人机能量约束下的空地协同探索问题，通过移动充电站支持和耦合路径规划，实现了在未知环境中的高效、可靠的协同探索。

Abstract: We present an energy-aware collaborative exploration framework for a UAV-UGV team operating in unknown environments, where the UAV's energy constraint is modeled as a maximum flight-time limit. The UAV executes a sequence of energy-bounded exploration tours, while the UGV simultaneously explores on the ground and serves as a mobile charging station. Rendezvous is enforced under a shared time budget so that the vehicles meet at the end of each tour before the UAV reaches its flight-time limit. We construct a sparsely coupled air-ground roadmap using a density-aware layered probabilistic roadmap (PRM) and formulate tour selection over the roadmap as coupled orienteering problems (OPs) to maximize information gain subject to the rendezvous constraint. The resulting tours are constructed over collision-validated roadmap edges. We validate our method through simulation studies, benchmark comparisons, and real-world experiments.

</details>


### [3] [Parallel OctoMapping: A Scalable Framework for Enhanced Path Planning in Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22508)
*Yihui Mao,Tian Tan,Xuehui Shen,Warren E. Dixon,Rushikesh Kamalapurkar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POMP是一种基于OctoMap的高效并行建图方法，通过优化自由空间表示和并行计算，在固定分辨率下提高路径规划成功率并缩短路径长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定分辨率建图方法在复杂环境中会产生过于保守的障碍物表示，导致路径规划失败或产生次优路径。需要一种既能保持地图保真度又能优化自由空间表示的方法。

Method: 提出Parallel OctoMapping (POMP)，一种基于OctoMap的高效建图技术，通过并行计算最大化可用自由空间，在固定占用网格分辨率下细化自由空间表示，同时保持与现有基于搜索的规划器的兼容性。

Result: POMP能够集成到现有规划流程中，在杂乱环境中显著提高路径规划成功率，缩短路径长度，同时大幅提升计算效率。

Conclusion: POMP是首个在固定分辨率下既能细化自由空间表示又保持地图保真度和规划器兼容性的方法，为机器人自主系统提供了更高效的建图解决方案。

Abstract: Mapping is essential in robotics and autonomous systems because it provides the spatial foundation for path planning. Efficient mapping enables planning algorithms to generate reliable paths while ensuring safety and adapting in real time to complex environments. Fixed-resolution mapping methods often produce overly conservative obstacle representations that lead to suboptimal paths or planning failures in cluttered scenes. To address this issue, we introduce Parallel OctoMapping (POMP), an efficient OctoMap-based mapping technique that maximizes available free space and supports multi-threaded computation. To the best of our knowledge, POMP is the first method that, at a fixed occupancy-grid resolution, refines the representation of free space while preserving map fidelity and compatibility with existing search-based planners. It can therefore be integrated into existing planning pipelines, yielding higher pathfinding success rates and shorter path lengths, especially in cluttered environments, while substantially improving computational efficiency.

</details>


### [4] [Learning Sidewalk Autopilot from Multi-Scale Imitation with Corrective Behavior Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22527)
*Honglin He,Yukai Ma,Brad Squicciarini,Wayne Wu,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出通过纠正行为扩展和多尺度模仿学习框架，解决人行道微移动设备在复杂城市环境中模仿学习存在的复合误差、鲁棒性差和泛化能力不足问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于学习的微移动设备控制方法在复杂城市环境中表现不佳，模仿学习虽然从人类演示中学习策略，但依赖固定离线数据导致复合误差、鲁棒性有限和泛化能力差

Method: 1) 数据层面：通过多样化的纠正行为和传感器增强来扩展遥操作数据集，使策略能够学习从自身错误中恢复；2) 模型层面：引入多尺度模仿学习架构，通过基于时间范围的轨迹聚类和分层监督，同时捕捉短时程交互行为和长时程目标导向意图

Result: 真实世界实验表明，该方法在多样化人行道场景中显著提高了鲁棒性和泛化能力

Conclusion: 通过纠正行为扩展和多尺度模仿学习框架，有效解决了模仿学习在微移动设备控制中的局限性，为复杂城市环境中的安全导航提供了更可靠的解决方案

Abstract: Sidewalk micromobility is a promising solution for last-mile transportation, but current learning-based control methods struggle in complex urban environments. Imitation learning (IL) learns policies from human demonstrations, yet its reliance on fixed offline data often leads to compounding errors, limited robustness, and poor generalization. To address these challenges, we propose a framework that advances IL through corrective behavior expansion and multi-scale imitation learning. On the data side, we augment teleoperation datasets with diverse corrective behaviors and sensor augmentations to enable the policy to learn to recover from its own mistakes. On the model side, we introduce a multi-scale IL architecture that captures both short-horizon interactive behaviors and long-horizon goal-directed intentions via horizon-based trajectory clustering and hierarchical supervision. Real-world experiments show that our approach significantly improves robustness and generalization in diverse sidewalk scenarios.

</details>


### [5] [Allometric Scaling Laws for Bipedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22560)
*Naomi Oke,Aja Carter,Ben Gu,Steven Man,Cordelia Pride,Sarah Bergbreiter,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了双足机器人的缩放规律，发现机器人质量与腿长呈L^2关系（而非等比例缩放的L^3），速度遵循L^(1/2)趋势，所需扭矩接近m*L（而非m*L^2），为机器人设计提供了新的缩放法则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人设计的缩放是一个基本挑战。生物系统遵循既定的等比例和异速生长缩放规律，但这些规律如何应用于机器人系统尚不清楚。本文旨在为双足机器人建立跨越三个数量级腿长的异速生长缩放规律。

Method: 1. 文献回顾：从现有文献中提取腿长(L)、体长、质量和速度之间的经验关系；2. 仿真研究：在Drake仿真环境中使用三种具有不同足部几何形状和控制策略的准被动髋关节驱动步行机器人变体，评估不同腿长缩放下的性能。

Result: 1. 机器人质量更接近L^2缩放（而非等比例预测的L^3）；2. 步行速度遵循预期的L^(1/2)动态相似性趋势；3. 最小所需扭矩更接近m*L（而非等比例模型的m*L^2）；4. 足部几何形状与L^1成比例缩放。

Conclusion: 研究结果为机器人设计的异速生长缩放提供了新见解，表明机器人缩放规律与等比例或生物缩放规律不同。这些发现有助于指导不同尺寸机器人的设计优化。

Abstract: Scaling the design of robots up or down remains a fundamental challenge. While biological systems follow well-established isometric and allometric scaling laws relating mass, stride frequency, velocity, and torque, it is unclear how these relationships translate to robotic systems. In this paper, we generate similar allometric scaling laws for bipedal robots across three orders of magnitude in leg length. First, we conduct a review of legged robots from the literature and extract empirical relationships between leg length (L), body length, mass, and speed. These data show that robot mass scales more closely to L^2, in contrast to the L^3 scaling predicted by isometric scaling. We then perform controlled simulation studies in Drake using three variants of real quasi-passive, hip-actuated walkers with different foot geometries and control strategies. We evaluate the performance of each design scaled with leg length, L. Across all robots, walking velocity follows the expected L^(1/2) trend from dynamic similarity. Minimum required torque scales more closely with m*L than the isometric model of m*L^2. Foot geometry scaled proportionally with L^1. These results provide new insight into how robot designs allometrically scale to different sizes, and how that scaling is different from isometric or biological scaling laws.

</details>


### [6] [GIFT: Generalizing Intent for Flexible Test-Time Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22574)
*Fin Amin,Nathaniel Dennler,Andreea Bobu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GIFT框架通过语言模型从用户演示中推断高级意图，而非依赖表面视觉或语义相似性，从而在测试时实现奖励函数在分布偏移下的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法基于视觉或语义相似性改进奖励泛化，但这些表面线索常偏离人类实际意图，导致学习到的奖励函数在新环境中泛化失败。

Method: GIFT利用语言模型通过对比偏好与非偏好行为从用户演示中推断高级意图，在部署时通过意图条件相似性将新测试状态映射到行为等价的训练状态，无需重新训练即可实现奖励泛化。

Result: 在包含50多个未见物体的四个模拟桌面操作任务中，GIFT在测试时配对胜率和状态对齐F1分数上持续优于视觉和语义相似性基线。7-DoF Franka Panda机器人的真实世界实验表明GIFT能可靠迁移到物理环境。

Conclusion: GIFT通过将奖励泛化建立在人类意图而非表面线索上，有效解决了学习奖励函数在分布偏移下的泛化问题，为机器人学习提供了更鲁棒的意图推断和奖励泛化框架。

Abstract: Robots learn reward functions from user demonstrations, but these rewards often fail to generalize to new environments. This failure occurs because learned rewards latch onto spurious correlations in training data rather than the underlying human intent that demonstrations represent. Existing methods leverage visual or semantic similarity to improve robustness, yet these surface-level cues often diverge from what humans actually care about. We present Generalizing Intent for Flexible Test-Time Rewards (GIFT), a framework that grounds reward generalization in human intent rather than surface cues. GIFT leverages language models to infer high-level intent from user demonstrations by contrasting preferred with non-preferred behaviors. At deployment, GIFT maps novel test states to behaviorally equivalent training states via intent-conditioned similarity, enabling learned rewards to generalize across distribution shifts without retraining. We evaluate GIFT on tabletop manipulation tasks with new objects and layouts. Across four simulated tasks with over 50 unseen objects, GIFT consistently outperforms visual and semantic similarity baselines in test-time pairwise win rate and state-alignment F1 score. Real-world experiments on a 7-DoF Franka Panda robot demonstrate that GIFT reliably transfers to physical settings. Further discussion can be found at https://mit-clear-lab.github.io/GIFT/

</details>


### [7] [Task-Agnostic Exoskeleton Control Supports Elderly Joint Energetics during Hip-Intensive Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22580)
*Jiefu Zhang,Nikhil V. Divekar,Chandramouli Krishnan,Robert D. Gregg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种任务无关的髋关节外骨骼控制器，在多种活动中显著减少老年人髋关节生物功，同时增加总功率输出，有望提升老年人活动能力和耐力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人活动能力下降常伴随关节动力学重新分配，即踝关节功能减弱时通过增加髋关节需求来补偿。然而这种补偿策略与年龄相关的髋关节最大功率下降相矛盾。传统外骨骼控制策略仅限于特定任务（如平稳行走），无法满足家庭和社区中的多样化活动需求。

Method: 实现了一种任务无关的髋关节外骨骼控制器，该控制器对关节功率具有内在敏感性。在8名老年人中验证其有效性，测试包括平地行走、斜坡上行、爬楼梯和坐站转换等多种髋关节密集型活动。

Result: 外骨骼能够高度准确地匹配生物功率曲线（平均余弦相似度0.89）。辅助显著减少了髋关节矢状面生物正功24.7%，下肢整体减少9.3%。同时增加了峰值总（生物+外骨骼）髋关节功率，并降低了峰值生物髋关节功率。

Conclusion: 髋关节外骨骼通过减少生物功可能增强耐力，通过增加总功率可能提高功能储备，为支持老年人活动能力提供了一种有前景的生物力学干预手段。

Abstract: Age-related mobility decline is frequently accompanied by a redistribution of joint kinetics, where older adults compensate for reduced ankle function by increasing demand on the hip. Paradoxically, this compensatory shift typically coincides with age-related reductions in maximal hip power. Although robotic exoskeletons can provide immediate energetic benefits, conventional control strategies have limited previous studies in this population to specific tasks such as steady-state walking, which do not fully reflect mobility demands in the home and community. Here, we implement a task-agnostic hip exoskeleton controller that is inherently sensitive to joint power and validate its efficacy in eight older adults. Across a battery of hip-intensive activities that included level walking, ramp ascent, stair climbing, and sit-to-stand transitions, the exoskeleton matched biological power profiles with high accuracy (mean cosine similarity 0.89). Assistance significantly reduced sagittal plane biological positive work by 24.7% at the hip and by 9.3% for the lower limb, while simultaneously augmenting peak total (biological + exoskeleton) hip power and reducing peak biological hip power. These results suggest that hip exoskeletons can potentially enhance endurance through biological work reduction, and increase functional reserve through total power augmentation, serving as a promising biomechanical intervention to support older adults' mobility.

</details>


### [8] [Trajectory Generation for Underactuated Soft Robot Manipulators using Discrete Elastic Rod Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22604)
*Beibei Liu,Akua K. Dickson,Ran Jing,Andrew P. Sabelhaus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向控制的离散弹性杆动力学重构方法，用于软体机器人轨迹生成，在保持物理精度的同时提高计算效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人因柔顺性适合接触丰富任务，但柔顺性导致精确建模困难。现有方法在物理精度和计算效率之间不平衡：连续杆模型物理准确但计算量大，降阶近似提高效率但牺牲建模保真度

Method: 引入离散弹性杆动力学的控制导向重构方法，获得控制仿射表示，同时保持基本力-变形关系。提出基于此动力学的轨迹生成框架

Result: 在气动软体机器人肢体上进行实验验证，轨迹跟踪性能相比基于恒定曲率的基线方法有显著提升，特别是在复杂驱动条件下

Conclusion: 提出的控制导向重构方法能够生成动态可行且与底层驱动假设一致的轨迹，在保持物理精度的同时实现计算效率，为软体机器人运动规划提供有效解决方案

Abstract: Soft robots are well suited for contact-rich tasks due to their compliance, yet this property makes accurate and tractable modeling challenging. Planning motions with dynamically-feasible trajectories requires models that capture arbitrary deformations, remain computationally efficient, and are compatible with underactuation. However, existing approaches balance these properties unevenly: continuum rod models provide physical accuracy but are computationally demanding, while reduced-order approximations improve efficiency at the cost of modeling fidelity. To address this, our work introduces a control-oriented reformulation of Discrete Elastic Rod (DER) dynamics for soft robots, and a method to generate trajectories with these dynamics. The proposed formulation yields a control-affine representation while preserving certain first-principles force-deformation relationships. As a result, the generated trajectories are both dynamically feasible and consistent with the underlying actuation assumptions. We present our trajectory generation framework and validate it experimentally on a pneumatic soft robotic limb. Hardware results demonstrate consistently improved trajectory tracking performance over a constant-curvature-based baseline, particularly under complex actuation conditions.

</details>


### [9] [Variable-Resolution Virtual Maps for Autonomous Exploration with Unmanned Surface Vehicles (USVs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22667)
*Ye Li,Yewei Huang,Wenlong GaoZhang,Alberto Quattrini Li,Brendan Englot,Yuanchang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可变分辨率虚拟地图（VRVM）方法，用于无人水面艇在近岸水域的自主探索，通过自适应四叉树和双变量高斯虚拟地标高效表示地图不确定性，平衡探索与利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近岸水域无人水面艇自主探索面临GNSS信号退化、环境引起的定位不确定性以及机载计算资源有限等挑战。现有虚拟地图方法采用固定分辨率离散化，存储和计算成本随工作空间增大而急剧增加，且在特征稀疏的开放水域过度估值可能导致SLAM失败。

Method: 提出可变分辨率虚拟地图（VRVM），使用自适应四叉树组织工作空间，在单元格中放置双变量高斯虚拟地标表示地图不确定性。自适应四叉树实现区域加权不确定性表示：对信息密集区域分配高分辨率，对远场区域保持粗分辨率和高不确定性。采用期望最大化（EM）规划器评估前沿区域的位姿和地图不确定性，平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在VRX Gazebo仿真器中，使用真实码头环境在不同难度测试场景下评估VRVM与多种先进探索算法。结果表明，在GNSS退化的近岸环境中，该方法提供更安全的行为和更好的机载计算资源利用率。

Conclusion: VRVM方法通过自适应分辨率表示地图不确定性，有效解决了固定分辨率虚拟地图的存储和计算扩展性问题，同时通过区域加权不确定性表示和EM规划器平衡了探索与利用，提高了近岸水域自主探索的安全性和计算效率。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration by unmanned surface vehicles (USVs) in near-shore waters requires reliable localisation and consistent mapping over extended areas, but this is challenged by GNSS degradation, environment-induced localisation uncertainty, and limited on-board computation. Virtual map-based methods explicitly model localisation and mapping uncertainty by tightly coupling factor-graph SLAM with a map uncertainty criterion. However, their storage and computational costs scale poorly with fixed-resolution workspace discretisations, leading to inefficiency in large near-shore environments. Moreover, overvaluing feature-sparse open-water regions can increase the risk of SLAM failure as a result of imbalance between exploration and exploitation. To address these limitations, we propose a Variable-Resolution Virtual Map (VRVM), a computationally efficient method for representing map uncertainty using bivariate Gaussian virtual landmarks placed in the cells of an adaptive quadtree. The adaptive quadtree enables an area-weighted uncertainty representation that keeps coarse, far-field virtual landmarks deliberately uncertain while allocating higher resolution to information-dense regions, and reduces the sensitivity of the map valuation to local refinements of the tree. An expectation-maximisation (EM) planner is adopted to evaluate pose and map uncertainty along frontiers using the VRVM, balancing exploration and exploitation. We evaluate VRVM against several state-of-the-art exploration algorithms in the VRX Gazebo simulator, using a realistic marina environment across different testing scenarios with an increasing level of exploration difficulty. The results indicate that our method offers safer behaviour and better utilisation of on-board computation in GNSS-degraded near-shore environments.

</details>


### [10] [CATNAV: Cached Vision-Language Traversability for Efficient Zero-Shot Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22800)
*Aditya Potnis,Francisco Affonso,Shreya Gummadi,Naveen Kumar Uppalapati,Girish Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CATNAV：基于多模态大语言模型的零样本、具身感知成本图生成导航框架，通过视觉语义缓存减少85.7%的VLM查询，在非结构化环境中实现更高的目标到达率和更少的行为约束违反


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化环境中导航需要根据机器人的物理能力评估穿越风险，这一挑战因机器人具体形态而异。现有方法缺乏对机器人具体形态的适应性，且在线视觉语言模型查询成本高。

Method: 提出CATNAV框架：1）利用多模态LLM进行零样本、具身感知的成本图生成，无需任务特定训练；2）引入视觉语义缓存机制，检测场景新颖性并重用语义相似帧的风险评估；3）基于VLM的轨迹选择模块，通过视觉推理评估候选轨迹以选择最安全路径。

Result: 在四足机器人室内外非结构化环境评估中，相比最先进的视觉-语言-动作基线方法，CATNAV在五项导航任务中平均目标到达率提高10个百分点，行为约束违反减少33%。视觉语义缓存将在线VLM查询减少85.7%。

Conclusion: CATNAV通过多模态LLM实现了零样本、具身感知的导航，视觉语义缓存显著降低了计算成本，在非结构化环境中展现出优越的导航性能和安全性。

Abstract: Navigating unstructured environments requires assessing traversal risk relative to a robot's physical capabilities, a challenge that varies across embodiments. We present CATNAV, a cost-aware traversability navigation framework that leverages multimodal LLMs for zero-shot, embodiment-aware costmap generation without task-specific training. We introduce a visuosemantic caching mechanism that detects scene novelty and reuses prior risk assessments for semantically similar frames, reducing online VLM queries by 85.7%. Furthermore, we introduce a VLM-based trajectory selection module that evaluates proposals through visual reasoning to choose the safest path given behavioral constraints. We evaluate CATNAV on a quadruped robot across indoor and outdoor unstructured environments, comparing against state-of-the-art vision-language-action baselines. Across five navigation tasks, CATNAV achieves 10 percentage point higher average goal-reaching rate and 33% fewer behavioral constraint violations.

</details>


### [11] [DecompGrind: A Decomposition Framework for Robotic Grinding via Cutting-Surface Planning and Contact-Force Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22859)
*Shunsuke Araki,Takumi Hachimine,Yuki Saito,Kouhei Ohnishi,Jun Morimoto,Takamitsu Masubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DecompGrind框架将机器人磨削分解为全局切削表面规划和局部接触力适应两个组件，通过几何分析处理形状过渡，通过模仿学习处理局部力控制，实现高效磨削不同形状和硬度的工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人磨削自动化面临挑战：不同形状和材料硬度的工件磨削时，去除阻力随局部接触条件变化；精确估计去除阻力和形状过渡分析建模困难；基于学习的方法需要大量训练数据覆盖多样加工条件。

Method: 提出DecompGrind框架，包含两个组件：1) 全局切削表面规划(GCSP)：通过几何分析当前和目标形状确定去除形状，无需学习；2) 局部接触力适应(LCFA)：使用基于双边控制的模仿学习在磨削每个去除形状时学习接触力适应策略。这种分解将学习限制在局部接触力适应，使策略能从少量演示中学习，同时通过几何方法处理全局形状过渡。

Result: 使用机器人磨削系统和3D打印工件进行实验，证明DecompGrind能够高效磨削不同形状和材料硬度的工件，同时保持安全的接触力水平。

Conclusion: 通过将机器人磨削分解为全局形状规划和局部力适应两个组件，DecompGrind框架解决了传统方法的局限性，实现了高效、安全的自动化磨削，减少了学习所需的数据量。

Abstract: Robotic grinding is widely used for shaping workpieces in manufacturing, but it remains difficult to automate this process efficiently. In particular, efficiently grinding workpieces of different shapes and material hardness is challenging because removal resistance varies with local contact conditions. Moreover, it is difficult to achieve accurate estimation of removal resistance and analytical modeling of shape transition, and learning-based approaches often require large amounts of training data to cover diverse processing conditions. To address these challenges, we decompose robotic grinding into two components: removal-shape planning and contact-force adaptation. Based on this formulation, we propose DecompGrind, a framework that combines Global Cutting-Surface Planning (GCSP) and Local Contact-Force Adaptation (LCFA). GCSP determines removal shapes through geometric analysis of the current and target shapes without learning, while LCFA learns a contact-force adaptation policy using bilateral control-based imitation learning during the grinding of each removal shape. This decomposition restricts learning to local contact-force adaptation, allowing the policy to be learned from a small number of demonstrations, while handling global shape transition geometrically. Experiments using a robotic grinding system and 3D-printed workpieces demonstrate efficient robotic grinding of workpieces having different shapes and material hardness while maintaining safe levels of contact force.

</details>


### [12] [Grounding Sim-to-Real Generalization in Dexterous Manipulation: An Empirical Study with Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22876)
*Ruixing Jin,Zicheng Zhu,Ruixiang Ouyang,Sheng Xu,Bo Yue,Zhizheng Wu,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过超过1万次真实世界试验，系统评估了Sim-to-Real泛化的四个关键维度对通用操作策略（如VLA模型）性能的影响，并发布了标准化评估协议和机器人平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有许多研究提出缩小Sim-to-Real差距的算法，但缺乏在真实世界操作任务中对这些方法进行系统评估的研究，特别是对通用策略（如VLA模型）性能的影响。真实世界数据收集成本高昂，而合成数据存在显著分布差异。

Method: 设计了全面的评估协议来量化操作任务的真实世界性能，考虑背景、光照、干扰物、物体类型和空间特征等关键变化。通过超过1万次真实世界试验，从四个维度实证检验Sim-to-Real泛化的主要决定因素：多级域随机化、照片级真实感渲染、物理真实建模和强化学习更新。

Result: 通过大规模实验得出了关于Sim-to-Real迁移的关键见解。研究发布了机器人平台和评估协议供公开访问，以促进独立验证，为灵巧操作策略建立了现实且标准化的基准。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了Sim-to-Real方法在真实世界通用操作策略评估方面的空白，通过系统实验提供了关键见解，并为未来研究提供了标准化基准和可验证的平台。

Abstract: Learning a generalist control policy for dexterous manipulation typically relies on large-scale datasets. Given the high cost of real-world data collection, a practical alternative is to generate synthetic data through simulation. However, the resulting synthetic data often exhibits a significant gap from real-world distributions. While many prior studies have proposed algorithms to bridge the Sim-to-Real discrepancy, there remains a lack of principled research that grounds these methods in real-world manipulation tasks, particularly their performance on generalist policies such as Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. In this study, we empirically examine the primary determinants of Sim-to-Real generalization across four dimensions: multi-level domain randomization, photorealistic rendering, physics-realistic modeling, and reinforcement learning updates. To support this study, we design a comprehensive evaluation protocol to quantify the real-world performance of manipulation tasks. The protocol accounts for key variations in background, lighting, distractors, object types, and spatial features. Through experiments involving over 10k real-world trials, we derive critical insights into Sim-to-Real transfer. To inform and advance future studies, we release both the robotic platforms and the evaluation protocol for public access to facilitate independent verification, thereby establishing a realistic and standardized benchmark for dexterous manipulation policies.

</details>


### [13] [Agile-VLA: Few-Shot Industrial Pose Rectification via Implicit Affordance Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22899)
*Teng Yan,Zhengyang Pei,Chengyu Shi,Yue Yu,Yikun Chen,Zilong Zhu,Zelin Fang,Kaile Guo,Zihang Wang,Peigen Tian,Bingzhuo Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Agile-VLA：用于边缘设备的分层视觉-语言-动作框架，通过隐式可供性锚定机制将几何视觉线索直接映射到参数化动作基元，减少语义推理延迟，实现10Hz感知与50Hz控制的异步双流架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘平台上部署VLA模型时，存在高延迟语义推理与动态操纵所需的高频控制之间的根本冲突，需要解决边缘机器人学习中固有的频率不匹配问题。

Method: 提出Agile-VLA分层框架，核心是隐式可供性锚定机制，将质心和边缘关键点锚定等几何视觉线索直接映射到结构化参数化动作基元。采用异步双流架构将感知（10Hz）与控制（50Hz）解耦。

Result: 在标准6自由度机械臂上的实验表明，Agile-VLA仅通过5次演示就能稳健地校正复杂、不规则工件，利用外部灵巧性实现高效姿态重定向。

Conclusion: Agile-VLA通过隐式可供性锚定和异步双流架构有效解决了边缘VLA模型中的频率不匹配问题，为工业姿态重定向任务提供了高效、低延迟的解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models on resource-constrained edge platforms encounters a fundamental conflict between high-latency semantic inference and the high-frequency control required for dynamic manipulation. To address the challenge, this paper presents Agile-VLA, a hierarchical framework designed for industrial pose reorientation tasks on edge devices such as the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano. The core innovation is an Implicit Affordance Anchoring mechanism that directly maps geometric visual cues, specifically centroid and rim keypoint anchors, into structured parametric action primitives, thereby substantially reducing reliance on high-latency semantic inference during closed-loop control. By decoupling perception (10 Hz) from control (50 Hz) via an asynchronous dual-stream architecture, the system effectively mitigates the frequency mismatch inherent in edge-based robot learning. Experimental results on a standard 6-DoF manipulator demonstrate that Agile-VLA achieves robust rectification of complex, irregular workpieces using only 5-shot demonstrations through extrinsic dexterity.

</details>


### [14] [Tightly-Coupled Radar-Visual-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23052)
*Morten Nissov,Mohit Singh,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种紧密耦合的视觉-雷达-惯性里程计方法，通过融合图像特征、雷达多普勒测量和IMU数据，在恶劣环境（黑暗、低纹理、雾等）下实现鲁棒的状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉惯性里程计在黑暗、低纹理、遮挡等恶劣环境下性能受限，而雷达惯性里程计虽然对这些视觉挑战具有鲁棒性，但信息密度较低且长期精度较差。需要结合两者的优势，实现无论环境条件或轨迹动态如何都能鲁棒运行的状态估计方法。

Method: 提出紧密耦合的视觉-雷达-惯性里程计方法，在迭代扩展卡尔曼滤波器框架中实时融合图像特征、雷达多普勒测量和IMU测量。利用雷达测距数据增强视觉特征深度初始化，提高系统在恶劣环境下的鲁棒性。

Result: 通过在室内外环境进行飞行实验，并在黑暗、雾、快速飞行等挑战性条件下测试，全面验证了所提方法的鲁棒性。该方法在各种环境条件和轨迹动态下均能稳定运行。

Conclusion: 成功开发了一种紧密耦合的视觉-雷达-惯性里程计方法，结合了视觉和雷达系统的优势，在恶劣环境条件下实现了鲁棒的状态估计，为受限和轻量级平台提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual-Inertial Odometry (VIO) is a staple for reliable state estimation on constrained and lightweight platforms due to its versatility and demonstrated performance. However, pertinent challenges regarding robust operation in dark, low-texture, obscured environments complicate the use of such methods. Alternatively, Frequency Modulated Continuous Wave (FMCW) radars, and by extension Radar-Inertial Odometry (RIO), offer robustness to these visual challenges, albeit at the cost of reduced information density and worse long-term accuracy. To address these limitations, this work combines the two in a tightly coupled manner, enabling the resulting method to operate robustly regardless of environmental conditions or trajectory dynamics. The proposed method fuses image features, radar Doppler measurements, and Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) measurements within an Iterated Extended Kalman Filter (IEKF) in real-time, with radar range data augmenting the visual feature depth initialization. The method is evaluated through flight experiments conducted in both indoor and outdoor environments, as well as through challenges to both exteroceptive modalities (such as darkness, fog, or fast flight), thoroughly demonstrating its robustness. The implementation of the proposed method is available at: https://github.com/ntnu-arl/radvio .

</details>


### [15] [AirSimAG: A High-Fidelity Simulation Platform for Air-Ground Collaborative Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23079)
*Yangjie Cui,Xin Dong,Boyang Gao,Jinwu Xiang,Daochun Li,Zhan Tu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AirsimAG是一个基于定制化AirSim框架的高保真空地协同仿真平台，专门用于无人机-无人车异构多智能体系统的协同仿真，支持同步多智能体仿真和异构传感与控制接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真平台主要针对单智能体动力学设计，缺乏专门的空地交互协同仿真框架，而无人机-无人车异构多智能体系统在搜救、城市监控、环境监测等复杂应用中展现出巨大潜力。

Method: 基于广泛定制的AirSim框架构建高保真空地协同仿真平台，实现同步多智能体仿真，支持无人机-无人车系统的异构传感和控制接口，并设计了一套代表性的空地协同任务进行验证。

Result: 开发了AirsimAG仿真平台，通过映射、规划、跟踪、编队和探索等代表性任务的定量分析，证明了平台在多智能体协调和跨模态数据一致性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: AirsimAG填补了现有仿真平台在空地协同仿真方面的空白，为无人机-无人车异构多智能体系统的研究和应用提供了有效的仿真工具，平台已开源供研究社区使用。

Abstract: As spatial intelligence continues to evolve, heterogeneous multi-agent systems-particularly the collaboration between Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) and Unmanned Ground Vehicles (UGVs), have demonstrated strong potential in complex applications such as search and rescue, urban surveillance, and environmental monitoring. However, existing simulation platforms are primarily designed for single-agent dynamics and lack dedicated frameworks for interactive air-ground collaborative simulation. In this paper, we present AirsimAG, a high-fidelity air-ground collaborative simulation platform built upon an extensively customized AirSim framework. The platform enables synchronized multi-agent simulation and supports heterogeneous sensing and control interfaces for UAV-UGV systems. To demonstrate its capabilities, we design a set of representative air-ground collaborative tasks, including mapping, planning, tracking, formation, and exploration. We further provide quantitative analyses based on these tasks to illustrate the platform effectiveness in supporting multi-agent coordination and cross-modal data consistency. The AirsimAG simulation platform is publicly available at https://github.com/BIULab-BUAA/AirSimAG.

</details>


### [16] [Active Robotic Perception for Disease Detection and Mapping in Apple Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23112)
*Hayden Feddock,Francisco Yandun,Srđan Aćimović,Abhisesh Silwal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于休眠苹果树病害检测的自主移动主动感知系统，针对火疫病，通过立体RGB传感、深度估计、实例分割和语义3D建图实现精确症状定位，并评估三种视点规划策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模果园生产需要及时精确的病害监测，但人工巡查劳动密集且成本高昂，导致病害发现晚且空间分辨率低。需要自动化系统实现精确病害检测与建图。

Method: 集成闪光照明立体RGB传感、实时深度估计、实例级分割和置信度感知语义3D建图。评估三种视点规划策略：确定性几何基线、最大化未知空间减少的体积化最优视点规划器、优先处理低置信度症状区域的语义最优视点规划器。

Result: 在模拟实验中，语义规划器在30个视点后获得最高F1分数(0.6106)，体积规划器获得最高ROI覆盖率(85.82%)。实验室设置中，语义规划器获得最高最终F1(0.9058)，两种最优视点规划器均显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 自主移动主动感知系统能够可靠定位和建图苹果树病害症状，语义最优视点规划器在症状检测方面表现最佳，为田间评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large-scale orchard production requires timely and precise disease monitoring, yet routine manual scouting is labor-intensive and financially impractical at the scale of modern operations. As a result, disease outbreaks are often detected late and tracked at coarse spatial resolutions, typically at the orchard-block level. We present an autonomous mobile active perception system for targeted disease detection and mapping in dormant apple trees, demonstrated on one of the most devastating diseases affecting apple today -- fire blight. The system integrates flash-illuminated stereo RGB sensing, real-time depth estimation, instance-level segmentation, and confidence-aware semantic 3D mapping to achieve precise localization of disease symptoms. Semantic predictions are fused into the volumetric occupancy map representation enabling the tracking of both occupancy and per-voxel semantic confidence, building actionable spatial maps for growers. To actively refine observations within complex canopies, we evaluate three viewpoint planning strategies within a unified perception-action loop: a deterministic geometric baseline, a volumetric next-best-view planner that maximizes unknown-space reduction, and a semantic next-best-view planner that prioritizes low-confidence symptomatic regions. Experiments on a fabricated lab tree and five simulated symptomatic trees demonstrate reliable symptom localization and mapping as a precursor to a field evaluation. In simulation, the semantic planner achieves the highest F1 score (0.6106) after 30 viewpoints, while the volumetric planner achieves the highest ROI coverage (85.82\%). In the lab setting, the semantic planner attains the highest final F1 (0.9058), with both next-best-view planners substantially improving coverage over the baseline.

</details>


### [17] [PHANTOM Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23152)
*Teng Yan,Jiongxu Chen,Qixiang Hua,Yue Yu,Zihang Wang,Yaohua Liu,Bingzhuo Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PHANTOM Hand：一种混合精度增强柔顺的欠驱动灵巧手，通过统一框架实现精确运动规划与稳定抓取的结合，解决了传统欠驱动手运动不可预测和力传输非线性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统肌腱驱动欠驱动手虽然在自适应抓取方面表现出色，但存在运动学不可预测性和高度非线性力传输问题，这限制了其执行精确自由运动成形和复杂操作任务中可靠载荷传递的能力。

Method: 提出PHANTOM Hand系统（6个执行器，15个自由度），采用统一框架结合精确解析成形与鲁棒柔顺抓取。通过从物理几何推导稀疏映射并集成基于力学的补偿模型，有效抑制弹簧反张力和肌腱弹性引起的运动学漂移。

Result: 实验验证显示：1）运动学分析确认工作空间内亚度级全局精度；2）静态表达能力测试展示复杂手势；3）多样化抓取实验覆盖强力、精密和工具使用类别；4）指尖力定量表征。系统成功结合解析运动学精度与连续可预测力输出。

Conclusion: PHANTOM手成功将解析运动学精度与连续可预测力输出相结合，显著扩展了欠驱动手的载荷能力和灵巧性。所有硬件设计和控制脚本完全开源，推动欠驱动操作生态系统发展。

Abstract: Tendon-driven underactuated hands excel in adaptive grasping but often suffer from kinematic unpredictability and highly non-linear force transmission. This ambiguity limits their ability to perform precise free-motion shaping and deliver reliable payloads for complex manipulation tasks. To address this, we introduce the PHANTOM Hand (Hybrid Precision-Augmented Compliance): a modular, 1:1 human-scale system featuring 6 actuators and 15 degrees of freedom (DoFs).
  We propose a unified framework that bridges the gap between precise analytic shaping and robust compliant grasping. By deriving a sparse mapping from physical geometry and integrating a mechanics-based compensation model, we effectively suppress kinematic drift caused by spring counter-tension and tendon elasticity. This approach achieves sub-degree kinematic reproducibility for free-motion planning while retaining the inherent mechanical compliance required for stable physical interaction.
  Experimental validation confirms the system's capabilities through (1) kinematic analysis verifying sub-degree global accuracy across the workspace; (2) static expressibility tests demonstrating complex hand gestures; (3) diverse grasping experiments covering power, precision, and tool-use categories; and (4) quantitative fingertip force characterization. The results demonstrate that the PHANTOM hand successfully combines analytic kinematic precision with continuous, predictable force output, significantly expanding the payload and dexterity of underactuated hands. To drive the development of the underactuated manipulation ecosystem, all hardware designs and control scripts are fully open-sourced for community engagement.

</details>


### [18] [LiZIP: An Auto-Regressive Compression Framework for LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23162)
*Aditya Shibu,Kayvan Karim,Claudio Zito*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LiZIP：基于神经预测编码的轻量级近无损零漂移LiDAR点云压缩框架，在保持高精度的同时显著提升压缩率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆LiDAR传感器产生海量数据，对实时处理和V2X传输造成瓶颈。现有无损压缩方法存在局限：工业标准算法（如LASzip）缺乏适应性，而深度学习方法计算成本过高

Method: 提出LiZIP框架，基于神经预测编码，使用紧凑的多层感知机（MLP）从局部上下文预测点坐标，仅编码稀疏残差，实现轻量级近无损零漂移压缩

Result: 在NuScenes和Argoverse数据集上评估，相比LASzip文件大小减少7.5%-14.8%，优于Google Draco 8.8%-11.3%，相比GZip减少38%-48%。在未见过的Argoverse数据集上无需重新训练即展现泛化能力

Conclusion: LiZIP在保持高几何精度的同时实现了卓越的压缩性能，为带宽受限的V2X应用和大规模云存储提供了显著优势

Abstract: The massive volume of data generated by LiDAR sensors in autonomous vehicles creates a bottleneck for real-time processing and vehicle-to-everything (V2X) transmission. Existing lossless compression methods often force a trade-off: industry standard algorithms (e.g., LASzip) lack adaptability, while deep learning approaches suffer from prohibitive computational costs. This paper proposes LiZIP, a lightweight, near-lossless zero-drift compression framework based on neural predictive coding. By utilizing a compact Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) to predict point coordinates from local context, LiZIP efficiently encodes only the sparse residuals.
  We evaluate LiZIP on the NuScenes and Argoverse datasets, benchmarking against GZip, LASzip, and Google Draco (configured with 24-bit quantization to serve as a high-precision geometric baseline). Results demonstrate that LiZIP consistently achieves superior compression ratios across varying environments. The proposed system achieves a 7.5%-14.8% reduction in file size compared to the industry-standard LASzip and outperforms Google Draco by 8.8%-11.3% across diverse datasets. Furthermore, the system demonstrates generalization capabilities on the unseen Argoverse dataset without retraining. Against the general purpose GZip algorithm, LiZIP achieves a reduction of 38%-48%. This efficiency offers a distinct advantage for bandwidth constrained V2X applications and large scale cloud archival.

</details>


### [19] [Path Planning and Reinforcement Learning-Driven Control of On-Orbit Free-Flying Multi-Arm Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23182)
*Álvaro Belmonte-Baeza,José Luis Ramón,Leonard Felicetti,Miguel Cazorla,Jorge Pomares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合轨迹优化(TO)和强化学习(RL)的混合方法，用于在轨服务场景中自由飞行多臂机器人的运动规划与控制，通过TO生成可行路径，RL实现自适应轨迹跟踪，提高空间操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨服务场景中，自由飞行多臂机器人面临动态不确定性、运动耦合和环境干扰等挑战，需要既能生成精确可行路径，又能适应动态变化的控制方法。传统方法难以同时满足精确规划和鲁棒适应的需求。

Method: 采用混合方法：轨迹优化(TO)负责生成考虑动态和运动学约束的可行高效路径，优化手臂运动和推进器力；强化学习(RL)负责自适应轨迹跟踪，处理不确定性和动态交互。多臂机器人设计配备推进器用于精确体控制，提供冗余性和稳定性。

Result: 通过综合仿真验证，混合方法在两种案例研究（初始接触的表面运动和需要表面逼近的自由漂浮场景）中均优于传统策略。推进器显著提高了运动平滑性、安全性和操作效率，RL策略有效跟踪TO生成的轨迹，处理高维动作空间和动态失配。

Conclusion: TO和RL的集成结合了精确任务特定规划和鲁棒适应性的优势，在空间环境特有的不确定动态条件下确保高性能。该框架通过解决运动耦合、环境干扰和动态控制需求等挑战，为推进空间机器人系统的自主性和有效性奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a hybrid approach that integrates trajectory optimization (TO) and reinforcement learning (RL) for motion planning and control of free-flying multi-arm robots in on-orbit servicing scenarios. The proposed system integrates TO for generating feasible, efficient paths while accounting for dynamic and kinematic constraints, and RL for adaptive trajectory tracking under uncertainties. The multi-arm robot design, equipped with thrusters for precise body control, enables redundancy and stability in complex space operations. TO optimizes arm motions and thruster forces, reducing reliance on the arms for stabilization and enhancing maneuverability. RL further refines this by leveraging model-free control to adapt to dynamic interactions and disturbances. The experimental results validated through comprehensive simulations demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed hybrid approach. Two case studies are explored: surface motion with initial contact and a free-floating scenario requiring surface approximation. In both cases, the hybrid method outperforms traditional strategies. In particular, the thrusters notably enhance motion smoothness, safety, and operational efficiency. The RL policy effectively tracks TO-generated trajectories, handling high-dimensional action spaces and dynamic mismatches. This integration of TO and RL combines the strengths of precise, task-specific planning with robust adaptability, ensuring high performance in the uncertain and dynamic conditions characteristic of space environments. By addressing challenges such as motion coupling, environmental disturbances, and dynamic control requirements, this framework establishes a strong foundation for advancing the autonomy and effectiveness of space robotic systems.

</details>


### [20] [AeroScene: Progressive Scene Synthesis for Aerial Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23224)
*Nghia Vu,Tuong Do,Dzung Tran,Binh X. Nguyen,Hoan Nguyen,Erman Tjiputra,Quang D. Tran,Hai-Nguyen Nguyen,Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AeroScene：用于无人机仿真场景合成的分层扩散模型，通过层次感知分词和多分支特征提取生成物理合理、语义一致的3D场景，可直接集成到NVIDIA Isaac Sim中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在多个领域已展现显著影响，但在机器人场景合成方面潜力尚未充分探索。无人机仿真环境仍严重依赖手动创建，耗时且难以扩展，需要自动化、高质量的3D场景生成方法。

Method: 提出AeroScene分层扩散模型，采用层次感知分词和多分支特征提取技术，能够同时推理全局布局和局部细节，确保物理合理性和语义一致性，特别适用于无人机导航、着陆和栖息等任务。

Result: 在新建数据集和公共基准测试中，AeroScene显著优于现有方法；生成了包含1000多个物理就绪、高保真3D场景的大规模数据集，可直接集成到NVIDIA Isaac Sim；在下游无人机导航任务中验证了生成环境的实用性。

Conclusion: AeroScene为无人机仿真提供了高效、可扩展的3D场景生成解决方案，填补了生成模型在机器人场景合成领域的空白，推动了无人机仿真环境创建的自动化和规模化。

Abstract: Generative models have shown substantial impact across multiple domains, their potential for scene synthesis remains underexplored in robotics. This gap is more evident in drone simulators, where simulation environments still rely heavily on manual efforts, which are time-consuming to create and difficult to scale. In this work, we introduce AeroScene, a hierarchical diffusion model for progressive 3D scene synthesis. Our approach leverages hierarchy-aware tokenization and multi-branch feature extraction to reason across both global layouts and local details, ensuring physical plausibility and semantic consistency. This makes AeroScene particularly suited for generating realistic scenes for aerial robotics tasks such as navigation, landing, and perching. We demonstrate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on our newly collected dataset and a public benchmark, showing that AeroScene significantly outperforms prior methods. Furthermore, we use AeroScene to generate a large-scale dataset of over 1,000 physics-ready, high fidelity 3D scenes that can be directly integrated into NVIDIA Isaac Sim. Finally, we illustrate the utility of these generated environments on downstream drone navigation tasks. Our code and dataset are publicly available at aioz-ai.github.io/AeroScene/

</details>


### [21] [Efficient Hybrid SE(3)-Equivariant Visuomotor Flow Policy via Spherical Harmonics for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23227)
*Qinglun Zhang,Shen Cheng,Tian Dan,Haoqiang Fan,Guanghui Liu,Shuaicheng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: E3Flow是一个新颖的机器人策略学习框架，首次将高效的整流流与稳定的多模态等变学习统一起来，解决了现有等变方法计算强度高、依赖单模态输入以及与快速采样方法结合不稳定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等变方法虽然提高了数据效率，但存在三个关键限制：1）计算强度高；2）依赖单模态输入；3）与快速采样方法结合时不稳定。这些限制阻碍了等变方法在实际机器人应用中的部署。

Method: E3Flow基于球谐表示确保严格的SO(3)等变性，引入新颖的不变特征增强模块(FEM)动态融合混合视觉模态（点云和图像），将丰富的视觉线索注入球谐特征中，实现了整流流与多模态等变学习的统一。

Result: 在MimicGen的8个操作任务上，E3Flow相比最先进的球面扩散策略(SDP)平均成功率提升3.12%，同时实现7倍推理加速。4个真实世界实验验证了其在物理环境中的有效性。

Conclusion: E3Flow在性能、效率和数据效率之间实现了新的高效权衡，为机器人策略学习提供了更实用的解决方案，首次成功统一了高效整流流与稳定的多模态等变学习。

Abstract: While existing equivariant methods enhance data efficiency, they suffer from high computational intensity, reliance on single-modality inputs, and instability when combined with fast-sampling methods. In this work, we propose E3Flow, a novel framework that addresses the critical limitations of equivariant diffusion policies. E3Flow overcomes these challenges, successfully unifying efficient rectified flow with stable, multi-modal equivariant learning for the first time. Our framework is built upon spherical harmonic representations to ensure rigorous SO(3) equivariance. We introduce a novel invariant Feature Enhancement Module (FEM) that dynamically fuses hybrid visual modalities (point clouds and images), injecting rich visual cues into the spherical harmonic features. We evaluate E3Flow on 8 manipulation tasks from the MimicGen and further conduct 4 real-world experiments to validate its effectiveness in physical environments. Simulation results show that E3Flow achieves a 3.12% improvement in average success rate over the state-of-the-art Spherical Diffusion Policy (SDP) while simultaneously delivering a 7x inference speedup. E3Flow thus demonstrates a new and highly effective trade-off between performance, efficiency, and data efficiency for robotic policy learning. Code: https://github.com/zql-kk/E3Flow.

</details>


### [22] [Learning Multi-Agent Local Collision-Avoidance for Collaborative Carrying tasks with Coupled Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23278)
*Francesca Bray,Simone Tolomei,Andrei Cramariuc,Cesar Cadena,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于强化学习的双四足机器人协作搬运系统，仅使用机载感知实现未知环境中的避障和速度跟踪，无需预计算轨迹或完整地图


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作搬运在仓库和建筑工地等场景有重要应用价值，但现有方法主要针对无障碍环境或依赖预计算地图，难以适应真实世界的复杂障碍环境

Method: 提出分层架构：感知层高级物体中心策略控制两个预训练的运动策略；使用游戏式渐进课程增加地形复杂度；仅依赖机载传感器，无需预计算轨迹或完整地图

Result: 在通过球关节连接的双四足机器人平台上验证，相比基于优化的方法和去中心化RL基线表现更优；硬件实验证明系统能在未知环境中运动，无需地图或路径规划器

Conclusion: 提出的强化学习方法成功实现了双四足机器人在未知环境中的协作搬运和避障，为真实世界应用提供了无需预计算地图的解决方案

Abstract: Robotic collaborative carrying could greatly benefit human activities like warehouse and construction site management. However, coordinating the simultaneous motion of multiple robots represents a significant challenge. Existing works primarily focus on obstacle-free environments, making them unsuitable for most real-world applications. Works that account for obstacles, either overfit to a specific terrain configuration or rely on pre-recorded maps combined with path planners to compute collision-free trajectories. This work focuses on two quadrupedal robots mechanically connected to a carried object. We propose a Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based policy that enables tracking a commanded velocity direction while avoiding collisions with nearby obstacles using only onboard sensing, eliminating the need for precomputed trajectories and complete map knowledge. Our work presents a hierarchical architecture, where a perceptive high-level object-centric policy commands two pretrained locomotion policies. Additionally, we employ a game-inspired curriculum to increase the complexity of obstacles in the terrain progressively. We validate our approach on two quadrupedal robots connected to a bar via spherical joints, benchmarking it against optimization-based and decentralized RL baselines. Our hardware experiments demonstrate the ability of our system to locomote in unknown environments without the need for a map or a path planner. The video of our work is available in the multimedia material.

</details>


### [23] [Strain-Parameterized Coupled Dynamics and Dual-Camera Visual Servoing for Aerial Continuum Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23333)
*Niloufar Amiri,Farrokh Janabi-Sharifi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于肌腱驱动空中连续体机械臂的通用动力学建模方法，结合了基于视觉的鲁棒控制方案，解决了现有方法计算成本高和未考虑平台欠驱动特性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肌腱驱动空中连续体机械臂的耦合动力学建模方法计算成本高，且未明确考虑空中平台的欠驱动特性，限制了系统的实际应用性能。

Method: 提出了一种通用动力学公式，将应变参数化的Cosserat杆模型与无人机刚体模型集成到SE(3)上的统一拉格朗日常微分方程框架中；基于该模型开发了鲁棒的双摄像头基于图像的视觉伺服方案，包含视野限制缓解、姿态引起的图像运动补偿和具有形式稳定性保证的低级自适应控制器。

Result: 在紧凑型定制原型上进行的广泛仿真和实验验证表明，所提出的框架在实际场景中具有有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种计算高效的通用动力学建模方法，结合鲁棒的视觉伺服控制，解决了肌腱驱动空中连续体机械臂的关键挑战，为实际应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Tendon-driven aerial continuum manipulators (TD-ACMs) combine the maneuverability of uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) with the compliance of lightweight continuum robots (CRs). Existing coupled dynamic modeling approaches for TD-ACMs incur high computational costs and do not explicitly account for aerial platform underactuation. To address these limitations, this paper presents a generalized dynamic formulation of a coupled TD-ACM with an underactuated base. The proposed approach integrates a strain-parameterized Cosserat rod model with a rigid-body model of the UAV into a unified Lagrangian ordinary differential equation (ODE) framework on $\mathrm{SE}(3)$, thereby eliminating computationally intensive symbolic derivations. Building upon the developed model, a robust dual-camera image-based visual servoing (IBVS) scheme is introduced. The proposed controller mitigates the field-of-view (FoV) limitations of conventional IBVS, compensates for attitude-induced image motion caused by UAV lateral dynamics, and incorporates a low-level adaptive controller to address modeling uncertainties with formal stability guarantees. Extensive simulations and experimental validation on a compact custom-built prototype demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed framework in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [24] [Edge Radar Material Classification Under Geometry Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23342)
*Jannik Hohmann,Dong Wang,Andreas Nüchter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于毫米波雷达的材料分类系统，在理想几何条件下性能优异（94.2% F1），但在实际几何变化（高度、倾斜）下性能显著下降至68.5%，论文分析了失效模式并提出了改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料感知能提升机器人导航和交互能力，特别是在摄像头和激光雷达性能下降的环境中。需要为超低功耗边缘设备开发轻量级毫米波雷达材料分类方案。

Method: 使用紧凑的距离仓强度描述符和多层感知机（MLP）构建轻量级毫米波雷达材料分类管道，在TI IWRL6432等超低功耗边缘设备上实现实时推理。

Result: 在标称训练几何条件下达到94.2%的宏F1分数，但在实际几何变化（传感器高度变化和小倾斜角）下性能显著下降至约68.5%。几何扰动引起系统性强度缩放和角度相关的雷达散射截面效应，导致特征分布外移。

Conclusion: 分析了材料分类系统在几何变化下的失效模式，提出了通过归一化、几何增强和运动感知特征等实用方向来提高系统鲁棒性。

Abstract: Material awareness can improve robotic navigation and interaction, particularly in conditions where cameras and LiDAR degrade. We present a lightweight mmWave radar material classification pipeline designed for ultra-low-power edge devices (TI IWRL6432), using compact range-bin intensity descriptors and a Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) for real-time inference. While the classifier reaches a macro-F1 of 94.2\% under the nominal training geometry, we observe a pronounced performance drop under realistic geometry shifts, including sensor height changes and small tilt angles. These perturbations induce systematic intensity scaling and angle-dependent radar cross section (RCS) effects, pushing features out of distribution and reducing macro-F1 to around 68.5\%. We analyze these failure modes and outline practical directions for improving robustness with normalization, geometry augmentation, and motion-aware features.

</details>


### [25] [PinPoint: Monocular Needle Pose Estimation for Robotic Suturing via Stein Variational Newton and Geometric Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23365)
*Jesse F. d'Almeida,Tanner Watts,Susheela Sharma Stern,James Ferguson,Alan Kuntz,Robert J. Webster*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PinPoint：一种用于单目内窥镜手术针姿态估计的概率变分推理框架，通过维持多模态姿态假设分布而非抑制模糊性，显著提升精度和不确定性校准


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目内窥镜手术中，深度模糊和旋转对称性使手术针姿态估计成为病态问题，产生多模态可行配置分布而非单一可靠估计，现有方法难以处理这种固有模糊性

Method: 提出概率变分推理框架，结合单目图像观测与机器人抓取约束，通过解析几何似然和闭式雅可比矩阵，采用Stein变分牛顿推理进行二阶粒子传输，同时保持多模态结构多样性

Result: 在真实针跟踪序列中，相比粒子滤波基线，平移误差降低80%（至1.00mm），旋转误差降低78%（至13.80°），不确定性校准显著改善；在诱导旋转序列中，84%时间保持双峰后验，是基线三倍

Conclusion: PinPoint框架能有效处理单目内窥镜手术针姿态估计的固有模糊性，通过维持多模态假设分布提供更可靠估计，在离体组织缝合实验中表现出稳定跟踪性能，即使针完全嵌入时仍能保持良好精度

Abstract: Reliable estimation of surgical needle 3D position and orientation is essential for autonomous robotic suturing, yet existing methods operate almost exclusively under stereoscopic vision. In monocular endoscopic settings, common in transendoscopic and intraluminal procedures, depth ambiguity and rotational symmetry render needle pose estimation inherently ill-posed, producing a multimodal distribution over feasible configurations, rather than a single, well-grounded estimate. We present PinPoint, a probabilistic variational inference framework that treats this ambiguity directly, maintaining a distribution of pose hypotheses rather than suppressing it. PinPoint combines monocular image observations with robot-grasp constraints through analytical geometric likelihoods with closed-form Jacobians. This framework enables efficient Gauss-Newton preconditioning in a Stein Variational Newton inference, where second-order particle transport deterministically moves particles toward high-probability regions while kernel-based repulsion preserves diversity in the multimodal structure. On real needle-tracking sequences, PinPoint reduces mean translational error by 80% (down to 1.00 mm) and rotational error by 78% (down to 13.80°) relative to a particle-filter baseline, with substantially better-calibrated uncertainty. On induced-rotation sequences, where monocular ambiguity is most severe, PinPoint maintains a bimodal posterior 84% of the time, almost three times the rate of the particle filter baseline, correctly preserving the alternative hypothesis rather than committing prematurely to one mode. Suturing experiments in ex vivo tissue demonstrate stable tracking through intermittent occlusion, with average errors during occlusion of 1.34 mm in translation and 19.18° in rotation, even when the needle is fully embedded.

</details>


### [26] [Rectify, Don't Regret: Avoiding Pitfalls of Differentiable Simulation in Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23393)
*Harsh Yadav,Christian Bohn,Tobias Meisen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种"分离式滚动时域推演"方法，通过切断仿真步骤间的计算图，解决闭环仿真中的捷径学习问题，使模型学习真实的恢复行为而非非因果地优化过去预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开环轨迹模型在现实自动驾驶中面临初始偏差累积问题，而完全可微的闭环仿真器存在捷径学习缺陷：损失梯度通过诱导状态输入反向传播，泄漏未来真值信息，使模型非因果地"后悔"过去错误而非学习真实恢复行为。

Method: 引入分离式滚动时域推演方法，明确切断仿真步骤间的计算图，使模型从漂移状态学习真实的恢复行为，强制其"纠正"错误而非非因果地优化过去预测。

Result: 在nuScenes和DeepScenario数据集上的广泛评估表明，该方法在高重规划频率下比完全可微的闭环训练减少目标碰撞达33.24%。相比标准开环基线，在密集环境中减少碰撞达27.74%，同时提高多模态预测多样性和车道对齐。

Conclusion: 分离式滚动时域推演通过切断计算图连接，有效解决了闭环仿真中的捷径学习问题，使自动驾驶模型能够学习真实的恢复行为，显著提高了轨迹预测的鲁棒性和安全性。

Abstract: Current open-loop trajectory models struggle in real-world autonomous driving because minor initial deviations often cascade into compounding errors, pushing the agent into out-of-distribution states. While fully differentiable closed-loop simulators attempt to address this, they suffer from shortcut learning: the loss gradients flow backward through induced state inputs, inadvertently leaking future ground truth information directly into the model's own previous predictions. The model exploits these signals to artificially avoid drift, non-causally "regretting" past mistakes rather than learning genuinely reactive recovery. To address this, we introduce a detached receding horizon rollout. By explicitly severing the computation graph between simulation steps, the model learns genuine recovery behaviors from drifted states, forcing it to "rectify" mistakes rather than non-causally optimizing past predictions. Extensive evaluations on the nuScenes and DeepScenario datasets show our approach yields more robust recovery strategies, reducing target collisions by up to 33.24% compared to fully differentiable closed-loop training at high replanning frequencies. Furthermore, compared to standard open-loop baselines, our non-differentiable framework decreases collisions by up to 27.74% in dense environments while simultaneously improving multi-modal prediction diversity and lane alignment.

</details>


### [27] [VTAM: Video-Tactile-Action Models for Complex Physical Interaction Beyond VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23481)
*Haoran Yuan,Weigang Yi,Zhenyu Zhang,Wendi Chen,Yuchen Mo,Jiashi Yin,Xinzhuo Li,Xiangyu Zeng,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu,Katherine Driggs-Campbell,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VTAM通过将触觉感知集成到视频动作模型中，解决了纯视觉模型在接触丰富场景中的局限性，显著提升了精细力控和接触转换的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频动作模型在长时程任务中表现出色，但在接触丰富的场景中存在局限性，因为精细的力调节和接触转换无法仅从视觉信息中可靠编码，导致行为不稳定或不精确。

Method: VTAM通过轻量级模态迁移微调，将触觉流集成到预训练的视频变换器中，无需触觉-语言配对数据或独立的触觉预训练。引入触觉正则化损失来稳定多模态融合，防止视觉潜在主导。

Result: VTAM在接触丰富的操作中表现出卓越性能，平均保持90%的鲁棒成功率。在需要高保真力感知的挑战性场景（如薯片拾取放置）中，比π0.5基线高出80%。

Conclusion: 集成触觉反馈对于纠正世界动作模型中的视觉估计误差至关重要，为物理基础的具体化基础模型提供了可扩展的方法。

Abstract: Video-Action Models (VAMs) have emerged as a promising framework for embodied intelligence, learning implicit world dynamics from raw video streams to produce temporally consistent action predictions. Although such models demonstrate strong performance on long-horizon tasks through visual reasoning, they remain limited in contact-rich scenarios where critical interaction states are only partially observable from vision alone. In particular, fine-grained force modulation and contact transitions are not reliably encoded in visual tokens, leading to unstable or imprecise behaviors. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Video-Tactile Action Model (VTAM), a multimodal world modeling framework that incorporates tactile perception as a complementary grounding signal. VTAM augments a pretrained video transformer with tactile streams via a lightweight modality transfer finetuning, enabling efficient cross-modal representation learning without tactile-language paired data or independent tactile pretraining. To stabilize multimodal fusion, we introduce a tactile regularization loss that enforces balanced cross-modal attention, preventing visual latent dominance in the action model. VTAM demonstrates superior performance in contact-rich manipulation, maintaining a robust success rate of 90 percent on average. In challenging scenarios such as potato chip pick-and-place requiring high-fidelity force awareness, VTAM outperforms the pi 0.5 baseline by 80 percent. Our findings demonstrate that integrating tactile feedback is essential for correcting visual estimation errors in world action models, providing a scalable approach to physically grounded embodied foundation models.

</details>

*J. P. Velasquez-Rodriguez*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了紧致p-adic李群上不变算子的乘子定理，建立了Littlewood-Paley分解，并证明了Vladimirov-Taibleson算子的有界函数在紧致Vilenkin群上的L^r有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究紧致p-adic李群上不变算子的乘子理论，为p-adic分析中的函数空间理论和算子理论提供基础工具，特别是针对Vladimirov-Taibleson算子这类p-adic分析中的核心算子。

Method: 利用Ruzhansky-Turunen差分算子和Saloff-Coste条件，建立紧致p-adic李群上不变算子在L^r_α空间上的乘子定理，并应用这些定理证明Littlewood-Paley分解。

Result: 证明了紧致p-adic李群上不变算子的乘子定理，建立了Littlewood-Paley分解，并证明了Vladimirov-Taibleson算子的有界函数在紧致Vilenkin群上的L^r有界性。

Conclusion: 该工作为p-adic分析中的函数空间理论和算子理论提供了新的工具，特别是为Vladimirov-Taibleson算子的谱理论及其函数演算建立了理论基础。

Abstract: Let $p$ be a prime number, and let $\mathbb{G}$ be a compact $p$-adic Lie group. This work provides multiplier theorems for invariant operators on $\mathbb{G}$ acting on $L^r_α(\mathbb{G})$, $1<r<\infty$, $α>0$, in terms of the Ruzhansky-Turunen difference operators and Saloff-Coste's condition. As an application, a Littlewood-Paley decomposition is proven, together with the $L^r$-boundedness of bounded functions of the Vladimirov-Taibleson operator on compact Vilenkin groups.

</details>


### [29] [Classify all representation which contains a Steinberg in its hyperspecial subgroup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22931)
*Runze Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文分类了所有不可约光滑表示，这些表示在超特殊极大紧子群的pro-幂零根的不动向量空间中包含有限约化群的Steinberg表示，证明了这些表示必须是Iwahori球面表示，且每个主系列恰好有一个这样的不可约子商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Dipendra Prasad在其问题列表中提出的问题：分类所有不可约光滑表示，使得在超特殊极大紧子群的pro-幂零根的不动向量空间中，作为有限约化群（由超特殊子群除以其pro-幂零根得到）的表示，包含Steinberg表示。

Method: 通过证明任何此类表示必须是Iwahori球面表示（因此是某个非分歧主系列的子商），然后详细分析Iwahori-Hecke代数在Iwahori不动空间上的作用，证明在每个主系列中恰好存在一个包含Steinberg表示的不可约子商，并给出该子商的显式构造。

Result: 建立了非分歧特征值的Weyl群轨道与包含Steinberg表示的不可约表示之间的双射。分类比Prasad的预期更丰富：除了Steinberg表示的扭曲和一般非分歧表示外，每个主系列都贡献一个具有所需性质的唯一子商。

Conclusion: 该论文完全解决了Prasad提出的分类问题，通过建立有限Iwahori-Hecke代数与p-adic群上紧支I-双不变函数构成的有限Hecke代数之间的同构，并比较它们在Iwahori不动向量上的作用，实现了对包含Steinberg表示的不可约光滑表示的完整分类。

Abstract: This paper addresses Question 1 posed by Dipendra Prasad in his recent problem list: classify all irreducible smooth representations of an unramified reductive p-adic group such that the space of vectors fixed by the pro-unipotent radical of a hyperspecial maximal compact subgroup, viewed as a representation of the finite reductive group obtained as the quotient of that hyperspecial subgroup by its pro-unipotent radical, contains the Steinberg representation. We prove that any such representation must be Iwahori-spherical, hence a subquotient of some unramified principal series. By a detailed analysis of the action of the Iwahori--Hecke algebra on the Iwahori-fixed space, we show that in each principal series there exists exactly one irreducible subquotient containing the Steinberg representation, and we give an explicit construction of this subquotient. Hence this gives a bijection between Weyl group orbits of unramified characters and irreducible representations containing the Steinberg representation in their hyperspecial subgroup. This classification is richer than Prasad's expectation: besides twists of the Steinberg representation and generic unramified representations, every principal series contributes a unique subquotient with the required property. The proof relies on establishing an isomorphism between the finite Iwahori--Hecke algebra of the finite Lie group obtained as the quotient of the hyperspecial subgroup by its pro-unipotent radical and the finite Hecke algebra of the p-adic group consisting of compactly supported I-bi-invariant functions on the hyperspecial subgroup, and comparing their actions on the Iwahori-fixed vectors.

</details>

Method: 通过建立条件，证明ind-coherent卷积范畴可以定义完全扩展的拓扑场论，特别应用于大仿射Hecke范畴。

Result: 成功证明大仿射Hecke范畴定义了一个定向、完全扩展的拓扑场论，并建立了更一般的ind-coherent卷积范畴的拓扑场论条件。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了拓扑场论与表示论的联系，将有限Hecke范畴的结果推广到大仿射Hecke范畴和更一般的卷积范畴框架。

Abstract: We show that the large affine Hecke category defines an oriented, fully extended topological field theory. More generally, we establish conditions under which ind-coherent convolution categories define such theories, analogously to known results for finite Hecke categories and $D$-modules.

</details>


### [31] [Cambrian lattices are fractionally Calabi-Yau via 2-cluster combinatorics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23354)
*Markus Kleinau*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 证明了任意有向有限型Coxeter图Γ对应的Cambrian格C_Γ的表示范畴是分数Calabi-Yau的，验证了Chapoton猜想，并计算了Calabi-Yau维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Chapoton关于Cambrian格表示范畴分数Calabi-Yau性质的猜想，将Rognerud对A型线性定向（Tamari格）的结果推广到任意有限型Coxeter图。

Method: 对于结晶型Γ，将C_Γ视为遗传代数Λ的挠类格，引入并研究一类特殊区间，其组合结构与Λ的2-丛范畴中2-丛倾斜对象的组合结构相匹配。

Result: 证明了任意Γ对应的Cambrian格C_Γ的表示范畴是分数Calabi-Yau的，并计算了其Calabi-Yau维数，推广了Tamari格的结果。

Conclusion: 验证了Chapoton猜想，建立了Cambrian格与丛范畴理论之间的深刻联系，为分数Calabi-Yau范畴的研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: Reading constructed a Cambrian lattice $C_Γ$ for each oriented finite type Coxeter diagram $Γ$. We show that the derived category of representations of $C_Γ$ is fractionally Calabi-Yau for any $Γ$, confirming a conjecture of Chapoton. This extends a result of Rognerud for Cambrian lattices of type $A$ with linear orientation, better known as Tamari lattices. If $Γ$ is crystallographic, then $C_Γ$ is given by the lattice of torsion classes of any hereditary algebra $Λ$ of type $Γ$. In this case we introduce and study a class of intervals in $C_Γ$ whose combinatorics matches the combinatorics of $2$-cluster tilting objects in the 2-cluster category of $Λ$. This allows us to compute the Calabi-Yau dimension of $C_Γ$.

</details>


### [32] [Perturbations of Dirac Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23453)
*Steffen Schmidt*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 研究基本经典李超代数的相对三次Dirac算子在彩色量子Weil代数统一形式下的扰动，得到三类互补的扰动和相应不变量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在彩色量子Weil代数的统一框架下系统研究相对三次Dirac算子的扰动理论，探索不同类型的扰动如何产生互补的不变量理论

Method: 使用彩色量子Weil代数的统一形式，定义三类扰动：半单扰动（参数化为有限维单超模的有限半单轨道集）、幂零扰动（参数化为二次李子超代数的自交换簇）、Weil协变微分扰动（由彩色量子Weil代数中的通用1-形式和Weil微分构造）

Result: 获得三类互补的不变量：1) 半单扰动产生有限维单超模的半单轨道集和非典型性度量空间；2) 幂零扰动产生结合Dirac上同调和Duflo-Serganova上同调的上同调理论族；3) Weil协变微分扰动产生Chern型不变量，将每个有限维模映射到Weil复形的上同调类

Conclusion: 彩色量子Weil代数框架为相对三次Dirac算子的扰动提供了统一视角，三类扰动产生互补且丰富的代数不变量理论，连接了半单轨道、上同调理论和Chern型不变量

Abstract: We study perturbations of relative cubic Dirac operators for basic classical Lie superalgebras within the uniform formalism of the colour quantum Weil algebra. This perspective leads to three complementary classes of perturbations and resulting invariants. First, we define semisimple perturbations that assign to each finite-dimensional simple supermodule a finite collection of semisimple orbits, together with canonically defined vector spaces measuring the degree of atypicality. Second, we introduce nilpotent perturbations parametrized by the self-commuting variety of a quadratic Lie subsuperalgebra; the resulting family of cohomology theories combines Dirac cohomology and Duflo--Serganova cohomology. Third, we deform the cubic Dirac operator by a Weil-covariant differential built from the universal $1$-form in the colour quantum Weil algebra and the Weil differential, producing a Chern-type invariant that assigns to each finite-dimensional module a natural class in the cohomology of the Weil complex.

</details>

Method: 使用多层感知机进行专门的事件选择，结合稳健的噪声抑制技术，采用现象学背景模型搜索标准晕模型预期的年度调制信号，并整合Migdal效应扩展灵敏度至亚GeV/c²区域。

Abstract: We report new constraints on the spin-dependent scattering cross section between low-mass dark matter and protons using data collected by the COSINE-100 experiment. By implementing a specialized event selection process using a multi-layer perceptron and robust noise mitigation, this analysis pioneers a detection threshold of 3 and 4 isolated peaks, corresponding to the reconstructed photoelectrons, which is significantly lower than the 8 photoelectron threshold used in previous analyses. In this unstudied few-photoelectron regime, where PMT-induced noise and phosphorescence are prevalent, we utilize a phenomenological background model to search for the annual modulation signal expected from the Standard Halo Model. No statistically significant annual modulation is observed in our data. We derive new 90% confidence level upper limits for the spin-dependent DM-proton cross section, establishing the world's most stringent constraints in the 1.75-2.25 GeV/c$^2$ mass range. Furthermore, by incorporating the Migdal effect, we extend the experimental sensitivity to the sub-GeV/c$^2$ regime, setting world-leading limits in the 15-58 MeV/c$^2$ range. These results demonstrate the capability of NaI(Tl) target materials to probe previously unexplored regions of the dark matter parameter space.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 使用ICARUS探测器（液氩时间投影室）收集2022-2023年BNB束流数据，选择μ子中微子通过带电流相互作用与氩核反应产生μ子和至少一个质子的末态（1μNp），与基于模拟的预期进行比较，在3+1模型框架下进行拟合分析。

Result: 未发现统计显著的μ子中微子消失现象，给出了Δm²₄₁ - sin²2θₘₘ参数空间的90%置信水平排除轮廓，分析受限于通量和相互作用模型的大系统误差。

Abstract: We present a search for muon neutrino disappearance in the Booster Neutrino Beam (BNB) at Fermilab using the ICARUS detector. Neutrino interactions identified as muon neutrinos interacting with argon nuclei via the charged current interaction and having only a muon and at least one proton in the final state (1$μ$Np) have been selected from data collected in 2022-2023 (ICARUS Run 2) and compared with a simulation-based expectation. In the context of a fit to a two-neutrino approximation of the sterile 3+1 model, including the impact of systematic uncertainty from the flux, neutrino interaction, and detector models, we find no statistically significant muon neutrino disappearance at the ICARUS baseline of 600 meters from the BNB target. Corresponding 90% C.L. exclusion contours in $Δm^2_{41}$ - sin$^22θ_{μμ}$ space are presented. This is the first oscillation analysis produced by ICARUS exposed to the BNB. We note that the analysis is systematics limited due to large unconstrained uncertainties from the flux and interaction models. In future joint analyses, data from ICARUS and the SBND detector, exposed to the BNB at 110 meters from target, will be combined to provide significant constraint of these uncertainties, enabling a robust, world-leading two-detector analysis.

Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,J. H. Zou*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 首次在粲介子辐射衰变到轴矢量介子中检验矢量介子主导机制，未发现显著信号，给出了分支比上限


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验矢量介子主导机制在粲介子辐射衰变到轴矢量介子过程中的适用性，这是该机制在此类衰变中的首次测试

Method: 使用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上收集的20.3 fb⁻¹正负电子湮灭数据，质心能量为3.773 GeV，搜索D⁰→γK̅₁(1270)⁰和D⁺→γK₁(1270)⁺辐射衰变

Result: 未观察到显著信号，在90%置信水平下确定D⁰→γK̅₁(1270)⁰和D⁺→γK₁(1270)⁺分支比上限分别为7.7×10⁻⁴和3.9×10⁻⁵

Conclusion: 首次在粲介子辐射衰变到轴矢量介子中测试了矢量介子主导机制，为理解此类衰变过程提供了重要约束

Abstract: A search for the radiative decays $D^0\to γ\bar K_1(1270)^0$ and $D^+\to γK_1(1270)^+$ is conducted using $20.3~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ of $e^+e^-$ annihilation data collected at the center-of-mass energy $\sqrt{s}=3.773$ GeV by the BESIII detector operating at the BEPCII collider. No significant signals are observed, and upper limits on the branching fractions of $D^0\to γ\bar K_1(1270)^0$ and $D^+\to γK_1(1270)^+$ at 90\% confidence level are determined to be $7.7\times10^{-4}$ and $3.9\times10^{-5}$, respectively. This represents the first test of the Vector Meson Dominance mechanism in the radiative decays of charmed mesons to axial-vector mesons.

</details>

<details>

Conclusion: 首次实现了J/ψ→γηπ⁰过程的振幅分析，发现了多个中间衰变过程，特别是首次观测到J/ψ到同位旋三重态标量介子的辐射跃迁，分支比测量精度较先前提高了一倍以上。

Abstract: Using $(10087 \pm 44)\times 10^{6}$ $\jpsi$ events collected with the BESIII detector, we perform the first amplitude analysis of the process $\jpsi\toγη\piz$. The decay is dominated by the intermediate processes $\jpsi\to\piz \bo \left( \toγη\right)$, $\jpsi\to\pizρ(1450)^0 \left( \toγη\right)$ and $\jpsi\toηh_1(1170) \left( \toγ\piz\right)$. Contributions from $\jpsi\toγa_0(980)^0(\toη\piz)$, $\jpsi\toγa_2(1320)^0(\toη\piz)$ and $\jpsi\toγa_2(1700)^0(\toη\piz)$ are observed with a statistical significance exceeding $5σ$, constituting the first observation of radiative transitions of $\jpsi$ to isospin-triplet scalar mesons. The total branching fraction of $\jpsi\toγη\piz$ is measured to be \num{25.7\pm0.3\pm1.5e-6}, where the first uncertainty is statistical and the second systematic. This result is consistent with the previous measurement, with the precision improved by more than a factor of two.

Method: KATRIN将使用新开发的TRISTAN探测器——一种硅漂移探测器阵列，专门为高计数率和远低于端点的能量区域的微分测量而优化。研究采用专门的模拟框架来评估灵敏度，分析β衰变电子能谱中由惰性中微子混合引起的扭折状畸变。

Result: 模拟显示，在4个月探测器运行时间内，KATRIN具有足够的统计能力，在(4-13)keV质量范围内探测到|U_{e4}|² ~ 10⁻⁶水平的混合振幅，显著超越了先前实验室搜索的范围。但主要的实验系统不确定性会使同一质量范围内的灵敏度降低10-50倍。

Conclusion: KATRIN实验在完成中微子质量测量后，通过TRISTAN探测器扩展物理研究范围，有望在keV尺度惰性中微子搜索中取得突破性进展，但需要仔细控制实验系统误差以确保最佳灵敏度。

Abstract: Sterile neutrinos in the keV mass range are a well-motivated extension of the Standard Model and viable dark matter candidates. Their existence can be probed in laboratory experiments, as the admixture of a sterile state would induce a characteristic kink-like distortion in the $β$-decay electron energy spectrum. The KATRIN experiment is designed to measure the effective electron neutrino mass with sub-eV sensitivity by analyzing the endpoint region of the tritium $β$-decay spectrum. Following the completion of its neutrino mass program, KATRIN will extend its physics reach to the search for keV-scale sterile neutrinos. This effort will be enabled by the TRISTAN detector, a newly developed silicon drift detector array optimized for differential measurements at high rates and energies well below the endpoint. In this article, we present the projected sensitivity of KATRIN to keV-scale sterile neutrinos using a dedicated simulation framework. With four months of detector livetime, KATRIN has the statistical power to probe mixing amplitudes at the level of $|U_{e4}|^2 \sim 10^{-6}$ for sterile neutrino masses in the (4$-$13) keV range, significantly extending the reach of previous laboratory searches. The major experimental systematic uncertainties investigated in this work reduces the sensitivity by a factor of 10$-$50 over the same mass range.

</details>

Result: 获得了ψ(4230)、ψ(4500)、ψ(4660)和ψ(4710)四个共振结构的质量和宽度参数。发现e⁺e⁻→Ds⁺Ds₁*⁻(2536)、Ds⁺Ds₂*⁻(2573)和φχ_{c1,2}过程主要来自ψ(4660)和ψ(4710)衰变的贡献，表明这些共振态在Ds介子对和φχ_c衰变道中具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 通过多道同时拟合方法成功提取了四个粲偶素激发态的参数，揭示了ψ(4660)和ψ(4710)在Ds介子对和φχ_c衰变道中的主导作用，为理解这些高激发态粲偶素的衰变性质提供了重要实验依据。

Abstract: We analysis the $\sqrt{s}$-dependent line shapes of the $e^+e^-\to D_s^{+}D_{s1}^{*-}(2536)$, $D_s^{+}D_{s2}^{*-}(2573)$, $φχ_{c1,2}$, $K^+K^-J/ψ$, $K_S^0 K_S^0 J/ψ$, and $K^+K^-ψ(2S)$ cross sections measured by the BESIII experiment using the four resonant structures $ψ(4230)$, $ψ(4500)$, $ψ(4660)$, and $ψ(4710)$, by performing a simultaneous $χ^2$-minimized fit. Their masses and widths are obtained. We find that the processes $e^+e^-\to D_s^{+}D_{s1}^{*-}(2536)$, $e^+e^-\to D_s^{+}D_{s2}^{*-}(2573)$, and $e^+e^-\to φχ_{c1,2}$ are all dominantly produced via the $ψ(4660)$ and $ψ(4710)$ decays.

### [39] [Search for new particles decaying into top quark-antiquark pairs in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23454)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中搜索新粒子衰变到顶夸克-反顶夸克对，使用138 fb⁻¹数据，排除了一系列重Z'玻色子、Kaluza-Klein胶子和暗物质媒介粒子，设定了最严格的tt̄终态限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过顶夸克-反顶夸克对衰变通道寻找超出标准模型的新物理粒子，特别是重Z'玻色子、额外维度模型中的Kaluza-Klein胶子、暗物质媒介粒子以及双希格斯二重态模型中的标量和赝标量希格斯玻色子。

Method: 使用CMS探测器在2016-2018年收集的13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据，对应138 fb⁻¹积分亮度。分析0、1和2轻子终态，覆盖顶夸克-反顶夸克对的所有衰变模式。针对不同相对宽度的重Z'玻色子、Randall-Sundrum模型中的Kaluza-Klein胶子、暗物质媒介粒子以及双希格斯二重态模型中的标量和赝标量希格斯玻色子进行搜索。

Result: 排除了相对宽度为1%、10%和30%的重Z'玻色子，质量范围分别为0.4-4.8 TeV、0.4-6.2 TeV和0.4-7.4 TeV。排除了Randall-Sundrum模型中质量0.5-5.5 TeV的Kaluza-Klein胶子和质量1.0-4.2 TeV的暗物质媒介粒子。在双希格斯二重态模型中，对质量0.5-1.0 TeV、相对宽度2.5%、10%和25%的标量和赝标量希格斯玻色子设定了耦合强度修正因子上限。

Conclusion: 该研究为顶夸克-反顶夸克对衰变通道中的新物理粒子搜索设定了迄今为止最严格的限制，排除了多个理论模型在特定质量范围内的粒子存在可能性，对超出标准模型的新物理探索具有重要意义。

Abstract: A search for new particles decaying to top quark-antiquark pairs is performed using proton-proton collision data at a centre-of-mass energy of 13 TeV. The data set recorded with the CMS detector between 2016 and 2018 is used, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. Final states with 0, 1, and 2 leptons are analyzed, covering all decay modes of the top quark-antiquark pairs. Heavy Z' bosons with relative widths of 1, 10, and 30% are excluded for masses in the ranges 0.4$-$4.8, 0.4$-$6.2, and 0.4$-$7.4 TeV, respectively. A Kaluza$-$Klein gluon in the Randall$-$Sundrum model and a dark-matter mediator are excluded for masses between 0.5$-$5.5 and 1.0$-$4.2 TeV, respectively. These results set the most stringent limits to date for the considered models in the $\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$ final state. In addition, in the two-Higgs-doublet models, upper limits are set on the coupling strength modifier for scalar and pseudoscalar Higgs bosons with relative widths of 2.5, 10, and 25% in the mass range of 0.5$-$1.0 TeV.

</details>

### [40] [Modelling SARS-CoV-2 epidemics via compartmental and cellular automaton SEIRS model with temporal immunity and vaccination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22498)
*J. Ilnytskyi,T. Patsahan*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: SEIRS流行病模型分析COVID-19疫情特征，包括未识别感染者、有限免疫期和疫苗接种，研究隔离和疫苗接种对基本再生数的影响，通过细胞自动机模拟社交距离效应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究COVID-19疫情动态控制策略，特别关注未识别感染者、有限免疫期和疫苗接种等因素对疫情传播的影响，为平衡隔离和疫苗接种措施提供理论依据

Method: 采用SEIRS流行病学模型，包含未识别感染者、有限免疫期和疫苗接种特征；通过隔室模型分析稳定状态和基本再生数；使用异步细胞自动机算法实现基于格点的模型，研究社交距离效应

Result: 确定了无病和流行病的稳定静止状态；分析了基本再生数与隔离和疫苗接种措施的关系；数值计算了疫情第一波峰值的位置和高度并拟合为代数形式；通过细胞自动机模拟发现社交距离（邻域大小变化）对疫情传播的影响

Conclusion: SEIRS模型能有效描述COVID-19疫情特征，隔离和疫苗接种措施可协同控制疫情传播，社交距离通过改变邻域大小影响传播动态，为疫情控制策略提供量化依据

Abstract: We consider the SEIRS epidemiology model with such features of the COVID-19 outbreak as: abundance of unidentified infected individuals, limited time of immunity and a possibility of vaccination. The control of the pandemic dynamics is possible by restricting the transmission rate, increasing identification and isolation rate of infected individuals, and via vaccination. For the compartmental version of this model, we found stable disease-free and endemic stationary states. The basic reproductive number is analysed with respect to balancing quarantine and vaccination measures. The positions and heights of the first peak of outbreak are obtained numerically and fitted to simple in usage algebraic forms. Lattice-based realization of this model is studied by means of the asynchronous cellular automaton algorithm. This permitted to study the effect of social distancing by varying the neighbourhood size of the model. The attempt is made to match the quarantine and vaccination effects.

</details>


### [41] [Balancing training load, rest and musculoskeletal injury risk: a mathematical modelling study in Thoroughbred racehorses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22680)
*Md Nurul Anwar,Michael Pan,Ashleigh V. Morrice-West,Fatemeh Malekipour,Peter Pivonka,Jennifer A. Flegg,R Chris Whitton,Peta L. Hitchens*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 通过数学模型分析赛马训练计划对骨骼损伤风险的影响，发现低强度高速度训练、增加休息频率和延长休息时间可降低骨骼损伤风险


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯血赛马的肌肉骨骼损伤是导致死亡和提前退役的主要原因，与训练实践密切相关。高速度训练累积距离与骨骼损伤相关，理解训练如何影响骨骼损伤积累对改善马匹福利和比赛结果至关重要

Method: 基于现有骨骼适应和损伤数学模型，构建多种训练计划（3种渐进式、4种比赛适应、6种休息计划，以及2种用低强度训练替代休息的计划），反映澳大利亚维多利亚州专业训练师的代表性实践。训练计划在训练量、休息频率和持续时间上有所不同

Result: 低强度但包含高速训练的计划能在实现足够骨骼适应的同时减少骨骼损伤积累，从而降低骨骼失效风险。增加休息频率（每年至少2次）和/或延长休息时间（至少6周）能提供更多修复机会，减少骨骼损伤

Conclusion: 研究提供了骨骼对训练反应的计算机数学模型，展示了训练计划对骨骼适应、损伤形成和修复的影响。研究结果可指导设计平衡骨骼适应和骨骼健康的训练计划，贯穿赛马整个职业生涯

Abstract: Musculoskeletal injuries (MSI) in Thoroughbred racehorses are a leading cause of death and premature retirement in racehorses and are heavily influenced by training practices. Greater distances of high-speed galloping accumulated during racing campaigns are associated with MSI. Bone injury is the most common MSI, and understanding how training practices influence bone damage accumulation is critical for improving both horse welfare and racing outcomes. This study builds on an existing mathematical model of bone adaptation and damage to investigate the impact of different training programs on bone injury risk. Several training programs (three progressive, four race-fit, six rest programs and two with rest replaced by low-intensity training) were constructed to reflect representative practices undertaken by professional trainers in Victoria, Australia. Training programs varied in training volume, rest frequency and program duration. Lower volume training programs that included high-speed training, achieved sufficient bone adaptation with less accumulation of bone damage, and subsequently lower risk of bone failure. In addition, incorporating more frequent rests (at least 2 per year) and/or longer rest periods (at least 6 weeks) reduced bone damage due to the extended opportunity to remove and repair bone damage. These results provide an in-silico mathematical model of the bone response to training, demonstrating the effects of training programs on bone adaptation, damage formation and repair. The findings can guide the design of training programs that balance both bone adaptation and bone health throughout horses racing career.

</details>
