### [42] [Should the Olympic sprint skaters run the 500 meter twice?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22298)
*Nils Lid Hjort*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 研究证实速滑500米比赛的最后内道存在约0.05秒的不公平劣势，导致国际滑冰联盟修改规则，要求选手在内外道各滑一次取平均成绩


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 速滑500米比赛中，抽签决定内外道起跑，选手普遍担心最后内道在高速（约55公里/小时）下需要克服更大的离心力，特别是在疲劳时，这可能造成比赛不公平

Method: 利用1984-1994年十一届世界短距离锦标赛数据，采用双变量混合效应模型，考虑冰面天气条件差异、选手水平差异、前100米通过时间，以及内外道信息，精确估计"不公平参数"

Result: 估计的不公平参数约为0.05秒，显著不为零（约三倍标准差），这一差异足以改变奖牌归属

Conclusion: 研究结果促使国际滑冰联盟和国际奥委会修改规则，从1998年长野冬奥会开始，500米选手需在内外道各滑一次取平均成绩，世界锦标赛也从1996年起采用相同规则

Abstract: The Olympic 500 meter sprint competition is the `Formula One event' of speed skating, and is watched by millions of television viewers. A draw decides who should start in inner lane and who in outer lane. Many skaters dread the last inner lane, where they need to tackle heavier centrifugal forces than their companions in the last outer lane, at maximum speed around 55 km/hour, at a time when fatigue may set in. The aim of this article is to investigate this potential difference between last inner and last outer lane. For this purpose data from eleven Sprint World Championships 1984--1994 are exploited. A bivariate mixed effects model is used that in addition to the inner-outer lane information takes account of different ice and weather conditions on different days, unequal levels for different skaters, and the passing times for the first 100 meter. The underlying `unfairness parameter', estimated with optimal precision, is about 0.05 seconds, and is indeed significantly different from zero; it is about three times as large as its estimated standard deviation. This is enough for medals to change necks.
  Results from the work reported on here played a decisive role in leading the International Skating Union and the International Olympic Committee to change the rules for the 500 meter sprint event; as of the Nagano 1998 Olympic Games, the sprinters are to skate twice, with one start in inner lane and one in outer lane. The best average result determines the final list, and the best skaters from the first run are paired to skate last in the second run. It has also been decided that the same rules shall apply for the single distance 500 meter World Championships;these are arranged yearly from 1996 onwards.

</details>

*Lizzie Neumann,Philipp Wittenberg,Jan Gertheiss*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究结构健康监测中环境因素对系统输出统计特性的影响，提出并比较了两种量化环境因素对输出协方差和相关性影响的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构健康监测中的系统输出（如特征频率、应变数据）不仅受损伤影响，还受环境条件干扰。传统方法通常只假设环境条件影响输出的均值，但实际数据表明环境条件还会影响高阶统计矩，包括方差、协方差和相关性。需要开发能够识别和量化这些多元混淆效应的方法。

Method: 提出了两种方法来识别和量化环境因素对输出协方差和相关性的影响：1）随机森林方法；2）非参数核方法。在人工数据和真实世界SHM数据上比较了这两种竞争方法。

Result: 核方法在准确性方面表现更好，而随机森林方法产生的估计更稳健，有时更容易解释。两种方法都能有效识别环境因素对高阶统计矩的影响。

Conclusion: 环境条件不仅影响结构健康监测输出的均值，还显著影响其高阶统计矩。核方法在准确性上优于随机森林，但随机森林在稳健性和可解释性方面有优势。需要根据具体应用需求选择合适的方法来量化环境因素的混淆效应。

Abstract: System outputs such as eigenfrequencies or strain data, often used in structural health monitoring (SHM), not only react to damage but also depend on environmental conditions. When trying to correct for these confounding effects, it is often (at least implicitly) assumed that only the expected, i.e., mean, output values are affected by environmental conditions. However, the evaluation of real-world SHM data indicates that environmental conditions may influence not only the mean output but also higher-order statistical moments, particularly the variances of and the covariances and correlations between the output quantities, such as eigenfrequencies of different modes or strain sensors at different locations. To address these issues, we discuss two approaches for identifying and quantifying multivariate confounding effects on output covariances and correlations: a random forest and a nonparametric, kernel-based approach. We compare the two competing methods on both artificial and real-world SHM data, finding that the kernel-based approach achieves higher accuracy, but the random forest produces estimates that are more robust and sometimes easier to interpret.

</details>

Result: Android地震预警系统在2025年土耳其马尔马拉地震中成功向用户提供高精度预警，在最强震动到达城市地区前提供超过1分钟的预警时间。统计分析显示用户信任与预警及时性之间存在显著相关性，研究发现"及时性就是用户心中的准确性"。

Abstract: Android's Earthquake Alert (AEA) system provided timely early warnings to millions during the Mw 6.2 Marmara Ereglisi, Türkiye earthquake on April 23, 2025. This event, the largest in the region in 25 years, served as a critical real-world test for smartphone-based Earthquake Early Warning (EEW) systems. The AEA system successfully delivered alerts to users with high precision, offering over a minute of warning before the strongest shaking reached urban areas. This study leveraged Large Language Models (LLMs) to analyze more than 500 public social media posts from the X platform, extracting 42 distinct attributes related to user experience and behavior. Statistical analyses revealed significant relationships, notably a strong correlation between user trust and alert timeliness. Our results indicate a distinction between engineering and the user-centric definition of system accuracy. We found that timeliness is accuracy in the user's mind. Overall, this study provides actionable insights for optimizing alert design, public education campaigns, and future behavioral research to improve the effectiveness of such systems in seismically active regions.

Abstract: It is shown that a generalization of the Painlevé-II equation (P-II) to a system of coupled equations with symmetry breaking terms is integrable. A Lax pair for this system is used to relate the asymptotic behavior of the solutions at different infinities via an asymptotically exact WKB approach. The analysis relies on an exact solution of the quantum mechanical Demkov-Osherov model (DOM). An application to the problem of unstable vacuum decay during a second order phase transition provides precise scaling of the number of excitations, including subdominant contributions.

*Junshan Lin,Hai Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米光子晶体中谱互易性破缺导致边缘模式（趋肤效应），但离散晶格模型的Toeplitz矩阵谱理论不适用于连续波模型，需要新的数学框架来描述连续介质中的趋肤效应。

Method: 采用传递矩阵方法描述一维周期性介质中的波传播，基于传递矩阵特征值定义新的谱拓扑不变量，该不变量等价于非厄米谱的绕数。

Result: 新拓扑不变量能够表征一维非厄米光子晶体中的边缘模式，为连续波模型中的趋肤效应提供了理论基础，建立了离散晶格模型与连续介质模型之间的数学联系。

Conclusion: 基于传递矩阵特征值的谱拓扑不变量为理解连续波模型中的趋肤效应提供了有效的数学工具，填补了非厄米光子晶体理论中离散与连续模型之间的理论空白。

Abstract: This work investigates edge modes in non-Hermitian photonic crystals with broken spectral reciprocity. In such systems, the spectra of the underlying operators generally form closed loops over the complex plane with nontrivial spectral topology, which gives rise to the so-called skin effect characterized by edge modes localized at interfaces. For discrete lattice models, the skin effect can be understood through the spectral theory of Toeplitz matrices. However, this mathematical framework no longer applies to continuous wave models, where finite-dimensional approximations break down. In this work, we employ a transfer matrix approach to describe wave propagation in one-dimensional periodic media and introduce a new spectral topological invariant based on the eigenvalues of the transfer matrix. The new topological invariant is equivalent to the winding number of the non-Hermitian spectrum and it enables the characterization of edge modes in one-dimensional non-Hermitian photonic crystals. The mathematical theory provides the theoretical foundation for the skin effect in continuous wave models.

</details>


### [47] [Edge density expansions for the classical Gaussian and Laguerre ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22974)
*Peter J. Forrester,Anas A. Rahman,Bo-Jian Shen*

Main category: math-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bornemann最近的工作揭示了高斯和Laguerre随机矩阵系综软边处间距分布和特征值密度渐近展开中的可积结构。本文旨在从不同视角重新审视这些结果，并将类似研究扩展到Laguerre系综的硬边情况，探索更广泛的β系综模型中的可积特征。

Method: 使用标量微分方程作为主要工具，这些方程满足软边缩放密度。与积分表示不同，这些微分方程在软边缩放变量中分离了作为展开变量的N函数。对于Laguerre系综的硬边情况，推导了相应的微分方程，并分析了正交、酉和辛对称性情况下的修正项。

Result: 对于高斯系综，证明了Dyson指数β=6情况下类似的特征，表明经典β系综的渐近展开具有可积特征。对于Laguerre系综的硬边情况，给出了酉对称性下二阶修正以及正交和辛对称性下一阶修正的显式形式，并展示了各种微分关系。

Conclusion: 微分方程方法为Bornemann发现的软边可积结构提供了新的视角，并将研究扩展到硬边情况。结果表明，更广泛的经典β系综模型在渐近展开中可能普遍存在可积特征，微分方程方法能够提供积分表示无法获得的关于修正项的补充信息。

Abstract: Recent work of Bornemann has uncovered hitherto hidden integrable structures relating to the asymptotic expansion of quantities at the soft edge of Gaussian and Laguerre random matrix ensembles. These quantities are spacing distributions and the eigenvalue density, and the findings cover the cases of the three symmetry classes orthogonal, unitary and symplectic. In this work we give a different viewpoint on these results in the case of the soft edge scaled density, and in the Laguerre case we initiate an analogous study at the hard edge. Our tool is the scalar differential equation satisfied by the latter, known from earlier work. Unlike integral representations, these differential equations in soft edge scaling variables isolate the function of $N$ which is the expansion variable. Moreover, they give information on the correction terms which supplements the findings from the work of Bornemann. In the case of the Gaussian ensemble, we can demonstrate analogous features for Dyson index $β= 6$, which suggests a broader class of models, namely the classical $β$ ensembles, with asymptotic expansions exhibiting integrable features. For the Laguerre ensembles at the hard edge, we give the explicit form of the correction at second order for unitary symmetry, and at first order in the orthogonal and symplectic cases. Various differential relations are demonstrated.

</details>

Abstract: In this work, we derive information-theoretic properties for a modified Tsallis entropy, hereinafter referred to as q-entropy. We introduce the notions of joint q-entropy, conditional q-entropy, relative q-entropy, conditional mutual q-information, and establish several inequalities analogous to those of classical information theory. Within the context of Markov chains, these results are employed to prove a version of the second law of thermodynamics. Furthermore, we investigate the maximum entropy method in this setting. Finally, we prove a Tsallis version of the Shannon-McMillan-Breiman theorem.

</details>


### [49] [Dirac Operators, APS Boundary Conditions, and Spectral Flow on a Finite Warped Cylinder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23275)

TL;DR: 研究有限扭曲柱面上Dirac算子与U(1)规范场的耦合，分析APS边界条件的端点算子，引入正则化APS型边界条件族以处理边界模穿越零点时的连续性，建立实辛边界表述框架。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Atiyah-Patodi-Singer边界条件在规范场家族参数变化时，当边界模穿越零点会导致投影子不连续，需要构建连续的正则化边界条件族来保持分析的一致性。

Method: 识别定义APS边界条件的内在端点算子，推导模态APS谱的行列式特征；在常数规范场可逆情形分析η贡献；引入正则化APS型自伴端点条件族，建立实辛边界表述，将零模集与边界零点集等同。

Result: 常数规范场可逆时端点约化η贡献相消，APS指标为零；正则化APS型边界条件族在边界模穿越零点时保持连续；非退化正则化下零模集与边界零点集重合，横向边界零点给出孤立正则穿越。

Abstract: We study the Dirac operator on a finite warped cylinder coupled to a background $U(1)$ gauge field. We identify the intrinsic endpoint operators defining the Atiyah-Patodi-Singer (APS) boundary condition and derive a determinant characterization of the modewise APS spectrum. In the constant-gauge, invertible setting, the endpoint reduced $η$ contributions cancel, so the APS index vanishes. For smooth gauge families, the APS projector becomes discontinuous when a boundary mode crosses zero. We therefore introduce a regularized APS-type family of self-adjoint endpoint conditions that remains continuous across such crossings. This regularized family admits a real-symplectic boundary formulation within the standard spectral-flow/Maslov framework: for nondegenerate regularization, the zero-mode set coincides with the boundary-zero set, and transverse boundary zeros give isolated regular crossings.

</details>


### [50] [Interfacial instability as a trigger for dryout inception in two-phase CO2 flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23371)
*G. Cantini,G. Arnone,F. Capone,J. A. Gianfrani,M. Carnevale*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新理论，认为两相环状流中的干涸起始是由气液界面不稳定性触发的，并通过数学模型和稳定性分析预测了临界蒸汽干度x_dry，实验数据验证了这一假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能粒子探测器冷却系统面临两个挑战：1) 焦耳效应导致过热需要有效冷却；2) 空间限制要求使用微通道冷却系统。在相对高蒸汽干度下，液相呈现环状流并提前出现干涸现象。干涸会导致传热系数急剧下降，可能引发灾难性后果。实验证据表明CO₂在相变冷却中的干涸行为与传统制冷剂不同，其基本起始机制尚未被理解。

Method: 提出了两相环状流的数学模型，通过线性理论研究两流体界面的稳定性。稳定性分析简化为求解耦合的四阶微分特征值问题，使用基于Chebyshev-τ方法的内部代码进行数值处理。数值研究识别了导致界面不稳定的临界蒸汽干度x_dry。

Result: 数值研究确定了导致界面不稳定的临界蒸汽干度x_dry。对x_dry的预测结果得到了两个独立实验活动收集的实验数据的证实，验证了干涸起始由界面不稳定性控制的假设。

Conclusion: 研究验证了干涸起始由气液界面不稳定性触发的假设，为理解CO₂相变冷却系统中干涸现象的物理机制提供了理论基础，有助于改进高能粒子探测器微通道冷却系统的设计和安全运行。

Abstract: Progress in particle physic leads to increasing in detector luminosity and a consequent increasing overheating induced by Joule effect. An effective cooling strategy is the exploitation of CO\textsubscript{2} heat latency in phase-change. An additional challenge, relevant to detectors for High Energy Particles, is the consequent geometrical constrain due to the limited space avialable for the cooling system within the detector arrangement, leading to the implementation of cooling system by means of millichannels. In this context, at relative high vapour quality the liquid phase exhibits annular flow, anticipating the dryout. Dryout is a critical condition where the heat transfer coefficient dramatically drops and dangerous temperature levels can be reached, potentially leading to catastrophic consequences. Experimental evidences reveal that its behavior in two-phase annular flows differs from conventional refrigerants and the fundamental inception-mechanism is not yet understood. This study aims at investigating the key new idea whereby dryout inception is triggered by instability of the liquid-vapour interface. A mathematical model for two-phase annular flow is presented and the stability of the interface between the two fluids is studied through the linear theory. The stability analysis reduces to solving a coupled forth-order differential eigenvalue problem that is treated numerically with an in-house code based on the Chebyshev-$τ$ method. Numerical investigations identify a critical value for the vapour quality, named $x_{dry}$, that leads to interface instability. The resulting predictions on $x_{dry}$ are confirmed by experimental data collected from two independent experimental campaigns, validating the hypothesis that dryout inception is governed by interfacial instabilities.

</details>

Abstract: We analyze the mixed Hessian of the dispersionless Toda $τ$-function for the $s$-fold symmetric one-harmonic polynomial conformal map. The inverse branch exhibits two distinct thresholds: an analytic threshold $ζ_c$, where the dominant square-root singularity reaches the circle of convergence, and a later geometric threshold $ζ_{\mathrm{univ}}>ζ_c$, where the map ceases to be univalent. We prove that the first spectral instability occurs already at $ζ_c$. In each symmetry sector, the weighted subcritical realization has exactly one logarithmically diverging eigenvalue, whereas the remaining spectrum stays bounded and, after removal of the singular direction, converges to that of a compact limiting remainder. We further continue the corresponding scalar Gram functions beyond $ζ_c$, showing that they admit a generalized hypergeometric description, a Cauchy--Stieltjes representation, and, for $1\le p\le s$, a realization as Weyl functions of bounded Jacobi operators. In particular, these scalar quantities remain finite at $ζ_{\mathrm{univ}}$. This identifies analytic criticality, rather than loss of univalence, as the first spectral threshold of the Toda Hessian.

Abstract: A lattice point $\vec x=(x_1,\dots,x_n)\in\mathbb Z^{n}$ is said to be visible if the line segment between $\vec x$ and the origin contains no other lattice point. In this paper, we compute the asymptotic density of visible lattice points on hyperplanes and their intersections. In particular, we show that the hyperplane $\vec a \cdot \vec x = b$ in $\mathbb R^{n}$ has visible point density $J_{n-1}(b)/b^{n-1}$ where $J$ is the Jordan totient function. We extend this basic result to find the density of visible points on the intersection of hyperplanes and to the density of $k$-th power free points. Finally, for a fixed dimension $n$, we consider the closure of the set of all possible densities that occur.

Abstract: We extend the relative theory of admissible pairs and $p$-adic Hodge structures introduced in Part II to allow variation in the underlying local systems of $\mathbb{Q}_p$-vector spaces and isocrystals. This extension accommodates, in particular, the families of $p$-adic Hodge structures that arise from the cohomology of certain smooth proper families. Such a variation gives rise to a cover with a global Hodge period map, and we study this cover and its period map from a differential perspective both classically and via the theory of inscription. This study is motivated by our transcendence results in Parts I and II and analogies with complex bi-algebraic geometry, and we also extend these ideas in other directions: First, we study the locus of special points on local Shimura varieties. We establish a refined version of a prediction of Rapoport-Viehmann on the density of special points, but give a robust counter-example to the local analog of the André-Oort conjecture in this setting. This shuts down a broader theory of unlikely intersection but leaves open the possibility of stronger geometric transcendence results than the bi-analytic Ax--Lindemann theorem of Part II. Using the Banach-Colmez Tangent Bundles for infinite level local Shimura varieties that arise from the theory of inscription, we define precise notions of generic and exceptional intersections and then formulate an Ax--Schanuel conjecture that we expect to refine our Ax--Lindemann theorem.

Abstract: We prove that over function fields F_q(t), the Tate-Shafarevich group |Sha| is an invariant of the cyclotomic type of the L-polynomial, so that |Sha|-stratified murmuration densities reduce to type-weighted densities with no within-type zero displacement (Theorem A). The function C(c) changes sign -- positive at small c, negative at large c -- a prediction confirmed empirically using 657,000 curves from the Cremona database. Empirically, the covariance is concentrated entirely in the Tamagawa product prod c_v: at fine L(1)-conditioning, the Tamagawa channel accounts for 100% of the signal, and cross-validated regression confirms that the nonlinear adjoint-Euler-factor weighting carries independent Tamagawa information beyond a full trace basis but adds nothing for |Sha|. Motivation: 传统上p进多重zeta值主要研究正整数索引，但整数索引可能包含零或负分量，这扩展了研究范围。需要建立包含这些更一般索引的理论框架，并验证基本代数关系是否仍然成立。

Abstract: This paper concerns the $p$-adic multiple zeta values of integer indices that may contain zero or negative components. We introduce the admissibility and regularizability conditions for integer indices. We define the $p$-adic multiple zeta values associated with admissible integer indices to be finite rational linear combinations of $p$-adic multiple zeta values associated with admissible positive integer indices. We prove that the double shuffle relations, that is, the shuffle and stuffle product formulas, both hold for the values.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二进制序列伪随机性度量（2-adic复杂度和线性复杂度）的对称版本与普通版本之间的关系，探讨对称化对这些复杂度度量的影响，特别是在周期和非周期序列中的差异。

Method: 1. 在周期序列中：分析由非回文素数的二进制展开构造的二进制周期序列，比较其对称和普通2-adic复杂度；直接证明线性复杂度在序列反转下保持不变，因此其对称版本与普通版本一致。
2. 在非周期序列中：构造显式的有限二进制序列族，使其对称复杂度显著小于普通版本；通过数学分析比较第N个有理复杂度、第N个指数线性复杂度及其对称版本的期望值。
3. 建立对称复杂度度量的下界：包括对称有理复杂度、对称2-adic复杂度、对称线性复杂度和对称指数线性复杂度。

Result: 1. 对于由非回文素数二进制展开构造的周期序列，对称2-adic复杂度可以严格小于普通2-adic复杂度。
2. 线性复杂度在序列反转下保持不变，因此其对称版本与普通版本相等。
3. 构造了有限二进制序列族，其第N个对称2-adic复杂度和第N个对称线性复杂度都显著小于普通版本。
4. 第N个有理复杂度和第N个指数线性复杂度的期望值比其对称版本至少大一个O(N)量级的项，表明对称化在指数尺度上产生明显影响。
5. 建立了对称复杂度度量的下界。

Conclusion: 对称化对二进制序列的伪随机性度量有显著影响：对于2-adic复杂度，对称版本可能严格小于普通版本；对于线性复杂度，两者在周期序列中相等。在非周期序列中，对称复杂度通常更小，其期望值在指数尺度上明显低于普通版本。这些发现对密码学和序列设计有重要意义，表明对称化可以降低某些复杂度度量，从而影响序列的伪随机性评估。

Abstract: We compare ordinary and symmetric variants of two classical measures of pseudorandomness for binary sequences, the $2$-adic complexity and the linear complexity.
  In the periodic setting, we show that for binary periodic sequences constructed from the binary expansions of non-palindromic primes, the symmetric $2$-adic complexity can be strictly smaller than the ordinary $2$-adic complexity. We also give a direct proof (of the known result) that the linear complexity of a periodic binary sequence is invariant under reversal, and hence coincides with its symmetric version.
  In the aperiodic setting, we provide explicit families of finite binary sequences for which both the $N$th symmetric 2-adic complexity and the $N$th symmetric linear complexity are substantially smaller than their ordinary counterparts. Furthermore, we show that the expected values of the $N$th rational complexity and of the $N$th exponential linear complexity exceed those of their symmetric analogues by at least a term of order of magnitude $N$. Thus, the effect of symmetrization is clearly visible on an exponential scale.
  We also establish lower bounds for the expected values of the symmetric rational complexity, symmetric $2$-adic complexity, symmetric linear complexity, and symmetric exponential linear complexity.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三点相关和（three-point correlations）在解析数论中具有重要意义，特别是在理解算术函数分布方面。先前方法主要基于谱理论或Heath-Brown型分解，存在局限性。本文旨在发展新方法处理这类问题。

Conclusion: 通过圆法与加权短指数和界的新颖结合，成功改进了三点相关和的估计，为处理更一般的算术函数相关性问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: Let $f_1, f_2, f_3$ be $k$-divisor-bounded functions, at least one of which satisfies certain second-moment integral bounds. We show that for any $\varepsilon > 0$ and \[ X^{1/2+100\varepsilon} \ll H \ll X^{1-\varepsilon}, \] we have \[ \sum_{|h|\le H} \left(1-\frac{|h|}{H}\right) \sum_{X \le n \le 2X} f_{1}(n)\, f_{2}(n+h)\, f_{3}(n+2h) = O\!\left(XH^{1-\varepsilon}\right). \] Our approach differs from previous methods based on spectral theory or Heath-Brown-type decompositions, and instead combines the circle method with weighted short exponential-sum bounds. The key input is short exponential-sum estimates obtained from integral moment bounds for $L$-functions.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究广义Markoff方程中具有k-斐波那契数分量的无限路径的结构和分布，探索这些特殊解在Markoff树中的位置和性质。

Method: 1. 对最后两个分量为k-斐波那契数且不在任何Markoff树根部的Markoff m-三元组进行完全分类；2. 证明每个这样的无限路径都包含在特定形式的三元组为起点的分支中；3. 分析这些分支在Markoff树中的分布情况。

Result: 1. 获得了具有k-斐波那契数分量的Markoff m-三元组的完整分类；2. 证明了每个无限路径都包含在形式为(F_k(4r)/3F_k(2r), F_k(ℓ+2r), F_k(ℓ+4r))的三元组为起点的分支中，其中r为奇数，ℓ∈{1,2,...,2r}且3∤k；3. 发现这些分支分布在恰好2r个不同的Markoff树中。

Conclusion: 广义Markoff方程中具有k-斐波那契数分量的无限路径具有特定的结构，它们都包含在由特定参数化三元组生成的分支中，并且这些分支在Markoff树中的分布是有限的（2r个树）。这为理解Markoff方程的特殊解与斐波那契数列之间的关系提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We study infinite paths of Markoff $m$-triples, that is, solutions to the generalised Markoff equation \[ x^2+y^2+z^2=3xyz+m, \] with $m>0$, with at least two $k$-Fibonacci components. First, we obtain a complete classification of Markoff $m$-triples whose last two entries are $k$-Fibonacci numbers and that are not roots of any Markoff trees. We then prove that every such infinite path is contained in a branch, starting at a triple of the form \[ \left(\frac{F_k(4r)}{3F_k(2r)},\,F_k(\ell+2r),\,F_k(\ell+4r)\right), \] where $r$ is an odd integer, $\ell\in\{1,2,\ldots, 2r\}$ and $3\nmid k$. These branches are distributed among exactly $2r$ distinct trees.

</details>


### [64] [Counting points in thin sets: A survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23334)
*Dante Bonolis,Lillian B. Pierce,Katharine Woo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文综述了Serre关于薄集中有界高度点计数问题的研究进展，重点讨论了四类薄集的计数结果、均匀性问题，并特别关注了II型薄集均匀性问题的复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serre在1980年代提出的关于薄集中有界高度点计数问题激发了大量研究，近期取得了一系列突破性进展。本文旨在总结已解决的核心问题，并突出尚未解决的开放性问题，特别关注均匀性问题在II型薄集中的复杂性。

Method: 首先，系统综述了四类薄集（射影/仿射、I型/II型）中有界高度点计数的最新进展。其次，深入探讨了均匀性问题：对于I型薄集，已有独立于多项式系数最大范数‖F‖的均匀上界；对于II型薄集，通过多种策略探索对‖F‖的依赖性，并构造反例说明均匀性问题的复杂性。

Result: I型薄集已建立独立于‖F‖的均匀上界，而II型薄集的均匀性问题尚未解决。通过构造反例表明，II型薄集的均匀性问题比I型更为微妙和复杂，目前缺乏统一的均匀上界。

Conclusion: Serre关于薄集点计数问题的研究取得了显著进展，特别是I型薄集的均匀性问题已基本解决。然而，II型薄集的均匀性问题仍然开放且复杂，需要进一步研究。论文为理解薄集点计数的不同类别及其均匀性特性提供了系统框架。

Abstract: In the 1980's Serre asked how many points of bounded height can lie in a thin set. This has motivated significant research ever since, culminating in a series of recent breakthroughs. It is a good time to take stock of the central questions that have been resolved, and also to highlight remaining open questions. First, we survey recent progress on counting points of bounded height in the four types of thin sets, according to the projective/affine and type I/type II designations. Second, we turn to questions of uniformity. Famously, in the setting of type I thin sets, the best-known upper bound for the number of points of bounded height is independent of the maximum size, say $\|F\|$, of the coefficients of the polynomials that define the thin set; such an upper bound is called uniform. A uniform upper bound in the setting of type II thin sets is not known. For type II thin sets, we explore the dependence on $\|F\|$ via several strategies, and construct counterexamples that suggest the question of uniformity is quite subtle in the setting of type II thin sets.

</details>

*Ralph Furmaniak*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Bohr最后问题的细化版本——解析Lindelöf假设，通过构造Cantor Dirichlet级数等工具，证明了Kahane的半平面反例不满足全纯性，而随机全纯例子几乎必然具有整数斜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Bohr最后问题（1952）的细化版本：对于具有全纯延拓且阶≤1的Dirichlet级数，验证解析Lindelöf假设是否成立，即其Lindelöf阶函数μ是否具有整数斜率的分段线性形式。这一问题与黎曼ζ函数的Lindelöf猜想密切相关。

Method: 1. 将Mellin积分变形到带状边界，将μ_L简化为生成函数在|x|=1上奇点的留数和；2. 构造Cantor Dirichlet级数L(s)=∑ν̂(n)n^{-s}，其中ν为三进制Cantor测度；3. 使用Montgomery-Vaughan方法处理乘积变量(m₁+α)(m₂+α)，利用Vieta恒等式保证频率的相异性；4. 分析Kahane的反例，证明其半平面例子不满足全纯性，而随机全纯例子几乎必然具有整数斜率。

Result: 1. 证明了对于经典L-函数，留数和即为函数方程的对偶，其有界性等价于Lindelöf猜想；2. 对于自相似或随机奇点，该和是Rajchman傅里叶变换；3. 证明了Kahane的半平面反例不满足全纯性，而其随机全纯例子几乎必然具有整数斜率；4. 无条件证明了Cantor Dirichlet级数满足μ_L(1/2)≤1/8；5. 若Cantor加权的Hurwitz二阶矩猜想成立，则可得到μ_L(1/2)=0。

Conclusion: 论文通过构造Cantor Dirichlet级数等创新方法，深化了对解析Lindelöf假设的理解，证明了Kahane反例的局限性，并为研究具有非平凡奇点结构的Dirichlet级数的Lindelöf性质提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: Bohr's last problem (1952) asks whether every ordinary Dirichlet series with nonzero Lindelöf order function $μ$ has $μ'(ω_μ{-}0)\le-1$; a negative answer would imply Lindelöf for $ζ$. Kahane (1989) refuted this with half-plane counterexamples. We study the refinement for series with entire continuation of order $\le 1$: the Analytic Lindelöf Hypothesis that $μ$ is piecewise linear with integer slopes.
  Deforming the Mellin integral to the strip boundary reduces $μ_L$ to a residue sum over singularities of the generating function on $|x|=1$, giving $μ_L(σ)=\max(0,\tfrac12-σ+ρ)$. For classical $L$-functions this sum is the functional-equation dual, and bounding it is Lindelöf; for self-similar or random singularities it is a Rajchman Fourier transform. We show Kahane's half-plane examples fail entirety, his entire random examples have integer slopes a.s., and Lerch-Lindelöf implies ALH.
  Our central construction is the Cantor Dirichlet series $L(s)=\sum\hatν(n)n^{-s}$, with $ν$ the ternary Cantor measure. Its Kaczorowski--Perelli twist spectrum is empty; we prove $μ_L(\tfrac12)\le\tfrac18$ unconditionally via a Montgomery--Vaughan argument on the product variable $(m_1+α)(m_2+α)$, where a Vieta identity guarantees distinct frequencies. A Cantor-weighted Hurwitz second-moment conjecture would give $μ_L(\tfrac12)=0$.

</details>

*Nicole Looper,Jit Wu Yap*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在特征0的函数域上，阿贝尔簇的有理挠点阶数有界，以及满足Zariski稠密轨道的点的Néron-Tate高度下界，从而证明了Lang-Silverman猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究函数域上阿贝尔簇的有理点性质，特别是挠点的阶数界限和高度下界问题，这对于理解函数域上的算术几何有重要意义。

Method: 使用几何方法和高度理论，结合函数域的特殊性质，通过分析曲线的gonality和阿贝尔簇的稳定Faltings高度来建立界限。

Result: 证明了：1) 任何K-有理挠点的阶数仅依赖于阿贝尔簇的维数g和底曲线的gonality；2) 对于前向轨道Zariski稠密的点，其Néron-Tate高度有仅依赖于稳定Faltings高度的下界。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了函数域上阿贝尔簇有理点的统一界限，证明了Lang-Silverman猜想在特征0函数域上的正确性，为函数域算术几何提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Let $k$ be a field of characteristic $0$ and let $K = k(B)$ be the function field of a geometrically irreducible projective curve $B$ over $k$. Let $A/K$ be a $g$-dimensional abelian variety with $\mathrm{Tr}_{K/k}(A) = 0$. We prove that any $K$-rational torsion point $x$ of $A$ has order uniformly bounded in terms of $g$ and the gonality of $B$. We also prove a uniform lower bound on the Néron-Tate height $\widehat{h}_{A,L}(x)$ in terms of the stable Faltings height $h_{\mathrm{Fal}}(A)$ for any $K$-rational point $x$ whose forward orbit is Zariski dense, proving the Lang-Silverman conjecture over function fields of characteristic $0$.

</details>

*Jason Bell,Daniel Smertnig*

<details>

Abstract: We prove that every Mahler series, over a field of characteristic $0$, with multiplicative coefficients is regular in the sense of Allouche and Shallit. We also obtain an explicit characterization of such series. This yields a joint extension of the characterization of rational series with multiplicative coefficients (by Bézivin and Bell--Bruin--Coons) and of multiplicative automatic sequences (by Konieczny--Lemańczyk--Müllner). Both of these results are used in our characterization, so we do not obtain new proofs of these special cases.

</details>

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出认知训练框架，通过交叉熵游戏的课程学习实现语言模型通用能力的自动构建

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在自然假设下，证明认知训练本质上只有一种可能的元目标（除少数超参数外），为相关技能发现提供了原则性方法

Conclusion: 认知训练为通过贪婪课程学习实现通用能力提供了解决方案，前提是具备足够能力的语言模型作为参与者和元采样器以及充足的训练时间

Abstract: Defining a constructive process to build general capabilities for language models in an automatic manner is considered an open problem in artificial intelligence. Towards this, we consider the problem of building a curriculum of tasks that grows a model via relevant skill discovery. We provide a concrete framework for this task, using a family of tasks called cross-entropy games, which we postulate is universal in a suitable sense. We show that if it is possible to grow the curriculum for relevant skill discovery by iterating a greedy optimization algorithm, then, under natural assumptions, there is essentially only one meta-objective possible (up to a few hyperparameters). We call the resulting process cognitive training. We postulate that, given sufficiently capable language models as players and meta-samplers and sufficient training time, cognitive training provides a principled way to relevant skill discovery; and hence to the extent general capabilities are achievable via greedy curriculum learning, cognitive training would be a solution.

</details>


### [69] [A variational geometric framework for multi-objective level set topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22739)
*Jan Oellerich,Takayuki Yamada*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于变分框架的多目标水平集拓扑优化方法，将水平集函数视为虚构材料的广义坐标，通过哈密顿原理推导运动方程，形成阻尼波方程控制优化过程，实现帕累托前沿的自适应探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多目标拓扑优化方法通常基于帕累托前沿的离散近似，缺乏对问题几何结构的深入理解。本文旨在建立变分框架，从几何角度重新解释多目标优化问题，揭示目标空间中解集的结构特性，并实现更有效的帕累托前沿探索。

Method: 1. 将水平集函数视为虚构材料的广义坐标；2. 应用哈密顿原理推导运动方程，得到控制优化过程的阻尼波方程；3. 采用加权和方法组合目标函数；4. 分析系统结构，揭示问题的几何解释；5. 引入权重参数的动态演化，形成水平集函数与权重的耦合动力系统；6. 实现目标空间的自适应探索。

Result: 1. 在适当正则性假设下，平稳解集在目标空间中形成结构化子集，帕累托前沿局部嵌入其中，权重因子作为内在坐标；2. 提出的框架能够稳定、均匀地近似帕累托前沿；3. 方法可扩展到高维目标空间；4. 数值结果验证了方法的有效性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 该变分框架为多目标水平集拓扑优化提供了新的几何视角，通过耦合动力系统实现了帕累托前沿的自适应探索。与传统离散近似方法相比，该方法具有更好的稳定性和可扩展性，能够有效处理高维目标空间问题，为多目标拓扑优化开辟了新途径。

Abstract: This paper proposes a variational framework for multi-objective level set topology optimization. The approach interprets the level set function as a generalized coordinate of a fictitious material and derives its equation of motion from Hamilton's principle, resulting in a damped wave equation governing the optimization process. The objective functionals are combined using a weighted sum formulation. An analysis of the underlying system structure reveals a geometric interpretation of the problem, shifting the perspective beyond conventional approaches based on purely discrete approximations of the Pareto frontier. Under suitable regularity assumptions, the set of stationary solutions forms a structured subset in objective space, in which the Pareto frontier is locally embedded and the weighting factors act as intrinsic coordinates. This perspective motivates the introduction of a dynamic evolution of the weights, leading to a coupled dynamical system for the level set function and the weighting parameters that enables adaptive exploration of the objective landscape. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed framework provides a stable and uniform approximation of the Pareto frontier and scales to higher-dimensional objective spaces.

</details>


### [70] [Equivalence of Finite- and Fixed-time Stability to Asymptotic Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22802)
*Kunal Garg*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于LaSalle类不变原理的有限时间和固定时间收敛新结果，通过非光滑Lyapunov函数放宽可微性要求，并证明可通过尺度变换将渐近稳定系统转化为固定时间稳定系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限时间和固定时间收敛分析需要可微的正定Lyapunov函数，这限制了应用范围。本文旨在放宽这一要求，探索不同收敛速率之间的内在联系，并建立通过变换相互转换的理论基础。

Method: 采用LaSalle类不变原理，提出一阶和二阶非光滑Lyapunov类结果，用于分析有限时间和固定时间收敛。通过尺度变换方法，将全局渐近稳定系统的平衡点转化为固定时间稳定平衡点。

Result: 成功放宽了有限时间和固定时间收敛分析中对可微Lyapunov函数的要求，证明了通过适当尺度变换可将渐近稳定系统转化为固定时间稳定系统，验证了不同收敛速率可通过变换相互关联的假设。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了对各种收敛速率的理解，强化了所有收敛速率可通过适当变换相互关联的假设，为非光滑系统分析提供了新工具，并为控制系统设计提供了理论支持。

Abstract: In this paper, we present new results on finite- and fixed-time convergence for dynamical systems using LaSalle-like invariance principles. In particular, we provide first and second-order non-smooth Lyapunov-like results for finite- and fixed-time convergence, thereby relaxing the requirement of existence a differentiable, positive definite Lyapunov function. Based on these findings, we show that a dynamical system whose equilibrium point is globally asymptotically stable can be modified through scaling so that the resulting dynamical system has a fixed-time stable equilibrium point. The results in this paper expand our understanding of various convergence rates and strengthen the hypothesis that all the convergence rates are interconnected through a suitable transformation.

</details>


### [71] [Quantifying Non-linearity in Topology Optimization with similarity based Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22889)
*Ziliang Wang,Jiahua Wu,Jun Yang,Shintaro Yamasaki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出可视化框架和定量非线性指标，用于分析拓扑优化问题的非线性复杂度，指导参数选择和稳定化策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑优化中，弱非线性问题（如柔度最小化）通常收敛良好，而强非线性问题（如最大应力最小化）需要稳定化策略。现有方法难以直观展示不同拓扑优化问题的非线性复杂度，也难以解释稳定化策略如何影响收敛机制，特别是在设计变量众多的情况下。

Method: 提出可视化框架和定量非线性指标：1）采用多起点固定梯度采样，结合基于相似性的降维方法控制计算成本；2）通过余弦相似度参数化样本，获得目标函数的低维可视化曲面；3）构建无量纲复杂度指标，通过测量可视化曲面与其凸包离散近似之间的差距来量化非线性程度，该指标具有清晰的几何解释。

Result: 大量对比实验表明，所提方法在各种代表性拓扑优化问题上具有适应性和区分性，能够为参数选择提供直观且可量化的指导。

Conclusion: 提出的可视化框架和定量非线性指标能够有效分析拓扑优化问题的非线性复杂度，实现跨任务、参数选择和稳定化策略的定量比较，为拓扑优化实践提供有价值的指导工具。

Abstract: Topology optimization (TO) can be viewed as seeking an optimal solution in the design space of a given TO problem. For weakly non-linear TO problems, e.g., compliance minimization, sensitivity-based methods typically converge well, whereas for strongly non-linear problems, e.g., maximum stress minimization, stabilization strategies such as stabilization terms and projection functions are often required to enhance convergence. Especially in scenarios with massive design variables, it is difficult to intuitively demonstrate the non-linear complexity of different TO problems and to elucidate the mechanisms by which stabilization strategies affect convergence. To address this challenge, we propose a visualization framework and a quantitative non-linearity index for objectives with varying complexity. We employ a multi-start fixed-gradient sampling tailored to similarity-based dimensionality reduction while keeping the computational cost under control. The samples are then parameterized via cosine similarity to obtain a low-dimensional visualization surface of the objective function. Based on this visualization, we construct a dimensionless complexity index with a clear geometric interpretation by measuring the gap between the visualization surface and the discrete approximation of its convex envelope, which enables quantitative comparisons of non-linearity across TO tasks, parameter choices, and stabilization strategies. Extensive comparative experiments show that the proposed approach is both adaptable and discriminative on a variety of representative TO problems, and it provides intuitive and measurable guidance for parameter selection.

</details>


### [72] [Positive Observers Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22924)
*David Ohlin,Anders Rantzer,Emma Tegling*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文证明正线性系统可以使用正Luenberger型观测器稳定，这与先前结论相矛盾。通过将观测器构建为状态单调收敛的上下界来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究认为正线性系统无法使用正Luenberger型观测器稳定，本文旨在推翻这一结论，扩展观测器设计的适用范围。

Method: 构建单调收敛的上下界观测器，分析闭环系统在线性观测器反馈下的性质，将结果扩展到非正系统（通过上界观测器反馈强制动态正性），并扩展到包含随机噪声的情况。

Result: 获得了比先前观测器设计覆盖更大类别的条件，成功将正观测器应用于非正系统，并在随机噪声下给出了期望收敛的条件。

Conclusion: 正线性系统确实可以使用正Luenberger型观测器稳定，这一发现推翻了先前结论，并扩展了观测器设计在正系统和非正系统中的应用范围，包括随机环境下的稳定性保证。

Abstract: The paper shows that positive linear systems can be stabilized using positive Luenberger-type observers, contradicting previous conclusions. This is achieved by structuring the observer as monotonically converging upper and lower bounds on the state. Analysis of the closed-loop properties under linear observer feedback gives conditions that cover a larger class than previous observer designs. The results are applied to nonpositive systems by enforcing positivity of the dynamics using feedback from the upper bound observer. The setting is expanded to include stochastic noise, giving conditions for convergence in expectation using feedback from positive observers.

</details>


### [73] [Structure-Aware Optimization of Decision Diagrams for Health Guidance via Integer Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22996)
*Nanako Shimaoka,Naoyuki Kamiyama,Shinji Hotta,Sayuri Kohmura,Yuta Kurume,Hiroko Suzuki,Akihiro Inomata,Eigo Segawa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该研究针对健康指导决策图的结构感知优化问题，通过整数规划建模，评估其在公共卫生通知策略中的实用性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共卫生部门需要有效的决策系统来确定应向哪些人群建议咨询医疗工作者，并选择合适的通知方式。现有决策图可能缺乏结构优化，影响通知效率和资源分配。

Method: 将决策图优化问题形式化为整数规划模型，通过数值实验评估方法的实际应用效果。

Result: 数值示例验证了整数规划方法在优化决策图结构方面的有效性，展示了该方法在公共卫生通知策略中的实用价值。

Conclusion: 提出的整数规划方法能够有效优化健康指导决策图的结构，为公共卫生部门提供更科学的通知策略决策支持。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a structure-aware optimization problem for decision diagrams used for health guidance. In particular, we focus on decision diagrams that decide to whom public sectors suggest consulting a medical worker. Furthermore, these diagrams decide which notification method should be used for each target person. In this paper, we formulate this problem as an integer program. Then we evaluate its practical usefulness through numerical examples.

</details>


### [74] [Minimizing Material Waste in Additive Manufacturing through Online Reel Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23042)
*Ilayda Celenk,Willem van Jaarsveld,Ivo J. B. F. Adan,Alp Akcay*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究3D打印中在线线轴分配问题，提出结合理论索引策略与深度强化学习的混合方法，显著减少材料浪费


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于线材的3D打印系统在按订单生产环境中的材料浪费问题，系统只能同时处理有限数量的有限容量线轴，组件到达时需要分配到线轴上，容量不足的线轴会被丢弃替换，导致材料浪费

Method: 1) 将问题建模为无限时域平均成本马尔可夫决策过程；2) 分析随机分配策略下的系统分解特性，推导平均浪费的闭式表达式；3) 构建理论基础的索引策略，证明其相对于随机分配的一步策略改进；4) 将索引结构嵌入深度强化学习框架，使用近似策略迭代方法

Result: 1) 随机分配策略下系统可分解为相同单线轴过程，获得平均浪费的闭式表达式；2) 索引策略被证明是随机分配的一步策略改进；3) 深度强化学习方法在模拟和实际场景中实现接近最优性能；4) 强化学习策略显著减少材料浪费，同时保持实时可行性和可解释性

Conclusion: 提出的结合理论索引策略与深度强化学习的混合方法有效解决了3D打印线轴分配问题，在减少材料浪费的同时保持了实时性能和可解释性，为在线装箱问题的实际应用提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: We study a variant of the online bin packing problem that arises in filament-based 3D printing systems operating in make-to-order settings, where only a limited number of filament reels of finite capacity can be handled at once. Components are assigned to reels upon arrival and insufficient reels are discarded to be replaced with new ones, resulting in material waste. To minimize the long-run average discarded filament through an online assignment policy, we formulate this problem as an infinite-horizon average-cost Markov Decision Process and analyze the structure of policies under stochastic, sequential demand. We first show that under a random allocation policy, the system decomposes into a collection of identical single-reel processes, allowing us to derive a closed-form expression for the average waste and enabling a tractable baseline analysis. Building on this decomposition, we construct a theoretically grounded index policy that assigns each reel a score reflecting the marginal cost of assignment and prove that it constitutes a one-step policy improvement over random allocation. We embed the index-based structure within a Deep Reinforcement Learning framework using approximate policy iteration. The resulting method achieves near-optimal performance across a range of simulated and real-world scenarios. Our results demonstrate that Reinforcement Learning policy significantly reduces material waste while maintaining real-time feasibility and interpretability.

</details>


### [75] [Convergence analysis of accelerated algorithms via a mixed-order dynamical system for separable nonsmooth convex optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23046)
*Geng-Hua Li,Hai-Yi Zhao,Xiangkai Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出两种基于混合阶动力系统的时间尺度Tikhonov正则化算法，分别对应隐式和半隐式乘子，解决带线性等式约束的"非光滑+非光滑"复合结构凸优化问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有"非光滑+非光滑"复合结构的线性等式约束凸优化问题，现有算法在处理这类问题时存在局限性。作者希望通过引入混合阶动力系统，结合时间尺度和Tikhonov正则化项，开发新的高效算法，并能收敛到最小范数解。

Method: 基于混合阶动力系统，结合时间尺度和Tikhonov正则化项，针对隐式乘子和半隐式乘子分别提出两种算法：1) 新的原始-对偶联合算法（隐式乘子）；2) 新的分裂算法（半隐式乘子）。联合算法可退化为求解相应不可分线性约束凸优化问题的算法。

Result: 建立了所有提出算法的非遍历收敛性质，证明了算法生成的序列强收敛到最小范数解。通过数值实验验证了所提算法的实际性能。

Conclusion: 成功开发了两种基于混合阶动力系统的新算法，分别适用于隐式和半隐式乘子情况。这些算法不仅具有理论上的收敛保证（强收敛到最小范数解），而且在数值实验中表现出良好的实际性能，为解决"非光滑+非光滑"复合结构的线性约束凸优化问题提供了有效工具。

Abstract: For a linear equality constrained convex optimization problem involving two objective functions with a ``nonsmooth" + ``nonsmooth" composite structure, we study two algorithms derived from a mixed-order dynamical system which incorporates time scales and a Tikhonov regularization term. We observe that different types of multipliers lead to distinct algorithms. For the implicit multiplier and semi-implicit multiplier, we develop a new primal-dual joint algorithm and a new splitting algorithm, respectively. Our proposed joint algorithm can reduce to an algorithm for solving the corresponding non-separable linearly constrained convex optimization problem. Then, we establish the nonergodic convergence properties of all our proposed algorithms. Moreover, we derive that the sequences generated by these algorithms strongly converge to the minimal norm solution. Finally, numerical experiments are conducted to validate the practical performance of the proposed algorithms.

</details>


### [76] [The relief distribution problem with trucks and drones under incomplete demand information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23135)
*Aaron Neugebauer,Alena Otto,Marie Schmidt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究无人机辅助的灾害救援物资配送问题，在需求不确定情况下，通过无人机侦察和卡车配送的协同优化，最小化救援完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害救援行动面临受灾地点需求不确定的挑战，传统救援车辆无法预先知道哪些地点需要物资。无人机能够快速侦察但无法配送物资，需要研究如何有效协同无人机侦察和卡车配送，提高救援效率。

Method: 提出救援物资配送问题(RDP)的形式化模型，研究两种自然策略：1) 无人机先侦察所有地点再派卡车配送；2) 无人机和卡车协同工作。通过竞争比分析（与完全信息最优解比较）和无人机影响分析（与无无人机情况比较）评估策略性能。

Result: 通过理论分析和计算实验，量化了两种策略在竞争比和无人机影响方面的性能，揭示了无人机侦察与卡车配送之间的操作权衡，为灾害响应中无人机的有效部署提供了理论依据。

Conclusion: 无人机在灾害救援中能够显著提高效率，但需要合理的协同策略。研究为无人机辅助的救援行动提供了理论框架和实用指导，有助于优化灾害响应中的资源分配和调度决策。

Abstract: Disaster relief operations often take place under uncertainty regarding the extent of damage across locations. In this paper, we study the delivery of relief aid in the aftermath of disasters when delivery vehicles are assisted by surveillance drones and the demand for relief supplies is initially unknown. We introduce a stylized problem that arises in many emergency supply delivery settings -- the relief distribution problem (RDP). In RDP, emergency vehicles, referred to as trucks, must distribute relief supplies on a network, starting from the depot to potential delivery locations, whose demand is initially unknown. The trucks are assisted by surveillance drones, which cannot deliver relief supplies, but scout delivery locations to see whether relief supplies are needed or not. The objective is to visit all location by any vehicle, deliver supplies to all damaged ones, and minimizing the completion time of the relief operation. We study two natural policies for the online problem RDP which we evaluate in two ways: the competitive ratio quantifies the performance in comparison to an optimal solution obtained under full information on damages, the drone-impact is the ratio of the algorithm's performance to the best outcome achievable without drones. Through theoretical analysis and computational experiments, we characterize the operational trade-offs between these policies and derive insights for the effective deployment of drones in disaster response.

</details>


### [77] [Mean Field Games for Renewable Energy Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23156)
*Luciano Campi,Zhuoshu Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一个均值场博弈框架来建模竞争性电力市场中可再生能源生产的演化，分析生产者在不确定性下的最优控制，并扩展到包含社会规划者的Stackelberg博弈，通过深度学习数值方法研究政策干预对产能动态的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究竞争性电力市场中可再生能源生产者的行为演化，这些生产者在现货价格下互动，同时面临生产、安装、产能调整成本和发电不确定性。需要理解市场均衡动态以及政策干预如何影响产能发展。

Method: 首先将市场建模为N玩家随机微分博弈，分析其均值场博弈极限。通过随机最大值原理推导前向-后向随机微分方程来表征代表性生产者的最优控制，确定均衡现货价格。证明FBSDE解的存在唯一性以及MFG均衡的唯一性。然后扩展到Stackelberg均值场博弈以纳入社会规划者角色，规划者的优化问题导致扩展的Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman系统。最后实现基于深度学习的数值方案来近似均衡。

Result: 建立了FBSDE解的存在唯一性，证明了MFG存在唯一均衡。对于Stackelberg博弈，证明了HJB系统粘性解的存在唯一性。数值模拟表明最优补贴设计取决于当前市场条件，可以有效缓解产能短缺和产能过剩问题。

Conclusion: 提出的均值场博弈框架能够有效建模竞争性电力市场中可再生能源的演化动态。通过纳入社会规划者的Stackelberg扩展，可以分析政策干预对市场均衡的影响。研究结果表明，最优补贴设计需要根据市场条件进行调整，以实现产能的合理发展，避免短缺或过剩。

Abstract: We propose a mean field game (MFG) framework to model the evolution of renewable energy production in competitive electricity markets. Producers interact through the spot price while optimising their profits under production, installation, and capacity adjustment costs, as well as the generation uncertainty. We first formulate the market as an $N$-player stochastic differential game and analyse its mean field game limit as $N\to\infty$. We characterise the representative producer's optimal control via forward-backward stochastic differential equations (FBSDEs) derived from the stochastic maximum principle and determine the corresponding equilibrium spot price. We establish existence and uniqueness of solutions to the FBSDEs and prove that the MFG admits a unique equilibrium. We then extend the model to a Stackelberg mean field game to incorporate the role of a social planner. The planner's optimisation problem leads to an extended Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) system, for which we prove existence and uniqueness of viscosity solutions. Finally, we implement a deep learning-based numerical scheme to approximate the equilibrium and investigate the impact of policy interventions on capacity dynamics. Our results highlight how optimal subsidy design depends on prevailing market conditions and can mitigate both capacity shortages and overproduction.

</details>


### [78] [Superlinear convergence in nonsmooth optimization via higher-order cutting-plane models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23236)
*Bennet Gebken,Michael Ulbrich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出高阶割平面模型用于非光滑优化，实现超线性收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光滑优化中，使用高阶模型可以获得超线性收敛，但在非光滑优化中，传统的割平面模型（一阶展开的最大值）只能实现线性收敛。本文旨在探索在非光滑优化中是否也能通过高阶割平面模型实现超线性收敛。

Method: 首先为lower-C^2函数形式化定义高阶割平面模型并推导误差估计；然后基于这些模型构建信赖域束方法；最后通过数值实验验证超线性收敛性。

Result: 证明了高阶割平面模型可用于非光滑优化，并实现了局部超线性收敛（对于严重步），对于某些有限最大值型函数实现了整体超线性收敛。数值实验验证了超线性收敛性。

Conclusion: 与光滑优化类似，在非光滑优化中使用高阶割平面模型可以实现超线性收敛，这扩展了非光滑优化的收敛理论并提供了更高效的算法框架。

Abstract: A cutting-plane model for a nonsmooth function is the maximum of several first-order expansions centered at different points. Using such a model in a bundle method leads to linear convergence (of serious steps) to a minimum. In smooth optimization, superlinear convergence can be achieved by using higher-order models. We show that the same is true for the nonsmooth case, i.e., we show that cutting-plane models involving higher-order expansions can be used to achieve superlinear convergence in nonsmooth optimization. We first formally define higher-order cutting-plane models for lower-$C^2$ functions and derive an error estimate. Afterwards, we construct a trust-region bundle method based on these models that achieves local superlinear convergence of serious steps, and overall superlinear convergence for certain finite max-type functions. Finally, we verify the superlinear convergence in numerical experiments.

</details>


### [79] [Enclosing minima in nonsmooth optimization via trust regions of higher-order cutting-plane models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23261)
*Bennet Gebken,Michael Ulbrich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种全局收敛的信任域束方法，用于最小化低C²函数，使用高阶切平面模型。在目标函数最小值附近的特定增长假设下，该方法能计算包含最小值的无限递减信任域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要开发一种能够全局收敛的优化方法，用于处理低C²函数（lower-C² functions），这类函数在非光滑优化中具有重要应用。传统方法可能无法保证全局收敛性或需要更强的函数性质假设。

Method: 提出基于信任域束方法（trust-region bundle method），使用高阶切平面模型来近似目标函数。该方法在目标函数最小值附近满足特定增长条件时，能够生成一系列递减的信任域，这些信任域都包含最小值。

Result: 证明了该方法在特定增长假设下具有全局收敛性，能够计算无限递减的信任域序列，这些信任域都包含最小值。对于具有尖锐增长或二次增长的有限最大值型函数，这些增长假设是满足的。

Conclusion: 该方法为低C²函数的优化提供了全局收敛的解决方案，并且可以通过生成的信任域来初始化局部超线性收敛方法，数值实验验证了这种组合方法的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a globally convergent trust-region bundle method for minimizing lower-$C^2$ functions using higher-order cutting-plane models. Under certain growth assumptions on the objective around its minimum, the method is able to compute infinitely many trust regions of decreasing size that contain the minimum. We show that these growth assumptions are satisfied for certain finite max-type functions with sharp or quadratic growth. Enclosing the minimum in this way can be used to initialize local superlinearly convergent methods, which we demonstrate in numerical experiments.

</details>


### [80] [Exponential Turnpike Theorems for Nonlinear Deterministic Meanfield Optimal Control Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23303)
*Benoît Bonnet-Weill,Giovanni Colombo,Denis Shishmintsev,Emmanuel Trélat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文建立了非线性确定性平均场最优控制问题的指数转向定理，同时在拉格朗日和欧拉框架下进行分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究平均场最优控制问题的长期动态行为，特别是系统在长时间范围内如何趋近于稳态解，这对于理解大规模交互系统的渐近性质具有重要意义。

Method: 采用双重框架分析：1) 拉格朗日框架：将问题提升到随机变量的希尔伯特空间，结合一阶最优性条件、提升哈密顿量的二阶展开和算子Riccati对角化论证；2) 欧拉框架：推导静态约束问题的内在KKT条件，分析欧拉二阶假设如何分解为水平部分（通过酉共轭转移到提升空间）和垂直部分（简化为乘法算子上的一致点可稳性和可检测性条件）。

Result: 证明了拉格朗日框架下的指数转向定理，以及在Wasserstein空间中关于最优Pontryagin三元组的指数转向定理，建立了Wasserstein Hessian与其拉格朗日提升之间的明确联系。

Conclusion: 该研究成功建立了非线性确定性平均场最优控制问题的指数转向定理，为理解这类系统在长时间尺度上的动态行为提供了理论框架，同时阐明了占用测度、局部欧拉极小化子和控制约束在结果中的作用。

Abstract: In this article, we establish exponential turnpike theorems for a class of nonlinear deterministic meanfield optimal control problems. We carry out our analysis simultaneously in the so-called Lagrangian and Eulerian frameworks. In the Lagrangian setting, the problem is lifted to a Hilbert space of random variables, and we prove an exponential turnpike theorem by combining first-order optimality conditions, a second-order expansion of the lifted Hamiltonian, and an operator Riccati diagonalization argument. In the Eulerian setting, we derive intrinsic KKT conditions for the static constrained problem, and show how the Eulerian second-order hypotheses split into a horizontal part, transferred by unitary conjugation to the lifted space, and a vertical part which reduces to uniform pointwise stabilizability and detectability conditions on multiplication operators. This yields an exponential turnpike theorem in the Wasserstein space for optimal Pontryagin triples. Along the way, we %provide explicit the link between Wasserstein Hessians and their Lagrangian lifts, and provide several remarks clarifying the role of occupation measures, local Eulerian minimizers, and control constraints in our results.

</details>


### [81] [Piecewise M-Stationarity and Related Algorithms for Mathematical Programs with Complementarity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23389)
*Kexin Wang,Lorenz T. Biegler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了带互补约束的数学规划（MPCC）的B-平稳性及算法收敛性，特别关注双活跃互补约束情况。提出了分段M-平稳性概念，证明了其在MPCC-ACQ下与B-平稳性的等价性，并分析了NCP基边界方法的收敛性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPCC问题在优化中具有重要应用，但B-平稳性验证通常计算成本高。双活跃互补约束情况下的收敛行为分析不足，需要更有效的平稳性概念和验证方法，同时避免非强平稳解附近的乘子无界问题。

Method: 1. 提出分段M-平稳性新概念；2. 证明在MPCC-ACQ条件下与B-平稳性的等价性；3. 分析NCP基边界方法的收敛性质，无需MPCC-LICQ条件；4. 结合分段M-平稳性概念降低B-平稳性验证成本；5. 讨论非强平稳解的收敛问题。

Result: 1. 分段M-平稳性与B-平稳性在MPCC-ACQ下等价；2. NCP基边界方法在无MPCC-LICQ条件下仍具有良好收敛性；3. 结合分段M-平稳性可显著降低B-平稳性验证成本；4. NCP基互补重构在避免非强平稳解附近乘子无界方面具有优势。

Conclusion: 分段M-平稳性为MPCC问题提供了有效的B-平稳性替代验证方法，NCP基边界方法在宽松条件下仍能收敛，且NCP基重构有助于处理非强平稳解问题，为MPCC算法设计和分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: This study explores B-stationarity of mathematical programs with complementarity constraints (MPCCs) and convergence behavior of MPCC algorithms. Special attention is given to the cases with biactive complementarity constraints. First, we propose the concept of piecewise M-stationarity and prove its equivalence to B-stationarity under MPCC-ACQ. Then, we investigate convergence properties of the NCP-based bounding methods we proposed in [31], without requiring MPCC-LICQ; an interpretation of the algorithm's behavior together with the concept of piecewise M-stationarity leads to a cost reduction in B-stationarity verification. In addition, practical issues related to convergence to non-strongly stationary solutions are discussed, which shows that the NCP-based complementarity reformulations have an advantage in avoiding unbounded multipliers near these solutions.

</details>


### [82] [Universal and Parameter-free Gradient Sliding for Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23492)
*Yan Wu,Yuyuan Ouyang,Zhe Zhang,Qi Luo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出参数无关的通用梯度滑动算法（PFUGS），用于求解凸复合优化问题，无需预先知道问题常数，在特定条件下达到最优复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的梯度滑动算法通常需要预先知道问题的常数参数（如Hölder指数ν、常数M_ν和Lipschitz常数L），这在实践中难以获得。作者旨在开发一个参数无关的算法，无需任何先验知识，同时保持最优的复杂度保证。

Method: 提出参数无关的通用梯度滑动算法（PFUGS），用于求解凸复合优化问题min_{x∈X} {f(x)+g(x)}。算法不依赖于问题常数ν、M_ν和L的先验知识，通过自适应机制调整参数。当f具有(M_ν,ν)-Hölder连续（次）梯度，g具有L-Lipschitz连续（次）梯度时，算法能自动适应这些未知常数。

Result: PFUGS算法在至多O((M_ν/ε)^{2/(1+3ν)})次f的（次）梯度评估和O((L/ε)^{1/2})次g的（次）梯度评估内计算近似解。这是第一个针对涉及两个具有不同问题常数的函数的参数无关梯度滑动算法。

Conclusion: PFUGS算法是参数无关的，无需任何问题常数的先验知识，同时在凸复合优化问题中实现了最优的复杂度保证。该算法填补了参数无关梯度滑动方法的空白，特别适用于实际问题中常数未知的情况。

Abstract: We propose a parameter-free universal gradient sliding (PFUGS) algorithm for computing an approximation solution to the convex composite optimization problem $\min_{x\in X} \{f(x) + g(x)\}$. When $f$ and $g$ have $(M_ν,ν)$-Hölder and $L$-Lipschitz continuous (sub)gradients respectively, our proposed PFUGS method computes an approximate solution within at most $\mathcal{O}((M_ν/\varepsilon)^{{2}/{(1+3ν)}})$ and $\mathcal{O}((L/\varepsilon)^{1/2})$ evaluations of (sub)gradients of $f$ and $g$ respectively. Moreover, the PFUGS algorithm is parameter-free and does not require any prior knowledge on problem constants $ν$, $M_ν$, and $L$. To the best of knowledge, for problems involving two functions with different sets of problem constants, PFUGS is the first gradient sliding algorithm that is parameter-free.

</details>

### [83] [Hook-decomposable modules and their resolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23008)
*Isabella Mastroianni,Marco Guerra,Ulderico Fugacci,Emanuela De Negri*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该论文比较了双参数持久性模的几类：单参数模的γ-积、钩可分解模、具有Smith型结构定理的模以及射影维数为1的模。确定了这些类之间的所有逻辑蕴含关系，并提供了反例证明逆命题不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解双参数持久性模的结构复杂性，特别是与单参数情况相比。通过比较不同类型的双参数模，揭示双参数持久性模的丰富结构层次，明确哪些结构定理在双参数情况下仍然成立，哪些失效。

Method: 方法包括：1) 形式化定义和比较几类双参数持久性模；2) 分析这些类之间的逻辑蕴含关系；3) 构造具体的反例来证明逆命题不成立；4) 特别关注γ-积（即钩可分解模）在双参数模中的位置。

Result: 主要结果：1) 确定了所有逻辑蕴含关系；2) 提供了适当的反例证明逆命题不成立；3) 发现γ-积（钩可分解模）构成双参数模的一个非常小的子类；4) 证明只有γ-积模仍然满足结构定理，突显了双参数情况相比单参数情况的更丰富结构复杂性。

Conclusion: 结论：双参数持久性模具有比单参数情况更丰富的结构复杂性。γ-积（钩可分解模）是双参数模中一个非常小的子类，恰好是结构定理仍然成立的那部分。这明确展示了从单参数到双参数时结构复杂性的显著增加。

Abstract: We compare several classes of biparameter persistence modules: $γ$-products of monoparameter modules, hook-decomposable modules, modules admitting a Smith-type structure theorem, and modules of projective dimension 1. We determine all logical implications among these classes, providing explicit counterexamples showing that the converses fail when appropriate. In particular, $γ$-products (i.e., hook-decomposable modules) form a very small subclass of biparameter modules, precisely the ones for which a structure theorem still holds, thus making explicit the richer structural complexity of the biparameter setting compared to the monoparameter one.

</details>


### [84] [On the equivalence of Brantner's and Chu--Haugseng's approaches to enriched $\infty$-operads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23019)
*Kensuke Arakawa*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 证明了两种单色富集∞-算符模型（Brantner模型与Chu-Haugseng模型）的等价性

Motivation: 建立不同框架下富集∞-算符理论的一致性，确保在不同模型中构造和结果的兼容性

Method: 通过证明两种对称序列单态∞-范畴模型及其复合积的等价性，推导出富集∞-算符模型的等价性

Result: 成功建立了Brantner模型与Chu-Haugseng模型的等价性，从而证明了两种框架下代数概念和Koszul对偶等构造的等价性

Conclusion: 两种富集∞-算符模型在数学上是等价的，这为∞-算符理论提供了统一的框架，确保了不同方法下结果的可移植性

Abstract: We prove that two models of (monochromatic) enriched $\infty$-operads, due to Brantner and Chu--Haugseng, are equivalent. We show this as a consequence of the equivalence of two models of monoidal $\infty$-categories of symmetric sequences and the composition product, due to Brantner and Haugseng. As a consequence, constructions and results formulated in either framework, such as notions of algebra and Koszul duality, are also shown to be equivalent.

</details>


### [85] [Infinite families of very exotic spheres with free $S^1$- and $S^3$-actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23241)
*Tilman Bauer,J. D. Quigley*

TL;DR: 使用拓扑模形式检测具有非平凡2-和3-局部Kervaire-Milnor不变量的非常异球面上无限族的光滑自由S¹-和S³-作用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非常异球面（不界定可平行化流形）上的光滑自由群作用。已知对于每个固定素数p，仅在有限多个具有非平凡p-局部Kervaire-Milnor不变量的非常异球面上存在光滑自由S¹-和S³-作用，且维度小于约p³。本文旨在发现无限族非常异球面上的此类作用。

Method: 使用拓扑模形式（topological modular forms）作为检测工具，研究具有非平凡2-和3-局部Kervaire-Milnor不变量的非常异球面。该方法超越了传统技术，能够检测无限族上的光滑自由S¹-和S³-作用。

Result: 成功检测到具有非平凡2-和3-局部Kervaire-Milnor不变量的非常异球面无限族上存在光滑自由S¹-和S³-作用。这突破了先前已知结果仅限于有限个非常异球面的限制。

Conclusion: 拓扑模形式是检测非常异球面上光滑自由群作用的强大工具，能够揭示无限族上的作用，扩展了对异球面几何结构的理解，特别是在高维情况下。

Abstract: There are two kinds of exotic spheres: bp spheres, which bound parallelizable manifolds, and non-bp spheres, or very exotic spheres, which do not. In the 1960s, W.-C. Hsiang showed that in each dimension where bp spheres exist, there is at least one which admits infinitely many inequivalent smooth free $S^1$-actions, and in each dimension congruent to $3$ modulo $4$, there is at least one bp sphere which admits infinitely many inequivalent smooth free $S^3$-actions. On the other hand, for each fixed prime $p$, smooth free $S^1$- and $S^3$- actions are only known to exist on finitely many very exotic spheres with nontrivial $p$-local Kervaire--Milnor invariant, all in dimension less than approximately $p^3$. In this paper, we use topological modular forms to detect smooth free $S^1$- and $S^3$-actions on infinite families of very exotic spheres with nontrivial $2$- and $3$-local Kervaire--Milnor invariants.

</details>


### [86] [A hypergraph model for the cyclic BV operad and its applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23368)

Abstract: A dg cyclic operad $BVHgraphs$ of hypergraphs is introduced which comes equipped with an explicit quasi-isomorphism $BV\rightarrow BVHgraphs$ from the { cyclic} operad $BV$ of Batalin-Vilkovisky algebras. A proof that the cohomology of $BVHgraphs$ equals $BV$ occupies most of this paper.
  We use this model to construct an explicit quasi-isomorphism $Chains(FFM_2) \rightarrow BVHgraphs$ from the chain operad of the cyclic operad $FFM_2$ of the compactified moduli spaces of genus zero curves with marked framed points to the dg cyclic operad $BVHgraphs$ which, combined with the main result mentioned above, gives a new proof of the cyclic formality of $FFM_2$.

</details>


### [87] [Function-Rips complexes in persistent homotopy theory: Local stability and Latschev theorems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23460)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Latschev定理为度量空间M与附近空间N的Vietoris-Rips复形之间的同伦类型一致性提供了充分条件。然而，在持久同调领域，需要研究子水平集过滤的持久同伦类型与函数Rips复形之间的对应关系，特别是解决从有限点样本估计子水平集持久同调这一长期存在的问题。

Method: 作者扩展了Latschev定理到持久版本，通过建立充分条件，使得对任意(M,f)和δ>0，其子水平集过滤的持久同伦类型与任何附近对(N,g)的函数Rips复形R^δ(N^•)的持久同伦类型交错。该方法结合了度量几何、拓扑数据分析中的持久同调理论以及函数Rips复形构造。

Result: 证明了Latschev定理的持久版本，为子水平集过滤的持久同伦类型与函数Rips复形的持久同伦类型之间的交错提供了充分条件。该结果特别解决了从有限点样本估计子水平集持久同调的长久问题，为拓扑数据分析提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 该工作成功地将Latschev定理扩展到持久同调框架，建立了子水平集过滤与函数Rips复形之间持久同伦类型的对应关系，为从有限点样本进行拓扑数据分析提供了理论保证，解决了该领域的一个重要理论问题。

Abstract: Latschev's theorem provides sufficient conditions on a metric space $M$ and $δ> 0$ for the homotopy type of $M$ to agree with that of the Vietoris-Rips complex $\mathcal{R}^δ(N)$ of any nearby space $N$ in the Gromov-Hausdorff distance. We prove a persistent version of this theorem, providing sufficient conditions on a pair $(M, f \colon M \to \mathbb{R}^N)$ and $δ> 0$ for the persistent homotopy type of the sublevel set filtration of $(M,f)$ to be interleaved with that of the function-Rips complex $\mathcal{R}^δ(N^{\bullet})$ of any nearby pair $(N,g)$. In particular, our result answers a longstanding question on the related topic of estimating sublevel set persistent homology from finite point samples.

</details>


### [88] [Interleaving distances from height-difference functions on posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20269)
*Toshitaka Aoki*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于高度差函数ρ的广义交错距离d_ρ，用于任意偏序集上的函子范畴，将经典交错距离推广到更一般的偏序结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典交错距离依赖于平移结构，但在一般偏序集上平移结构稀缺，限制了该框架的应用范围。需要一种更通用的交错型距离定义方法。

Method: 引入高度差函数ρ，将其关联到Fun(P,C)上的ℝ≥0-索引伴随自函子族，作为广义平移。基于此在纯范畴论框架下定义高度交错距离d_ρ。

Result: 1) 在P=ℝ^d且ρ=ρ_diag时，d_ρ退化为经典多参数持久模交错距离；2) 一般情况下d_ρ不满足三角不等式，但在CIP条件下有加性缺陷的三角不等式；3) 建立了关于ρ扰动的稳定性；4) 研究了侵蚀型构造的类似物。

Conclusion: 提出的高度交错距离为任意偏序集上的函子范畴提供了统一的交错距离框架，扩展了经典交错距离的应用范围，同时保持了关键的理论性质。

Abstract: Interleaving distances provide a fundamental tool for comparing persistence modules and have been widely used in topological data analysis. Their definitions are typically based on translation structures (shift operations) on the indexing poset, but on general posets such structures can be scarce, making this framework restrictive. In this paper, we introduce a new interleaving-type distance for functor categories over arbitrary posets, induced by a height-difference function $ρ$. The key idea is to associate to $ρ$ an $\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}$-indexed family of adjoint endofunctors on $\mathrm{Fun}(P,\mathcal{C})$, which play the role of generalized translations and allow us to formulate interleavings in a purely categorical manner and define the distance $d_ρ$, called the height-interleaving distance. In particular, any height function (i.e., a real-valued order-preserving map) canonically induces such a height-difference function, so our framework remains useful on finite posets. Moreover, when $P=\mathbb{R}^d$ and $ρ=ρ_{\mathrm{diag}}$, the resulting distance coincides with the classical interleaving distance for multiparameter persistence modules. However, in general, $d_ρ$ need not satisfy the triangle inequality. Under suitable hypotheses (e.g. CIP for $(P,ρ)$; this condition is automatic when $P$ is a tree poset), we prove a triangle inequality up to an additive defect bounded by a constant $c(ρ)$; in particular, when $c(ρ)=0$ this yields an extended pseudo-distance. We also establish a stability property with respect to perturbations of height-difference functions: small changes in $ρ$ induce small changes in the associated height-interleaving distance. Finally, we study an analogue of the erosion-type constructions from classical interleavings within our framework.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用有效部分子平均场理论进行解析分析，同时使用变分蒙特卡洛方法进行数值验证，比较U(1)和SU(m)陈-西蒙斯场描述Laughlin序的不同情况

Result: 在空穴掺杂ν=1/3费米子分数陈绝缘体的实验相关场景中，发现当能量最低的激发是基本1/3电荷任意子时，会形成半整数中心荷的任意子超导态，无需这些任意子配对成2/3电荷复合体

Conclusion: 能带贝里曲率与有效任意子色散的相互作用对实现何种任意子相具有决定性影响，为实验观测的任意子超导提供了新的理论机制，突破了传统任意子超导构造中需要任意子配对的假设

Abstract: We study phases of itinerant anyons when hole-doping Laughlin-like states in fractional Chern insulators (FCIs). In light of the recent observation of time-reversal-broken superconductivity near FCIs in van der Waals materials, a theoretical understanding of doped fractional quantum Hall states on a lattice has been developed by Shi and Senthil [Phys. Rev. X 15, 031069], reviving old ideas about "anyon superconductivity". We test these ideas analytically within an effective parton mean-field theory and numerically with variational Monte Carlo, pointing out that the predicted state depends on whether the Laughlin order at $ν=1/m$ is described by a U(1), or an SU(m) Chern-Simons field, the latter implying a symmetry between the m parton species. Our results demonstrate that the interplay between band Berry curvature and effective anyon dispersion has crucial implications for which anyonic phase is realized. In the experimentally relevant scenario of hole-doping the $ν=1/3$ fermionic FCI, our results uncover a mechanism for the formation of an anyon superconducting state of half-integer central charge in the case when the energetically cheapest excitations are the fundamental 1/3 charge anyons, bypassing the need for these anyons to pair into charge-2/3 composites, which has generally been assumed in similar anyon superconductivity constructions.

</details>


### [90] [Discovery of BKT correlations in the quantum kagome compound Cs$_2$Cu$_3$SnF$_{12}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22481)
*M. S. Grbić,I. Jakovac,I. Kupčić,H. Tanaka,M. Horvatić*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过铜核四极共振研究kagome化合物Cs₂Cu₃SnF₁₂，发现其具有负矢量手性的自旋结构、无能隙基态，并在宽温区（约130K）内表现出二维BKT型关联和短程手性序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究kagome化合物Cs₂Cu₃SnF₁₂的微观性质，特别是其量子自旋液体行为和可能的BKT相变，以理解这种量子kagome系统的独特物理特性。

Method: 使用⁶³,⁶⁵Cu核四极共振技术，分析奈尔温度以下的局域超精细场以确定自旋结构，测量自旋-晶格弛豫率T₁⁻¹来探测基态能隙和关联行为，并观察NQR线宽变化以研究短程手性序。

Result: 1. 自旋结构符合P2₁/n对称性，具有负矢量手性；2. 基态无能隙；3. 在零磁场下约130K的宽温区内存在二维BKT型关联；4. NQR线宽增加与中子散射发现的短程手性序一致；5. 确认Cs₂Cu₃SnF₁₂为具有BKT行为的独特量子kagome系统。

Conclusion: Cs₂Cu₃SnF₁₂是一个表现出二维Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless行为的独特量子kagome系统，在宽温区内存在无能隙基态、BKT型关联和短程手性序，为研究量子自旋液体和拓扑相变提供了重要平台。

Abstract: We investigate the microscopic properties of the kagome compound Cs$_2$Cu$_3$SnF$_{12}$ using $^{63,65}$Cu nuclear quadrupolar resonance (NQR). Analysis of the local hyperfine fields below the Néel temperature $T_N = 20$ K indicates a spin structure consistent with $P2_1/n$ symmetry of negative vector chirality. Measurements of the spin-lattice relaxation rate $T_1 ^{-1}$ reveal signatures of a gapless ground state and two-dimensional Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT)-type correlations above $T_N$, extending over a broad temperature range of approximately 130 K in zero magnetic field. Within the same temperature range, the observed increase in the NQR linewidth is consistent with short-range chiral order recently identified by neutron scattering. Our results establish Cs$_2$Cu$_3$SnF$_{12}$ as a unique quantum kagome system exhibiting BKT behavior.

</details>


### [91] [Dimensionality-Dependent Exciton Dispersion in a Single-Band Mott Insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22695)
*Zhibin Su,Junjian Mi,Shaohua Yan,Jiade Li,Siwei Xue,Zhiyu Tao,Enling Wang,Xiongfei Shi,Hechang Lei,Zhuan Xu,Jiandong Guo,Xuetao Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过高分辨率电子能量损失谱直接观测到单带Mott绝缘体Nb3Cl8中维度依赖的激子色散，发现高温相中激子具有准二维无质量线性色散，低温相中激子分裂为两个三维抛物线色散带，这一变化源于相变过程中层间耦合增强导致的维度突变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子能带结构对研究激子动力学至关重要，理论上电子-空穴对之间的交换散射量子效应会显著调制激子色散。然而，维度如何影响激子行为的直接实验观测仍然缺乏。

Method: 使用高分辨率电子能量损失光谱（HREELS）技术，在单带Mott绝缘体Nb3Cl8中直接测量激子色散。通过对比高温相和低温相的激子色散行为，分析维度变化对激子动力学的影响。

Result: 在高温相中，Nb3Cl8的激子具有异常大的结合能，表现出清晰的准二维无质量线性色散。在低温相中，激子分裂为两个能带，均显示三维抛物线色散。这些激子色散的显著变化源于相变过程中层间耦合大幅增强驱动的维度突变。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了激子行为如何随维度演化的清晰典型范例，表明维度突变（从准二维到三维）会显著改变激子色散特性，为理解维度对激子动力学的影响提供了直接实验证据。

Abstract: Excitonic band structure is critical for investigating exciton dynamics. Theoretically, quantum effects from exchange scattering between electron-hole pairs significantly modulate exciton dispersion. Here, we report the direct observation of dimensionality-dependent exciton dispersion in a single-band Mott insulator Nb3Cl8 through high-resolution electron energy loss spectroscopy. In the high-temperature phase, the exciton in Nb3Cl8 hosts an exceptionally large binding energy, and exhibits clear quasi-two-dimensional massless linear dispersion. In contrast, in the low-temperature phase, the exciton splits into two bands, both displaying three-dimensional parabolic dispersion. These dramatic changes in the exciton dispersion stem from the dimensional mutation driven by a substantial enhancement of interlayer coupling across the phase transition. This Letter provides a clear and typical example of how exciton behavior evolves with dimensionality.

</details>


### [92] [Non-Hermitian skin effect in periodic, random, and quasiperiodic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22919)
*F. Iwase*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究比较了周期、随机和准周期（斐波那契）结构在非厄米趋肤效应（NHSE）下的行为差异，发现准周期系统能有效抑制边界积累同时保持拓扑间隙清晰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米趋肤效应（NHSE）使体态向边界聚集，改变了体边对应关系，使拓扑边缘模的识别变得复杂。虽然已知破坏平移对称性会影响这一行为，但不同结构类别之间的系统比较仍然有限。

Method: 使用一维非厄米量子行走模型，在拓扑非平凡区域匹配局部散射参数，研究周期、随机和准周期（斐波那契）系统。通过分析波函数来理解空间结构在NHSE存在下的作用。

Result: 周期系统表现出显著的体态边界积累；随机系统通过安德森局域化抑制这种积累，但拓扑间隙被局域化的带隙态部分填充；斐波那契准周期系统在抑制大规模边界积累的同时保持了明确的拓扑间隙。

Conclusion: 准周期系统的分层结构使体态在多个长度尺度上碎片化，从而缓解了NHSE。确定性准周期性被确定为控制非厄米趋肤动力学和分离拓扑边界模的独特结构机制。

Abstract: The non-Hermitian skin effect (NHSE), which drives bulk states toward system boundaries, modifies bulk-boundary correspondence and complicates the identification of topological edge modes. Although breaking translational symmetry is known to influence this behavior, a systematic comparison of different structural classes remains limited. Here we investigate periodic, random, and quasiperiodic (Fibonacci) systems using a one-dimensional non-Hermitian quantum walk model. By matching the local scattering parameters in a topologically nontrivial regime, we isolate the role of spatial structure in the presence of the NHSE. We find that periodic systems exhibit pronounced boundary accumulation of bulk states. Random systems suppress this accumulation through Anderson localization, but the topological gap becomes partially filled with localized in-gap states. In contrast, the Fibonacci quasiperiodic system suppresses large-scale boundary accumulation while maintaining a well-defined topological gap. Analysis of the wave functions suggests that the hierarchical quasiperiodic structure fragments bulk states across multiple length scales, thereby mitigating the NHSE. These results identify deterministic quasiperiodicity as a distinct structural regime for controlling non-Hermitian skin dynamics and isolating topological boundary modes.

</details>


### [93] [Synergistic chemical and optical switching of chiral symmetry breaking in 1\textit{T}-TaS$_2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22921)
*Qingzheng Qiu,Mengxian Zhao,Roman Mankowsky,Henrik Till Lemke,Serhane Zerdane,Mathias Sander,Zihao Tao,Qizhi Li,Xiquan Zheng,Shilong Zhang,Qian Xiao,Xinyi Jiang,Yang Yang,Sheng Meng,Yingying Peng*

Motivation: 光学控制对称性破缺量子相是量子材料研究的重要目标，但单畴基态的稳定性往往阻碍确定性开关。1T-TaS₂中的手性电荷密度波为此提供了天然平台，但原始材料被锁定在单一手性中。

Method: 结合化学掺杂（Ti替代）和飞秒光学激发。Ti掺杂稳定了共存手性畴的基态，为光学操控创造了可调能量景观。飞秒光激发诱导从主导手性畴到少数手性畴的不对称和各向异性开关。

Result: 实现了手性CDW态的高效非热开关，开关时间尺度与相干声子振荡相当（~2 THz）。观察到面内畴生长和向非手性构型的重新分布，揭示了通过瞬态畴壁态的声子介导路径。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了通过化学和超快光学调谐相结合来工程化和控制手性序参数的广泛适用策略，为量子材料中对称性破缺相的光学控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Optical control of symmetry-breaking quantum phases is a central goal in quantum materials, yet deterministic switching is often hindered by the stability of single-domain ground states. The chiral structure of the charge density wave (CDW) in 1T-TaS$_2$ provides a natural platform for such control, but the pristine material remains locked in a single chirality. Here we show that combining chemical doping with femtosecond optical excitation enables efficient and non-thermal switching of the chiral CDW state and reveal its microscopic mechanism. Ti substitution stabilizes a ground state with coexisting chiral domains, creating a tunable energy landscape for optical manipulation. Femtosecond photoexcitation then induces asymmetric and anisotropic switching from dominant to minority chiral domains, characterized by in-plane domain growth and a redistribution toward an achiral configuration. The switching occurs on a timescale comparable to a coherent phonon oscillation ($\sim$2 THz), revealing a phonon-mediated pathway that proceeds through a transient domain-wall state. Our results establish a broadly applicable strategy for engineering and controlling chiral order parameters through combined chemical and ultrafast optical tuning.

</details>

Method: 采用磁化强度、比热和低温至90mK的中子衍射测量，结合密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，对TbOF的磁性和结构性质进行全面研究。

Result: 1. 在9.7K观察到结构相变，伴随短程磁关联，而非之前提出的长程反铁磁序；2. 低温下存在两个磁有序转变：非公度自旋密度波以及反铁磁和铁磁关联；3. 在2.0K以下观察到亚稳态和滞后行为；4. 澄清了TbOF的磁相图，揭示了稀土氧氟化物中结构与磁性的复杂相互作用。

Conclusion: TbOF展现出丰富的复杂磁相互作用，包括结构相变、多种磁有序转变以及低温亚稳态行为。这些结果不仅澄清了TbOF的磁相图，还强调了稀土氧氟化物中结构与磁性之间错综复杂的相互作用，为理解受挫混合阴离子系统中的非常规磁性提供了重要见解。

Abstract: We have identified TbOF as a unique frustrated and mixed-anion lattice, hosting unconventional magnetism. By means of magnetization, specific heat and neutron diffraction measurements down to 90 mK, as well as DFT calculations, we present a comprehensive study of the magnetic and structural properties of TbOF. We show that at 9.7 K, TbOF undergoes a structural phase transition accompanied by short-range magnetic correlations, in contrast to previously proposed long-range antiferromagnetic order. At lower temperatures, we observe two magnetic ordering transitions, consisting of incommensurate spin density waves and antiferromagnetic and ferromagnetic correlations. Furthermore, we observe metastable and hysteresis behavior below 2.0 K, highlighting the richness of complex magnetic interactions in TbOF. These results uniquely clarify the magnetic phase diagram of TbOF and highlight the intricate interplay between structure and magnetism in rare-earth oxyfluorides.

</details>


### [95] [Impurity quadrupole moments as local probes of flux sectors in the Kitaev spin liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23145)
