Method: 采用SO(6) Majorana表示处理自旋-3/2杂质算符，结合各向同性Kitaev自旋液体模型，使用自洽平均场近似处理杂质相关项，分析杂质四极矩在通量扇区转变点的行为。

Result: 1. 杂质四极矩在通量扇区转变点出现不连续跳跃，可用于识别基态通量扇区；2. 磁场下杂质间的四极矩关联呈现指数衰减，衰减率对通量扇区敏感；3. 分析了与杂质结合的π通量相对于模型参数和内部通量构型的稳定性，并与Lieb关于通量构型的猜想建立了联系。

Conclusion: 磁性杂质的四极矩可作为研究Kitaev磁体中分数量化激发和通量物理的灵敏探针，为探测量子自旋液体中的通量构型提供了新方法。

Abstract: Emergent fluxes play a central role in the low-energy properties of quantum spin liquids (QSLs), where they encode the underlying gauge structure and fractionalization of spins. Here, we show that the quadrupole moment of magnetic impurities provides a direct probe of these flux configurations in QSLs. Employing the SO(6) Majorana representation for spin-3/2 impurity operators in the isotropic Kitaev spin liquid together with a self-consistent mean-field approximation for impurity-related terms, we show that the ground-state flux sector can be identified by discontinuous jumps of the impurity quadrupole moment at the flux sector transition points. We also demonstrate that the quadrupole correlations between impurities under a magnetic field exhibit exponential decay, with decay rates that depend sensitively on the flux sector. Furthermore, we discuss the stability of pi fluxes bound to impurities with respect to model parameters and internal flux configurations, and relate our findings to Lieb's conjecture on flux configurations. These results establish the quadrupole moments of magnetic impurities as a sensitive tool to study fractionalized excitations and flux physics in Kitaev magnets.

</details>


### [96] [From Quantum Dimers to the $π$-flux Toric Code via Deconfined Multicriticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23154)
*Ankush Chaubey,Sergej Moroz,Subhro Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过张量积正则化方法，将RK二聚体模型与π-通量环面码连接，实现了从二聚体晶体到Z2拓扑液体的转变，揭示了分数化与规范涨落之间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二维RK二聚体模型在二分晶格上通常只能形成平移对称性破缺的二聚体晶体，限制了拓扑液体相的探索。作者旨在构建一个理论框架，能够从RK模型连续过渡到Z2拓扑液体，从而研究分数化与规范涨落之间的相互作用。

Method: 引入二聚体希尔伯特空间的张量积正则化，构建一个连接RK模型与π-通量环面码的量子比特哈密顿量。使用无限密度矩阵重整化群(iDMRG)结合低能场论方法，确定相图并分析相变性质。

Result: 获得包含三个相变的相图：1) Z2液体与柱状/斑块VBS之间的3D XY*连续相变；2) 两个二聚体晶体之间的量子Lifshitz相变；3) 交错晶体与Z2液体之间的一级相变。场论表明存在一个由z=2的Abelian Higgs模型描述的去禁闭多临界点，三个相变在此交汇。

Conclusion: 通过张量积正则化框架成功实现了从RK二聚体模型到Z2拓扑液体的转变，揭示了Z2液体源于多临界U(1)自旋液体通过电荷-2希格斯场凝聚而形成，避免了禁闭。该研究强调了分数化与涌现规范涨落之间的深刻相互作用。

Abstract: Two-dimensional Rokhsar-Kivelson (RK) dimer models on bipartite lattices are generally limited to translation-symmetry-broken dimer crystals. We introduce a tensor-product regularisation of the dimer Hilbert space that yields a qubit Hamiltonian interpolating from the RK model to the $π$-flux toric code, thereby accessing a deconfined $\mathbb{Z}_2$ topological liquid. In this framework, the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ liquid descends from a multicritical $U(1)$ spin liquid through condensation of a charge-2 Higgs field, thus avoiding confinement. Using iDMRG together with low-energy field theory, we determine a phase diagram containing two continuous quantum phase transitions -- a $3\mathrm{D}$ XY$^{\ast}$ transition between the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ liquid and the columnar/plaquette-VBS, and a quantum Lifshitz transition between two dimer crystals -- alongside a first-order transition between the staggered crystal and the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ liquid. Our field theory suggests a deconfined multicritical point described by an Abelian Higgs model with dynamical critical exponent, $z=2$, where the three transitions meet, highlighting the interplay of fractionalisation and emergent gauge fluctuations.

</details>


### [97] [Open Quantum Cluster Embedding Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23163)
*Petar Brinić,Hugo U. R. Strand,Jakša Vučičević*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出量子团簇嵌入理论，通过嵌入团簇和Lindblad方程计算强关联电子系统的动力学响应函数，避免解析延拓问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联电子系统的模拟面临挑战，特别是某些实验相关的动力学响应函数计算困难，存在有限尺寸效应和病态解析延拓问题

Method: 提出量子团簇嵌入理论：使用Lindblad方程描述的开放量子系统作为嵌入团簇代表短程关联；通过逆线性响应理论获得频率依赖的动力学磁化率；调整团簇的马尔可夫浴使团簇和晶格的双线性算符期望值和双占据期望值保持一致

Result: 方法在非相互作用、原子和无限团簇尺寸极限下数值精确，满足总电荷和能量守恒；能够处理大晶格并避免解析延拓；在方晶格Hubbard模型中计算电荷-电荷关联函数，与冷原子实验定性一致

Conclusion: 量子团簇嵌入理论为计算强关联电子系统的动力学响应函数提供了一种有效方法，避免了传统方法的解析延拓问题，能够处理大系统并与实验观测定性吻合

Abstract: The simulation of strongly correlated electron systems remains a formidable challenge. Certain experimentally relevant dynamical response functions are especially difficult to calculate, due to issues of finite-size effects and the ill posed analytic continuation. To address this we propose the quantum cluster embedding theory, an embedded cluster method aimed at computing the response of the system following an external perturbation; the frequency dependent dynamical susceptibility is obtained subsequently by means of inverse linear response theory. The embedded clusters, used within the method as representative of short range correlations, are open quantum systems governed by the Lindblad equation. The short-range correlations extracted from the clusters are used to close the equations of motion for the fermionic bilinear and the local double occupancy on the lattice. In turn, the clusters' Markovian baths are tuned to keep the bilinear and the double occupancy expectation values on the clusters and the lattice identical, throughout the concomitant evolution of the two sets of equations. The theory becomes numerically exact in the non-interacting, atomic and infinite cluster size limits, and it respects the total charge and energy conservation laws. We show that our approach can treat very large lattices while avoiding analytic continuation through the explicit time evolution. Finally we compute the charge-charge correlation function in the square lattice Hubbard model and compare with a recent cold atom experiment, finding good qualitative agreement.

</details>


### [98] [Fermiology, charge transfer energy, and robust paramagnons in high-$T_c$ cuprate superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23281)
*Maciej Fidrysiak*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过Hubbard和t-J-U模型，揭示了铜氧化物高温超导体中稳健顺磁子能量与电子结构参数（次近邻与最近邻跳跃积分比r和电荷转移能Δ_CT）的关系，解释了不同铜酸盐中磁激发带宽可比性的原因，并对Bi系铜酸盐的磁动力学进行了定量预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物高温超导体中的顺磁子激发具有稳健性，其传播能量对空穴浓度不敏感，且与最大超导转变温度相关。然而，不同铜酸盐家族（甚至同一家族内）的电子结构存在差异，阐明与高温超导相关的微观参数与顺磁子动力学之间的关系仍是理论面临的关键挑战。

Method: 采用描述CuO2平面的标准Hubbard模型和t-J-U模型，将稳健的顺磁子能量与高温超导费米学（通过次近邻与最近邻跳跃积分比r = t'/|t|）和电荷转移能Δ_CT联系起来。利用实验测得的r和Δ_CT值作为理论输入，研究了Bi系铜酸盐（最多包含三个CuO2平面）的磁动力学。

Result: 研究表明，不同材料间r和Δ_CT的变化对顺磁子能量产生相反的影响，这解释了多种铜酸盐中磁激发带宽可比性的原因。利用经验r和Δ_CT值，理论计算得到的顺磁子能量与实验测量结果在6%的误差范围内定量吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作为强关联电子系统中稳健顺磁子物理的定量控制提供了一条途径，建立了微观电子结构参数与顺磁子动力学之间的定量关系，有助于理解铜氧化物高温超导机制。

Abstract: Copper-oxide high-temperature (high-$T_c$) superconductors host robust paramagnon excitations whose propagation energies are insensitive to hole concentration and correlate with maximal measured superconducting transition temperatures. Given variation of electronic structure across (and within) cuprate families, elucidation of the relationship between microscopic parameters relevant to high-$T_c$ superconductivity and paramagnon dynamics remains a key challenge to theory. Employing canonical Hubbard- and $t$-$J$-$U$ models of a $\mathrm{CuO_2}$ plane, we relate robust paramagnon energies to high-$T_c$ fermiology (via the ratio $r \equiv t^\prime/|t|$ of next-nearest- to nearest-neighbor hopping integrals) and charge transfer energy, $Δ_\mathrm{CT}$. It is shown that variation of $r$ and $Δ_\mathrm{CT}$ between materials has an opposite effect on paramagnon energy, rationalizing comparable bandwidth of magnetic excitations across multiple cuprates. Utilizing empirical values of $r$ and $Δ_\mathrm{CT}$ as input to theory, we address magnetic dynamics in Bi-family of cuprates with up to three $\mathrm{CuO_2}$ planes, and demonstrate quantitative (within $6\,\%$ margin) agreement of calculated paramagnon energies with experiment. Our work offers a route toward quantitative control of robust paramagnon physics in strongly-correlated electron systems.

</details>


### [99] [Multistage spin correlations in the $s$ = 1/2 stuffed hyper-star lattice Li$_{2}$Cu$_{2}$(MoO$_{4}$)$_{3}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23350)
*J. Khatua,Taeyun Kim,G. Senthil Murugan,S. M. Kumawat,C. -L. Huang,Yugo Oshima,Hiroyuki Nojiri,Gerald Morris,Sarah R. Dunsiger,Heung-Sik Kim,K. Sritharan,Shankar Mani,R. Sankar,Kwang-Yong Choi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 三维星晶格材料Li₂Cu₂(MoO₄)₃在低温下表现出从短程自旋关联到无序驱动的随机单重态（RS-like）态的交叉，并在更低温度下形成准冻结态


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三维星晶格（hyper-star lattice）作为实现奇异量子态（如量子自旋液体和无序驱动的随机单重态）的稀有平台，探索其基态特性

Method: 通过热力学和微观测量研究Li₂Cu₂(MoO₄)₃材料，分析其在不同温度区间的幂律行为变化

Result: 在T*~15.8 K以下观察到从短程自旋关联到无序驱动的RS-like态的交叉，表现为观测量的幂律行为从~T^0.25变为~T^{-0.50}；在T_f=0.32 K附近出现准冻结态

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了孤立自旋和弱残余相互作用在三维无序驱动量子无序态稳定中的关键作用

Abstract: Star lattice, which can be visualized as a honeycomb network with each vertex replaced by a triangle, provides a rare platform for realizing exotic quantum states such as quantum spin liquids and disorder-driven random-singlet (RS) states. Herein, we investigate the ground-state properties of the three-dimensional (3D) stuffed hyper-star lattice Li$_2$Cu$_2$(MoO$_4$)$_3$, which exhibits a crossover from short-range spin correlations to a disorder-driven RS-like state below $T^{*}\sim$15.8 K. Thermodynamic and microscopic measurements capture this crossover through a change in the power-law behavior of various observables, from $\sim T^{0.25}$ for $T > T^{*}$ to $\sim T^{-0.50}$ for $T < T^{*}$. Upon further cooling, a quasi-frozen state emerges near $T_{\rm f} = 0.32$ K, likely associated with weakly coupled spin chains within the hyper-star spin network. Our results underscore the crucial role of orphan spins and weak residual interactions in stabilizing a disorder-driven quantum-disordered state in 3D.

</details>


### [100] [Topological Filtering and Emergent Kondo Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23403)
*Ryosuke Yoshii,Rio Oto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 拓扑孤子诱导的Kondo效应：一维狄拉克系统中具有变号质量项的拓扑孤子产生局域零模，其空间扩展波函数导致动量依赖的交换耦合，产生抑制高能散射的非平庸形状因子，从而形成能量依赖的有效Kondo耦合，使孤子的实空间结构直接控制涌现的Kondo能标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拓扑缺陷如何通过其波函数结构控制多体物理的能量标度，探索通过拓扑工程调控多体能标的一般原理。

Method: 研究一维狄拉克系统中具有变号质量项的拓扑孤子，分析孤子所承载的局域零模及其空间扩展波函数，推导动量依赖的交换耦合，计算由此产生的非平庸形状因子，研究其对高能散射过程的抑制效应，建立能量依赖的有效Kondo耦合理论。

Result: 拓扑孤子的空间扩展波函数导致动量依赖的交换耦合，产生抑制高能散射的非平庸形状因子，形成能量依赖的有效Kondo耦合，使孤子的实空间结构直接控制涌现的Kondo能标。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了拓扑缺陷通过其波函数结构控制多体能量标度的机制，提出了通过拓扑工程调控多体能标的一般原理。

Abstract: We study the Kondo effect induced by a topological soliton in a one-dimensional Dirac system with the sign-changing mass term. The soliton hosts a localized zero mode whose spatially extended wavefunction leads to a momentum-dependent exchange coupling with itinerant electrons. We show that this structure generates a nontrivial form factor that suppresses high-energy scattering processes, resulting in an energy-dependent effective Kondo coupling. As a consequence, the real-space structure of the soliton directly controls the emergent Kondo scale. This work establishes a mechanism by which topological defects control many-body energy scales through their wavefunction structure, suggesting a general principle for engineering many-body energy scales via topology.

</details>

### [101] [Continuous-data-assimilation-enabled fast and robust convergence of an Uzawa-based solver for Navier-Stokes equations with large Reynolds number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22534)
*Victoria Luongo Fisher,Jessica C. Franklin,Leo G. Rebholz*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合连续数据同化(CDA)的Uzawa非线性求解器(CDA-Uzawa)，用于不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程，证明其能加速收敛并在任意大雷诺数下保持收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程求解中，基于物理分裂的非线性求解器虽然每次迭代效率高，但通常收敛缓慢且鲁棒性不足的问题，研究如何利用连续数据同化来改善求解器的收敛性能。

Method: 开发了CDA-Uzawa求解器，将部分解数据通过连续数据同化技术整合到Uzawa迭代中。该方法包含理论分析证明收敛性加速和鲁棒性提升，并针对含噪声数据提出了结合牛顿法的混合策略。

Result: 理论证明：1) CDA-Uzawa能加速收敛的Uzawa迭代，且部分解数据越多加速效果越明显；2) 在足够多的部分解数据下，即使存在多个NSE解，CDA-Uzawa也能在任意大雷诺数下收敛。对于含噪声数据，收敛结果可达到噪声水平，并提出达到下限后转用牛顿法的策略。

Conclusion: CDA-Uzawa是一种高效有效的求解器，数值测试验证了理论结果。虽然本文聚焦于NSE的特定分裂求解器，但其核心思想具有普适性，可扩展到更广泛的非线性求解器类别。

Abstract: This paper shows how continuous data assimilation (CDA) can be used to provably enable and accelerate convergence of a (efficient at each iteration due to a physics-splitting, but generally slowly converging and not robust) nonlinear solver for incompressible Navier-Stokes equations (NSE). Herein we develop, analyze and test an Uzawa-based nonlinear solver for incompressible NSE that incorporates partial solution data into the iteration through continuous data assimilation (CDA-Uzawa). We rigorously prove that i) CDA-Uzawa will accelerate a converging Uzawa iteration, and more partial solution data yields more acceleration, and ii) with enough partial solution data CDA-Uzawa will converge for arbitrarily large Reynolds numbers, even if multiple NSE solutions exist. In the case of noisy data, we prove that the convergence results hold down to the size of the noise, and we propose a strategy to pass to Newton once CDA-Uzawa convergence reaches its lower limit. Results of several numerical tests illustrate the theory and show CDA-Uzawa is a very effective and efficient solver. While this paper focuses a particular splitting-based solver for the NSE, the key ideas are quite general and extendable to a wide class of nonlinear solvers.

</details>


### [102] [Matrix-Free Stabilized BDF Schemes for Semilinear Parabolic Equations with Unconditional Maximum Bound Principle Preservation and Energy Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22762)
*Haishen Dai,Huan Lei,Bin Zheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一族稳定化的向后差分公式(sBDF)方案(1-4阶)，用于半线性抛物方程，同时实现最大界原理无条件保持、无条件离散能量稳定性和实用无矩阵实现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发高阶时间离散方法，同时满足三个通常难以兼得的特性：无条件保持最大界原理、无条件离散能量稳定性、实用无矩阵实现，以解决一般域和混合边界条件下FFT指数时间差分方法不可用或效率低的问题

Method: 结合精心设计的稳定项、定点迭代和逐点截断策略，构建sBDF方案；通过定点迭代求解隐式sBDF离散产生的非线性代数系统，实现完全无矩阵算法

Result: 建立了完全实现方案的统一分析框架，证明了定点迭代的无条件收缩性和误差估计；对于Allen-Cahn方程，证明了无条件离散能量耗散定律；数值实验验证了理论收敛率，展示了方法在混合边界条件下的鲁棒性和效率

Conclusion: sBDF方案成功实现了三个关键特性的同时满足，为一般域和混合边界条件下的半线性抛物方程提供了高效、稳定且实用的高阶时间离散方法，特别优于基于ETD的方法

Abstract: We develop a family of stabilized backward differentiation formula (sBDF) schemes of orders one through four for semilinear parabolic equations. The proposed methods are designed to achieve three properties that are rarely available simultaneously in high-order time discretizations: unconditional preservation of the maximum bound principle (MBP), unconditional discrete energy stability, and practical matrix-free implementation. The construction integrates carefully designed stabilization terms, fixed-point iterations, and a pointwise cut-off strategy. The nonlinear algebraic systems arising from the implicit sBDF discretizations are solved by fixed-point iteration, resulting in fully matrix-free algorithms. This makes the approach particularly attractive for practical computations on general domains and under mixed boundary conditions, where FFT-based exponential time differencing methods are often unavailable or inefficient. We further present a unified analysis for the fully implemented schemes, explicitly incorporating the interplay among time discretization, nonlinear iteration, and cut-off. Unconditional contractivity of the fixed-point iterations and error estimates are established. For the Allen-Cahn equation, we additionally prove an unconditional discrete energy dissipation law. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical convergence rates and demonstrate the robustness and efficiency of the proposed methods, particularly relative to ETD-based approaches for problems with mixed boundary conditions.

</details>


### [103] [Data-dependent approximation through RBF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23074)
*José Kuruc,David Levin,Pep Mulet,Juan Ruiz-Álvarez,Dionisio F. Yáñez*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种改进的径向基函数插值方法，通过自适应调整形状参数来减少间断附近的振荡，使核函数在间断处近似Kronecker delta函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典RBF插值在间断附近会产生虚假振荡，需要开发一种能自适应处理间断的插值方法，在保持平滑区域精度的同时减少间断附近的振荡。

Method: 引入自适应机制，使RBF形状参数依赖于数据并在间断附近趋于无穷大，从而局部近似Kronecker delta函数。使用光滑度指示器检测间断：对于网格数据使用二阶差分平方，对于散乱数据使用拉普拉斯算子的最小二乘近似乘以局部点间距平方。

Result: 证明了改进后插值矩阵的可逆性，并提出了保持条件数可控的求解策略。数值实验表明，该方法能显著减少一维和二维间断附近的振荡，同时保持平滑区域的插值精度和矩阵条件数基本不变。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应RBF插值方法能有效减少间断附近的虚假振荡，适用于局部和全局支持的核函数类型，在保持平滑区域性能的同时改善了间断处的插值质量。

Abstract: In this article we present a modification of classical Radial Basis Function (RBF) interpolation techniques aimed at reducing oscillations near discontinuities in one and two dimensions. Our approach introduces an adaptive mechanism by varying the shape parameter of the RBFs and making it data-dependent, forcing it to tend to infinity in the vicinity of discontinuities. This modification results in kernel functions that locally resemble %Kronecker delta functions, effectively minimizing spurious oscillations.
  To detect discontinuities, we employ smoothness indicators: for grid-based data, these are computed as undivided second-order differences squared. For scattered data, we use least squares approximations of the Laplacian multiplied by the square of the mean local separation of the stencil points, and then squared. These indicators guide the adaptive adjustment of the shape parameter.
  We prove the invertibility of the resulting interpolation matrix and propose a solution strategy that maintains the condition number comparable to that of a system where points near discontinuities are excluded. Numerical experiments in one and two dimensions demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces oscillations near discontinuities across various kernel types, whether locally or globally supported. At the same time, the interpolation accuracy and matrix conditioning in smooth regions remain essentially unchanged, as measured by the infinity norm of the error and the condition number.

</details>


### [104] [A Systematic Framework for Stable and Cost-Efficient Matrix Polynomial Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23143)
*J. M. Alonso,J. Sastre,J. Ibáñez,E. Defez*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出系统化框架和MATLAB工具MatrixPolEval1，用于获取和验证矩阵多项式求值的稳定系数集，在保持Paterson-Stockmeyer方法数值精度的同时减少一次矩阵乘法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化矩阵多项式求值方法比Paterson-Stockmeyer方法少一次矩阵乘法，但系数来自非线性方程组的多解，不当选择会导致浮点运算数值不稳定，需要系统化方法确保稳定性

Method: 开发系统化框架和MATLAB实现MatrixPolEval1，引入结构变体保持稳定性，使用变精度算术(VPA)识别稳定系数集，并提供可靠性指标预测精度

Result: 成功获得m∈{8,10,12}及以上度数矩阵多项式的稳定系数集，在矩阵指数和几何级数等应用中的数值实验显示，在保持PS方法数值精度的同时实现了1M节省

Conclusion: 提出的框架能有效获取稳定系数集，在减少计算成本的同时保持数值稳定性，为大规模矩阵计算提供了实用的优化工具

Abstract: A method for evaluating matrix polynomials have recently been developed that require one fewer matrix product ($1M$) than the Paterson--Stockmeyer (PS) method. Since the computational cost for large-scale matrices is asymptotically determined by the number of matrix products, this reduction directly affects the total execution time. However, the coefficients in these optimized formulas emerge as solutions to systems of nonlinear polynomial equations, resulting in multiple potential solution sets. An inappropriate selection of these coefficients can lead to numerical instability in floating-point arithmetic.
  This paper presents a systematic framework and a MATLAB implementation, MatrixPolEval1, used to obtain and validate stable coefficient sets for matrix polynomials of degrees $m \in \{8, 10, 12\}$ and above. The framework introduces structural variants to maintain stability even when the original configuration fails to yield a robust solution. The provided tool identifies stable coefficient sets using variable precision arithmetic (VPA) and provides a reliability indicator for expected accuracy. Numerical experiments on polynomials arising in applications, including the matrix exponential and geometric series, show that the framework achieves the $1M$ saving while maintaining numerical accuracy comparable to the PS method.

</details>


### [105] [Long-time error analysis of finite element fully discrete schemes for SPDEs with non-globally Lipschitz coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23167)
*Ruisheng Qi,Xiaojie Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了新的全离散格式，用于长时间逼近有界域中具有非全局Lipschitz系数的随机偏微分方程，该格式基于线性隐式时间步进和有限元空间离散，在Banach空间L^r(D)中保持时间一致矩有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有非全局Lipschitz系数的SPDEs的长时间数值逼近问题，现有方法通常需要时间-空间离散步长比的限制，本文旨在开发无需此限制且能保持时间一致矩有界性的数值格式。

Method: 提出基于标准Galerkin有限元空间半离散的新型线性隐式时间步进格式，结合驯服策略处理非线性项；首先推导确定性线性抛物方程在Banach空间L^r(D)中的长时间误差估计，然后利用半群在L^r(D)中的压缩性、非线性项的耗散性和驯服策略的优势建立时间一致矩有界性。

Result: 证明了所提格式在Banach空间L^r(D)中具有时间一致矩有界性，无需时间-空间离散步长比限制；在低正则性设置下分析了强误差和弱误差界，对空间-时间白噪声和迹类噪声情况获得了时间一致收敛率；数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文成功开发了用于非全局Lipschitz系数SPDEs长时间逼近的新型全离散格式，该格式在无需离散步长比限制下保持时间一致矩有界性，为低正则性设置下的数值分析提供了新工具，具有理论和实际意义。

Abstract: The present paper proposes new fully discrete schemes for long-time approximations of stochastic partial differential equations (SPDEs) with non-globally Lipschitz coefficients in a bounded domain $D \subset \R^d, d =1,2,3 $. A novel family of linearly implicit time-stepping schemes is introduced, based on a standard Galerkin finite element spatial semi-discretization. A distinguishing feature of the schemes is that the proposed finite element fully discrete approximations preserve uniform-in-time moment bounds in a Banach space $L^{r}(D), r >2$, without requiring any restriction on the time-space discretization stepsize ratio. %established... To show it, some non-standard arguments are developed. First, we derive long-time error estimates in the Banach space $L^r(D)$ for finite element fully discrete approximations of the deterministic linear parabolic equation with non-smooth initial value, which is, to our knowledge, new for the literature on numerical PDEs and of independent interest. These error estimates together with the contractive property of the semi-group in $L^{r}(D), r > 2$, the dissipativity of the nonlinearity and the particular benefit of the taming strategy help us establish the desired uniform-in-time moment bounds. Then both strong and weak error bounds of the proposed schemes are carefully analyzed in a setting of low regularity, with uniform-in-time convergence rates obtained for cases of both space-time white and trace-class noises. The analysis is highly nontrivial, due to the finite element discretization, the low regularity and the presence of the super-linearly growing nonlinearity. %in the long-time scenario... the discretization parameters $h$ and $τ$. Finally, numerical results are presented to verify the previous theoretical findings.

</details>


### [106] [Arbitrary order stationarity preserving stabilized finite elements for multidimensional nonlinear hyperbolic problems. Application to the Euler equations with gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23185)
*Moussa Ziggaf,Davide Torlo,Mario Ricchiuto*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 开发了任意高阶、保持定常性的稳定化有限元方法，用于多维非线性双曲平衡律，能够高精度逼近所有稳态（包括非平凡多维平衡态），并扩展到非线性平衡律问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在保持定常性方面存在局限，特别是对于非线性双曲平衡律中的多维平衡态。需要开发能够高精度逼近所有稳态（包括非平凡多维平衡态）的数值方法，同时保持方法的鲁棒性和准确性。

Method: 将多维全局通量求积重新表述为物理通量的局部预处理，将连续多项式向量场映射到具有Raviart-Thomas型结构的局部空间。在Galerkin和SUPG公式中应用此框架，扩展了定常性保持有限体积方法到高阶连续有限元和非线性平衡律。

Result: 方法实现了局部守恒性和离散稳态核的节点超收敛性，与低马赫数相容离散化相关。应用于重力作用下可压缩欧拉方程时，通过简单的源项重构实现了等温流体静力平衡的机器精度保持。数值基准测试显示在鲁棒性和准确性上优于标准SUPG和参考有限体积方法。

Conclusion: 提出的框架成功地将定常性保持方法扩展到高阶连续有限元和非线性平衡律，能够高精度逼近多维平衡态，在多种流动状态下表现出优越的性能，为复杂物理问题的数值模拟提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We develop arbitrarily high-order, stationarity-preserving stabilized finite element methods for multidimensional nonlinear hyperbolic balance laws on Cartesian grids. We aim at approximating all the steady states of the problem at hand, including non-trivial genuinely multidimensional equilibria, with a level of accuracy higher than the nominal one of the underlying scheme. We formalize more precisely the meaning of stationarity preservation, providing some technical conditions for its realizability. We then recast the multidimensional global-flux quadrature of Barsukow et al. (Num. Meth. PDEs, 2025) as a local preprocessing of the physical fluxes that maps continuous polynomial vector fields to a local space with Raviart--Thomas-type structure. Both the Galerkin and SUPG formulations are recast in this setting. The resulting methods extend the stationarity-preserving finite-volume approach of Barsukow et al. (J. Comput. Phys., 2026) to high-order continuous finite elements and Barsukow et al. (Num. Meth. PDEs, 2025) to nonlinear balance laws. We analyze key properties of the proposed schemes, including local conservation and nodal superconvergence of the discrete steady kernel, and we discuss their relation to low-Mach-compliant discretizations. We apply the framework to the compressible Euler equations with gravity. A simple source-term reformulation yields machine-precision preservation of isothermal hydrostatic equilibria. Extensive numerical benchmarks, including moving equilibrium, near-equilibrium, and instability-dominated regimes, demonstrate clear improvements in robustness and accuracy over standard SUPG and reference finite-volume methods.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Shaping the diffuse X-ray sky: Structure, Variability and Visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22392)
*Philipp Girichidis,Erika Rea,Ralf S. Klessen,Michael C. H. Yeung,Efrem Maconi,Manami Sasaki,Michael Freyberg,Juan D. Soler*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过磁流体动力学模拟研究局部泡类似物的X射线特性，发现X射线发射高度集中在超新星周围的小体积区域，柱密度效应显著影响可观测信号，且X射线通量在百万年时间尺度上呈现剧烈变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部泡是太阳系所在的低密度热腔，其X射线发射受气体密度、温度和视线柱密度影响，但控制该发射形成和演化的物理过程尚未完全理解。研究旨在通过模拟分析揭示塑造局部泡X射线特性的关键物理因素。

Method: 在磁流体动力学模拟中识别局部泡类似物，通过后处理分析X射线发射的空间分布、变异性和观测约束条件，研究超新星事件对发射特性的影响。

Result: 发现三个主要结果：1）超新星后X射线发射高度集中在超新星周围的小体积区域（95%光度来自<1%体积）；宁静期发射形态变化显著。2）柱密度效应强烈调制可观测X射线信号，NH>10²⁰ cm⁻²的气体有效吸收软X射线光子。3）X射线通量在百万年时间尺度上呈现剧烈变化，超新星事件导致快速瞬时光度增强，随后因绝热冷却而陡降。

Conclusion: 局部泡的X射线特性由超新星驱动的热区域空间分布、视线柱密度效应和时间变异性共同塑造，这些因素对理解气泡X射线发射的物理过程具有重要意义。

Abstract: The Local Bubble (LB) is a hot, low-density cavity in the solar neighborhood, inside which the Solar System is currently located. The X-ray emission from such bubbles is strongly governed by the gas density, temperature, and the effects of line-of-sight column density. Yet the physical processes that control the formation and evolution of this emission remain incompletely understood. We analyze a LB analogue identified within a magnetohydrodynamical simulation to investigate the key physical factors that shape its X-ray properties. In post-processing, we examine the spatial distribution, variability, and observational constraints of the X-ray emission. Our study reveals three main results: (1) Shortly after a supernova (SN), the bulk of the X-ray emission arises from a small fraction of the bubble's volume, concentrated in hot regions around recent SN sites. Approximately 95% of the X-ray luminosity originates from less than 1% of the total bubble volume. During quiescent phases without recent SNe, the emission morphology changes substantially, with X-ray-bright regions becoming more volume-filling. (2) Column density effects strongly modulate the observable X-ray signal. Gas with column densities exceeding $N_\mathrm{H} \gtrsim 10^{20} \,\mathrm{cm}^{-2}$ efficiently absorbs soft X-ray photons, limiting the depth to which observations can probe. This absorption causes a significant fraction of the sky to be obscured from external soft X-rays. Differences between active and quiescent phases further influence how much of the total bubble emission is visible from within. (3) The X-ray flux shows pronounced temporal variability on Myr timescales, with SN events producing rapid, transient luminosity enhancements, followed by steep declines due to adiabatic cooling. The total flux varies by several orders of magnitude, with SN-driven peaks fading within $10^5$ years.

</details>


### [108] [The Crimson Kiss of Two Giants: Helium Detonation and High-Energy Neutrino Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22419)
*Cecilia Romero Rodríguez,Pau Amaro Seoane*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 红巨星碰撞中简并氦核合并（称为erythrohenosis）产生高能中微子源，可解释IceCube观测到的弥散中微子通量，并产生独特的多信使信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究红巨星碰撞中简并氦核合并过程，探索其作为高能中微子新来源的可能性，并解释IceCube观测到的弥散中微子通量。

Method: 使用MESA生成恒星模型和SPH模拟最终螺旋阶段，开发半解析模型估计氢混合量、能量释放、磁场放大和中微子通量。

Result: 预测的TeV-PeV中微子信号可解释IceCube观测的弥散通量；单个2 Mpc内的合并事件在该能区可探测；磁场放大至1.77×10¹⁰ G；温度升至5.3×10⁸ K；能量释放4.28×10⁴⁹ erg。

Conclusion: erythrohenosis事件是致密恒星系统中可观测的奇异现象，能产生高能强子中微子、引力波和可能的电磁信号，构成独特的多信使特征。

Abstract: The coalescence of degenerate helium cores during red giant collisions - a process we term erythrohenosis - introduces a novel class of transient astrophysical sources of high-energy neutrinos. Using stellar models generated with MESA and SPH simulations of the final inspiral phase, we develop a semi-analytical model to estimate the amount of hydrogen mixed into the cores, the energy release ($\approx 4.28 \times 10^{49}$ erg) that heats the remnant to $T_f \approx 5.3 \times 10^8$ K, the magnetic field amplification ($B \approx 1.77 \times 10^{10}$ G), and the resulting neutrino flux. We find that the predicted TeV--PeV neutrino signal can account for the diffuse neutrino flux observed by IceCube and demonstrate that a single merger event within $\sim 2$ Mpc would be detectable in this energy regime. Furthermore, we discuss the probability of a magnetized helium flash and assess the subsequent activation of the CNO cycle in the remnant core due to hydrogen mixing. In particular, neutrinos from the decay of $^{18}$F offer a direct observational test of the detonation. The simultaneous emission of high-energy hadronic neutrinos, gravitational waves, and -- if the optical depth permits -- an electromagnetic signal would constitute a unique multimessenger signature of red giant core collisions, positioning erythrohenosis events as exotic yet potentially observable phenomena in dense stellar systems.

</details>


### [109] [A large population of over-massive black hole quasars at z=0.3-0.8 revealed by eROSITA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22425)
*Johannes Buchner,Isabelle Gauger,Qiaoya Wu,Hattie Starck,Catarina Aydar,Yue Shen,Vardha N. Bennert,Kirpal Nandra,Sophia G. H. Waddell,Andrea Merloni,Mara Salvato,Roberto J. Assef,Zsofi Igo,Franz E. Bauer,Dong-Woo Kim,Anton M. Koekemoer,Donald P. Schneider*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究发现演化星系中也存在超大质量黑洞，表明黑洞质量过大并非黑洞形成通道的独特特征，而是存在与恒星形成无关的独立吸积通道


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近JWST在矮星系和宇宙黎明期观测到黑洞质量占比高达10%的极端超大质量黑洞，这被解释为超大质量黑洞起源的迹象。本研究旨在验证这种超大质量黑洞是否也存在于演化星系中，以确定其是否仅为黑洞形成通道的特征

Method: 利用eROSITA X射线望远镜的大面积巡天数据识别200个类星体，结合SDSS光学光谱、紫外到红外观测数据，采用星系-类星体分解技术，获得无偏的宇宙学距离、黑洞质量和宿主星系恒星质量估计

Result: 安全识别出黑洞质量占比超过5%的超大质量黑洞样本，这些黑洞可能经历了持续约10亿年的指数吸积爆发。在宇宙正午附近发现至少4/Gpc³的高空间密度超大质量黑洞

Conclusion: 超大质量黑洞并非黑洞形成通道的独特特征，而是存在与恒星种群无关的独立吸积通道，这一通道目前未被纳入星系演化模型。如果该通道在宇宙早期也存在，可以解释JWST观测到的活动星系核，而不需要将其归因于黑洞播种机制的印记

Abstract: In most galaxies, the central black hole accounts for no more than a percent of the total mass in stars. Recently, however, extremely over-massive black holes with ratios of 10% have been reported in dwarf galaxies at z<1 and at cosmic dawn (z>5.5) by JWST. Both findings have been interpreted as signatures of the still mysterious origins of super-massive black holes, such that most of the black hole mass was built at birth rather than through black hole accretion. Here we show that among evolved galaxies over-massive black holes are also present, indicating that overmassive BHs are not a signature unique to black hole formation channels. The first large-area sky survey of the eROSITA X-ray telescope on board SpectrRG identified 200 quasars by their luminous hard X-ray radiation. These signpost rapidly growing black holes. Complementary optical spectroscopy from the Sloan Digital Sky Survey and archival UV to IR photometric data combined with galaxy-quasar decomposition techniques allow us unbiased estimates of cosmological distances, black hole masses and host galaxy stellar masses. We securely identify a sample of over-massive black holes with BH-to-host ratios of more than 5%, which may have undergone exponential accretion spurts lasting about a billion years. Our survey identified a high space density of at least 4/Gpc^3 of overmassive black holes near cosmic noon. This indicates an accretion channel disconnected from the stellar population that cause strong deviations from galaxy scaling relations. This channel is currently not part of galaxy evolution models. The identified channel, if applicable also for the first billion years of cosmic time, can explain JWST AGN without requiring them to signify imprints of black hole seeding mechanism.

</details>


### [110] [Impact of eccentricity on the population properties of neutron star - black hole mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22461)
*Gonzalo Morras,Geraint Pratten,Patricia Schmidt*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 重新分析GWTC-4中的中子星-黑洞并合事件，发现GW200105在20Hz处具有显著残余轨道偏心率，支持动力学形成机制，而其他NSBH事件无可测量偏心率，与孤立恒星演化一致。首次获得NSBH质量、自旋和偏心率分布的联合约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究中子星-黑洞并合系统的群体性质，区分不同形成通道（动力学形成vs孤立恒星演化），利用轨道偏心率和自旋进动作为关键鉴别器来理解紧凑双星系统的天体物理起源。

Method: 使用pyEFPE波形模型（仅包含旋近阶段，能捕捉轨道偏心率与自旋诱导进动效应）重新分析GWTC-4中的所有双中子星和中子星-黑洞事件。采用分层贝叶斯推断方法，首次联合约束NSBH系统的质量、自旋和偏心率分布。

Result: GW170817和GW190425完全符合准圆形旋近；GW200105是唯一在20Hz处显示显著残余偏心率的NSBH系统，支持动力学形成通道；其余NSBH事件无可测量偏心率，与低自旋孤立演化双星一致。首次同时约束了NSBH并合中的自旋进动和轨道偏心率，并合率与先前LVK测量完全一致。

Conclusion: 轨道偏心率已成为区分形成通道的关键指标。将所有NSBH系统视为单一群体时，结果与层次三重系统形成兼容；准圆形群体则与孤立演化一致。随着NSBH探测数量增加，质量、自旋和轨道偏心率的联合约束将能更精确地检验动力学与孤立双星演化模型，确立NSBH系统作为紧凑天体物理的强大探针。

Abstract: We revisit the population properties of neutron star-black hole (NSBH) mergers using low-mass compact binary coalescences reported through GWTC-4. Employing pyEFPE, an inspiral-only waveform model that captures both orbital eccentricity and spin-induced precession, we reanalyse all binary neutron star (BNS) and NSBH events observed via gravitational waves. The BNS systems GW170817 and GW190425 are fully consistent with quasi-circular inspirals, while GW200105 stands out among the NSBH binaries as the only system exhibiting significant residual eccentricity at 20 Hz, strengthening evidence for dynamically driven formation pathways. The remaining NSBH events show no measurable eccentricity and appear broadly compatible with low-spin binaries formed through isolated stellar evolution. Using hierarchical Bayesian inference, we obtain the first joint constraints on the mass, spin, and eccentricity distributions of NSBH binaries. Our results also yield the first simultaneous constraints on spin precession and orbital eccentricity in NSBH mergers, while the inferred merger rates remain fully consistent with previous LVK measurements. Treating all NSBH systems as a single population yields results compatible with formation in hierarchical triples, whereas the quasi-circular population remains broadly consistent with isolated evolution. Our results highlight the emerging role of eccentricity as a key discriminator between formation channels. As the number of NSBH detections grows, joint constraints on masses, spins, and orbital eccentricity will enable increasingly sharp tests of dynamical versus isolated binary evolution, establishing NSBH systems as powerful probes of compact-object astrophysics.

</details>


### [111] [A Multi-messenger Search for Ultra-high-energy Gamma Rays in Coincidence with Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22555)
*R. Alfaro,C. Alvarez,A. Andrés,E. Anita-Rangel,M. Araya,J. C. Arteaga-Velázquez,D. Avila Rojas,H. A. Ayala Solares,R. Babu,P. Bangale,E. Belmont-Moreno,A. Bernal,K. S. Caballero-Mora,T. Capistrán,A. Carramiñana,S. Casanova,U. Cotti,J. Cotzomi,S. Coutiño de León,E. De la Fuente,C. de León,P. Desiati,N. Di Lalla,R. Diaz Hernandez,M. A. DuVernois,J. C. Díaz-Vélez,K. Engel,T. Ergin,C. Espinoza,K. Fang,N. Fraija,S. Fraija,A. Galván-Gámez,J. A. García-González,F. Garfias,N. Ghosh,M. M. González,J. A. González,J. A. Goodman,S. Groetsch,D. Guevel,J. Gyeong,J. P. Harding,S. Hernández-Cadena,I. Herzog,J. Hinton,D. Huang,F. Hueyotl-Zahuantitla,P. Hüntemeyer,A. Iriarte,S. Kaufmann,A. Lara,K. Leavitt,J. Lee,T. Lewis,H. León Vargas,J. T. Linnemann,A. L. Longinotti,G. Luis-Raya,K. Malone,M. Martin,O. Martinez,J. Martínez-Castro,J. A. Matthews,P. Miranda-Romagnoli,P. E. Mirón-Enriquez,J. A. Morales-Soto,E. Moreno,M. Mostafá,M. Najafi,A. Nayerhoda,L. Nellen,N. Omodei,M. Osorio-Archila,E. Ponce,Y. Pérez Araujo,E. G. Pérez-Pérez,C. D. Rho,A. Rodriguez Parra,D. Rosa-González,M. Roth,H. Salazar,D. Salazar-Gallegos,A. Sandoval,M. Schneider,J. Serna-Franco,M. Shin,A. J. Smith,Y. Son,R. W. Springer,O. Tibolla,K. Tollefson,I. Torres,R. Torres-Escobedo,E. Varela,L. Villaseñor,X. Wang,Z. Wang,I. J. Watson,H. Wu,S. Yu,S. Yun-Cárcamo,X. Zhang,H. Zhou*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种从HAWC天文台选择高质量超高能伽马射线事件的方法，并用于搜索HAWC伽马射线与IceCube中微子警报之间的相关性，发现了24个空间重合事件，表明可能存在关联，但受限于仪器角分辨率无法确定具体天体物理源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高能伽马射线源普遍存在但其性质高度不确定，区分其产生机制（轻子性的逆康普顿散射或强子性的π介子衰变）需要多信使观测。中微子仅通过强子通道产生，因此是确定发射机制的关键。

Method: 开发了一种从HAWC天文台选择高质量超高能伽马射线事件的度量标准，使用该标准搜索HAWC档案数据与IceCube公开中微子警报之间的相关性。

Result: 发现了24个空间重合事件，这个数量高于随机偶然的预期值，表明HAWC伽马射线与IceCube中微子之间可能存在关联。

Conclusion: HAWC伽马射线与IceCube中微子之间可能存在关联，但由于两个仪器的角分辨率限制，无法明确地将重合事件与特定的天体物理源联系起来，需要更灵敏的探测器来获得决定性结论。

Abstract: The last five years have shown us that ultra-high-energy (UHE; $>$100 TeV) gamma-ray sources are ubiquitous, but the nature of these sources remain highly uncertain. UHE gamma rays can be produced via either leptonic (Inverse compton) or hadronic (pion decay) emission mechanisms. To decisively determine the emission mechanisms, multimessenger searches are essential. Neutrinos are of particular interest as they are only created via hadronic channels. In this work, we describe a metric to select high-quality UHE events from the High Altitude Water Cherenkov (HAWC) Observatory. We use this metric to search for correlations between HAWC archival data and IceCube public neutrino alerts. 24 spatial coincidences are found, which is higher than the number of events expected by random chance. Therefore, we conclude that there are likely associations between HAWC gamma rays and IceCube neutrinos, but the angular resolutions of the two instruments prevent us from conclusively making any definitive associations between the coincidences and specific astrophysical sources. More sensitive detectors are needed.

</details>

Loporchio,L Lulić,P. Majumdar,M. Makariev,G. Maneva,M. Manganaro,S. Mangano,S. Marchesi,M. Mariotti,M. Martínez,P. Maruševec,D. Mazin,S. Menchiari,J. Méndez Gallego,S. Menon,D. Miceli,R. Mirzoyan,M. Paoletti,J. M. Paredes,M. Peresano,M. Persic,M. Pihet,G. Pirola,F. Podobnik,P. G. Prada Moroni,E. Prandini,W. Rhode,M. Ribó,J. Rico,A. Roy,N. Sahakyan,F. G. Saturni,K. Schmitz,F. Schmuckermaier,T. Yeung,E. Torresi,G. Migliori,P. Grandi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: MAGIC望远镜对低光度射电星系NGC 4278在TeV能段的观测分析，未探测到显著γ射线辐射，但通过能谱建模揭示了粒子主导的发射区域和高效加速机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHAASO合作组首次在TeV能段探测到低光度射电星系NGC 4278，本研究旨在利用MAGIC望远镜进一步研究该源在耀发态和准宁静态的高能特性，验证LHAASO结果并深入理解其物理机制。

Method: 重新分析MAGIC望远镜在2010-2024年间对NGC 4278及其邻近耀变体1ES 1215+303和1ES 1218+304的观测数据；对耀发态和准宁静态分别建立宽带能谱分布模型；计算通量上限并进行物理参数拟合。

Result: MAGIC未探测到NGC 4278的显著γ射线辐射，通量上限（>150 GeV为1.5×10⁻¹² ph s⁻¹ cm⁻²）与LHAASO结果一致；模型显示发射区域强烈粒子主导，需要高效加速机制才能达到TeV能量；耀发态到准宁静态的转变不能简单用辐射冷却解释；喷流功率约10⁴² erg s⁻¹，以动能为主，与先前时均估计相符。

Conclusion: NGC 4278的TeV耀发可能具有重复性，但喷流功率仍不足以突破宿主星系的束缚；研究支持低光度射电星系中存在高效粒子加速过程，为理解此类天体在极高能段的辐射机制提供了重要线索。

Abstract: The Large High Altitude Air Shower Observatory (LHAASO) Collaboration has recently reported the first detection at TeV energies of a low-luminosity radio galaxy, NGC 4278. The aim of this work is to investigate the high-energy properties of NGC 4278 during the flaring and subsequent quasi-quiescent states with the Florian Goebel Major Atmospheric Gamma Imaging Cherenkov (MAGIC) telescopes. NGC 4278 is located in the field of view of two blazars, 1ES 1215+303 and 1ES 1218+304, previously observed by the MAGIC telescopes. Therefore, we re-analyzed MAGIC observations made between 2010 and 2024 on these sources. We also modeled the broadband spectral energy distribution of the source during and after the flaring state at TeV energies. We did not detect any statistically significant $γ$-ray emission from NGC 4278 with MAGIC. The corresponding upper limits obtained using the entire MAGIC dataset ($F_{{\rm UL, }\, >150\, \mathrm{GeV}}=1.5 \times 10^{-12}\, \mathrm{ph \, s^{-1}\, cm^{-2}}$) are consistent with the LHAASO results. The best-fit models obtained for both emission states suggest that the emitting region is strongly particle-dominated, and an efficient acceleration mechanism has to be in action in order to reach TeV energies. The transition between the flaring and quasi-quiescent state cannot be explained by a simple radiative cooling of the emitting particles. The inferred jet power, of the order of $L_{\rm jet}\sim 10^{42}\, \mathrm{erg\,s^{-1}}$, is dominated by the kinetic component in both states and it is in a good agreement with previous, time-averaged observational estimates, supporting the idea that such high-energy flares might be recurrent. The jet, however, remains too weak to break the host-galaxy confinement.

</details>


### [113] [The Persistent Radio Sources and Multi-wavelength Counterparts of Fast Radio Bursts in Massive Binary Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23144)
*Z. Y. Zhao,F. Y. Wang,Z. G. Dai*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文研究快速射电暴（FRBs）在磁星-大质量星双星系统中的电磁对应体，包括持续射电源和风碰撞产生的弓激波辐射，解释了观测到的光度特征和长期变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速射电暴（FRBs）是来自宇宙学距离的毫秒级脉冲，其多波段对应体对揭示物理起源至关重要。最近的观测证据表明，一些活跃FRB的源位于大质量星双星系统中，因此需要研究这些系统中的电磁对应体。

Method: 研究磁星-大质量星双星系统中FRBs的电磁对应体，包括：1）年轻磁星风星云（MWN）产生的持续射电源（PRSs）；2）风碰撞产生的弓激波辐射；3）通过同步辐射和逆康普顿散射产生的多波段辐射。

Result: 发现：1）光度10^38-10^39 erg/s的PRSs可由年轻磁星风星云产生，磁星年龄约数十年；2）PRSs通量密度的长期变化可用磁星内部磁场衰减解释；3）弓激波辐射可解释FRB 20201124A的低光度PRS；4）双星模型在keV、GeV和TeV波段的辐射可分别在约10-100 Mpc、1-10 Mpc和0.1 Mpc距离被现有仪器探测。

Conclusion: 磁星-大质量星双星系统能解释FRBs的多波段对应体观测特征，包括PRSs的光度和变化，以及弓激波辐射。该模型预测的多波段辐射可被现有仪器探测，为FRB物理起源提供了重要线索。

Abstract: Fast radio bursts (FRBs) are millisecond-duration pulses originating from cosmological distances. Multi-wavelength counterparts associated with FRBs are important for unveiling their physical origins. Recent observations provide strong evidence that the sources of some active FRBs are residing in massive star binaries. In this paper, we study the electromagnetic counterparts of FRBs, including the persistent radio sources (PRSs) and the bow shock radiation from wind collisions for FRBs residing in magnetar - massive star binaries. We find that the PRSs with luminosity $10^{38}-10^{39}$ erg s$^{-1}$ can be generated by young magnetar wind nebulae (MWN). The age of magnetars is a few decades. The observed long-term variation of flux density for PRSs can be explained by the internal magnetic field decay of magnetars. The bow shock radiation can account for the less luminous PRS of FRB 20201124A. The multi-wavelength emission arising from synchrotron radiation and inverse-Compton scattering in the bow shock can be the electromagnetic counterpart of FRBs. The emission at keV, GeV and TeV bands from the binary model can be detected at the distances of $\sim10-100$ Mpc, $\sim 1-10$ Mpc and $\sim0.1$ Mpc by current instruments, respectively.

</details>


### [114] [Photometric and late-time spectropolarimetric observations of GRB 250129A afterglow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23359)
*A. Ghosh,S. Razzaque,J. Barnard,J. C. Joshi,R. Gupta,D. A. H. Buckley,B. van Soelen,N. Dukiya,A. Gupta,A. S. Moskvitin,J. Cooper,S. Chandra,K. M. Jayasurya,K. Misra,N. Rawat,L. Resmi,O. I. Spiridonova,R. I. Uklein*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: GRB 250129A的晚期(~19小时)光谱偏振观测显示微弱线偏振信号，无偏振角旋转，表明晚期偏振与喷流几何结构相关而非反向激波


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伽马射线暴余辉偏振观测是探测发射区磁场几何和结构的独特诊断工具，但晚期偏振现象罕见且机制不明确，需要观测验证

Method: 使用南非大望远镜(SALT)对GRB 250129A进行晚期(~19小时后)光谱偏振观测，结合多波段建模分析前向激波和反向激波贡献

Result: 观测到微弱线偏振信号(无偏振角旋转)，反向激波在~100秒后快速衰减，余辉发射最佳解释为高斯核心+翼结构的离轴喷流在均匀密度环境中演化

Conclusion: GRB 250129A提供了罕见的观测证据，将晚期偏振与喷流几何结构联系起来，表明偏振可作为喷流结构诊断工具而非仅反向激波指标

Abstract: Gamma-Ray Burst (GRB) afterglows arise from the interaction of relativistic ejecta with the circumburst medium and are observed across the electromagnetic spectrum. Afterglow polarisation is expected at early and late phases depending on the presence of reverse shocks (RS) and the observer's viewing geometry relative to the jet. Polarimetric observations of GRB afterglows provide a unique diagnostic tool to probe the geometry and structure of magnetic fields in the emitting region, which cannot be inferred from photometric or spectroscopic data alone. We report late-time (~19 hours post-burst) spectropolarimetric observations of GRB 250129A using the Southern African Large Telescope (SALT). The data reveal a hint of linear polarisation, with no evidence for rotation in the polarisation angle across wavelengths. Polarisation is typically expected during the early afterglow (<100 s) when the RS dominates. However, multi-wavelength modelling shows no indication of RS contribution at late times. Modelling incorporating both forward shock (FS) and RS components confirms that the RS fades rapidly after ~100 s. The afterglow emission is best explained by an off-axis viewing geometry of a jet with a Gaussian core and wings evolving in a uniform density environment. GRB 250129A thus provides rare observational evidence linking late-time polarisation to jet geometry and structure.

</details>


### [115] [Investigating peculiar prompt emission properties of the multi-Peaked GRB 250129A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23384)
*Ankur Ghosh,Tamador K. M. Aldowma,Soebur Razzaque*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对GRB 250129A的高能光谱分析显示其具有复杂多峰时间结构，最佳拟合为断折幂律模型，表明非热起源。光谱峰值能量与通量存在强度追踪行为，该暴位于长暴与超长暴之间的中间区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析GRB 250129A的光谱特性，研究其时间演化特征，探索其在伽马暴分类中的位置，特别是作为长暴与超长暴之间的过渡类型。

Method: 使用Swift-BAT触发数据，在15-150 keV能量范围内进行时间积分和时间分辨光谱分析。采用断折幂律模型拟合，使用贝叶斯块技术进行时间分辨分析，并检验Amati关系和硬度-强度相关性。

Result: 时间积分光谱分析显示断折幂律模型提供最佳拟合，表明非热起源。时间分辨分析显示主发射峰区间可用断折幂律描述，低计数区间约束较差。观测到通量与光谱峰值能量之间的强度追踪行为。Amati关系和硬度-强度相关性表明该暴位于长暴与超长暴之间的中间区域。

Conclusion: GRB 250129A表现出复杂的时间结构和非热光谱特征，其光谱演化显示强度追踪行为。该暴在伽马暴分类中占据中间位置，可作为连接长暴与超长暴的桥梁，为理解伽马暴的连续谱提供重要线索。

Abstract: We present a high-energy spectral analysis of GRB 250129A, which was triggered by the Swift-BAT. The burst exhibits a complex, multi-peaked temporal structure characterized by two distinct emission episodes, with the main peak occurring approximately 180 seconds after the BAT trigger. The time-integrated spectral analysis in the 15 - 150 keV energy range indicates that a broken power-law (BPL) model provides the best fit, signifying a non thermal origin of the prompt emission. A time resolved spectral analysis, performed using the Bayesian block technique, shows that the intervals around the main emission peak are well described by the BPL model, while the fits for low count intervals remain less constrained. An evident intensity tracking behavior is observed between the flux and the spectral peak energy (Ep). Furthermore, both the Amati relation and hardness - intensity correlation suggest that GRB~250129A occupies an intermediate regime, acting as a bridge between long and ultra long GRBs.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [ETH Flippers Approach to Parallel Reconfiguration of Triangulations: SAT formulation and Heuristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22456)
*Lorenzo Battini,Marko Milenković*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: ETH Flippers团队在CG:SHOP 2026挑战赛中采用混合策略：对小/中型实例使用SAT求解器获得精确解，对大型实例使用启发式算法，最终获得总排名第二、初级组第一的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决CG:SHOP 2026挑战赛中的三角剖分中心化问题，即在共同点集上找到使到输入三角剖分集合总并行翻转距离最小的中心三角剖分。

Method: 1. 精确方法：将问题建模为包含XOR子句的SAT实例，用于建模多轮边转换，并通过精确成对距离推导的下界进行优化；2. 启发式方法：对大型实例使用贪婪局部搜索和边着色技术识别最大独立翻转集合。

Result: 在250个实例中计算出了186个的证明最优解，最终排名为总排名第二、初级组第一。

Conclusion: 混合策略（精确求解器+启发式算法）在CG:SHOP 2026挑战赛中表现优异，证明了该方法在解决三角剖分中心化问题上的有效性。

Abstract: We describe the algorithms used by the ETH Flippers team in the CG:SHOP 2026 Challenge. Each instance consists of a set of triangulations on a common point set, and the objective is to find a central triangulation that minimizes the total parallel flip distance to the input set. Our strategy combines an exact solver for small and medium-sized instances with a suite of heuristics for larger instances. For the exact approach, we formulate the problem as a SAT instance with XOR clauses to model edge transitions across multiple rounds, further optimized by lower bounds derived from exact pairwise distances. For larger instances, we use a greedy local search and edge-coloring techniques to identify maximal sets of independent flips. Our approach ranked second overall and first in the junior category, computing provably optimal solutions for 186 out of 250 instances.

</details>


### [117] [Product Range Search Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22500)
*Oliver Chubet,Niyathi Kukkapalli,Anvi Kudaraya,Don Sheehy*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 提出两种用于倍增度量空间中近似乘积范围搜索的数据结构，基于贪婪树变体，分别通过扩展范围树和使用度量乘积构造单一贪婪树实现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统度量范围搜索只考虑单一度量，但在实际应用中经常需要同时满足两个不同度量的约束条件。乘积范围查询需要找到同时满足两个度量半径约束的点，即两个范围搜索的交集。这在实际应用中具有重要意义，但缺乏高效的数据结构支持。

Method: 提出两种基于贪婪树的数据结构：1）将计算几何中的范围树推广到倍增度量空间，用贪婪树替代二叉树的层次结构；2）直接在两个度量的乘积空间上构造单一贪婪树。贪婪树是网树的一种变体，能高效处理倍增度量空间中的近似范围搜索。

Result: 两种数据结构都能有效支持倍增度量空间中的近似乘积范围搜索。第一种方法通过层次化贪婪树结构实现，第二种方法通过度量乘积空间的单一贪婪树实现。两种方法都利用了贪婪树在倍增度量空间中的高效性。

Conclusion: 在倍增度量空间中，通过贪婪树变体可以构建高效的近似乘积范围搜索数据结构。两种方法各有优势，为同时满足多个度量约束的范围搜索问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Given a metric space, a standard metric range search, given a query $(q,r)$, finds all points within distance $r$ of the point $q$. Suppose now we have two different metrics $d_1$ and $d_2$. A product range query $(q, r_1, r_2)$ is a point $q$ and two radii $r_1$ and $r_2$. The output is all points within distance $r_1$ of $q$ with respect to $d_1$ and all points within $r_2$ of $q$ with respect to $d_2$. In other words, it is the intersection of two searches. We present two data structures for approximate product range search in doubling metrics. Both data structures use a net-tree variant, the greedy tree. The greedy tree is a data structure that can efficiently answer approximate range searches in doubling metrics. The first data structure is a generalization of the range tree from computational geometry using greedy trees rather than binary trees. The second data structure is a single greedy tree constructed on the product induced by the two metrics.

</details>


### [118] [Simple but not Simpler: A Surface-Sliding Method for Finding the Minimum Distance between Two Ellipsoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22683)
*Dariush Amirkhani,Junfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 提出一种计算两个椭球体最小距离的迭代方法，通过在参数空间中滑动表面点，利用连接线段张力引导方向，当线段垂直于两表面时达到最小距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种高效、稳定且几何意义明确的方法来计算两个椭球体之间的最小距离，这在计算机图形学、工程设计、材料建模和科学模拟等领域有广泛应用需求。

Method: 采用表面滑动方法：在theta-phi参数空间中维护两个表面点，利用连接线段张力作为滑动方向指导，迭代更新点位置直至线段垂直于两椭球体表面，此时达到最小距离。

Result: 方法表现出优异的数值性能：高精度、一致性、稳定性和鲁棒性。相比现有技术，具有几何表示清晰、公式简洁、算法简单、易于扩展到其他情况等优势。

Conclusion: 该表面滑动方法为计算椭球体最小距离提供了一种有效解决方案，有望在计算机图形学、工程设计、材料建模和科学模拟等领域的未来研究中发挥重要作用。

Abstract: We propose a novel iterative process to establish the minimum separation between two ellipsoids. The method maintains one point on each surface and updates their locations in the theta-phi parametric space. The tension along the connecting segment between the two surface points serves as the guidance for the sliding direction, and the distance between them decreases gradually. The minimum distance is established when the connecting segment becomes perpendicular to the ellipsoid surfaces, at which point the net effect of the segment tension disappears and the surface points no longer move. Demonstration examples are carefully designed, and excellent numerical performance is observed, including accuracy, consistency, stability, and robustness. Furthermore, compared to other existing techniques, this surface-sliding approach has several attractive features, such as clear geometric representation, concise formulation, a simple algorithm, and the potential to be extended straightforwardly to other situations. This method is expected to be useful for future studies in computer graphics, engineering design, material modeling, and scientific simulations.

</details>


### [119] [Linear time single-source shortest path algorithms in Euclidean graph classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22948)
*Joachim Gudmundsson,Yuan Sha,Sampson Wong*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 该论文为满足特定条件的欧几里得图类提供了线性时间单源最短路径算法，通过证明这些图类的收缩图具有亚线性分隔器来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Henzinger等人（1997）的平面图线性时间单源最短路径算法无法扩展到欧几里得图类，需要为满足特定条件的欧几里得图类开发类似的线性时间算法。

Method: 提出标准并证明满足这些标准的欧几里得图类具有线性时间单源最短路径算法，关键是通过证明这些图类的收缩图具有亚线性分隔器来实现。

Result: 建立了满足特定条件的欧几里得图类存在线性时间单源最短路径算法的理论框架，证明了这些图类的收缩图具有亚线性分隔器。

Conclusion: 为满足特定标准的欧几里得图类提供了线性时间单源最短路径算法，扩展了Henzinger等人对平面图的结果，填补了算法理论中的一个重要空白。

Abstract: In the celebrated paper of Henzinger, Klein, Rao and Subramanian (1997), it was shown that planar graphs admit a linear time single-source shortest path algorithm. Their algorithm unfortunately does not extend to Euclidean graph classes. We give criteria and prove that any Euclidean graph class satisfying the criteria admits a linear time single-source shortest path algorithm. As a main ingredient, we show that the contracted graphs of these Euclidean graph classes admit sublinear separators.

</details>


### [120] [Covering and Partitioning Complex Objects with Small Pieces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23216)
*Anders Aamand,Mikkel Abrahamsen,Reilly Browne,Mayank Goswami,Prahlad Narasimhan Kasthurirangan,Linda Kleist,Joseph S. B. Mitchell,Valentin Polishchuk,Jack Stade*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了用单位正方形内的小多边形覆盖或分割多边形（可能带洞）的最小数量问题，证明了覆盖与分割问题等价，在二维情况下提出了首个PTAS算法，在三维情况下证明了NP-hard近似下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多边形覆盖和分割问题，旨在找到用单位正方形内的小多边形覆盖或分割多边形的最小数量。这个问题在计算几何和实际应用中具有重要意义，但现有算法只有13-近似且仅适用于无洞多边形，需要更高效的算法和理论分析。

Method: 1. 证明覆盖和分割问题的等价性：最优覆盖和最优分割具有相同数量的片段。2. 在二维情况下，提出基于局部搜索的算法：重复尝试用k-1个片段替换候选覆盖中的k个片段，证明当无法进行此类交换时，覆盖是1+O(1/√k)-近似，从而获得首个PTAS。3. 在三维情况下，证明即使对于简单的多面体（亏格0且无洞），近似最优覆盖或分割也是NP-hard的，近似因子与n的对数相关。

Result: 1. 覆盖和分割问题等价，最优解具有相同数量片段。2. 在二维情况下获得了首个PTAS算法，近似比为1+O(1/√k)。3. 在三维情况下证明了NP-hard近似下界，近似因子与n的对数相关，即使对于简单多面体也是如此。

Conclusion: 该论文在二维多边形覆盖和分割问题上取得了重要理论突破，证明了问题等价性并提出了首个PTAS算法，同时在三维情况下建立了NP-hard近似下界，揭示了二维和三维问题在计算复杂度上的本质差异。

Abstract: We study the problems of covering or partitioning a polygon $P$ (possibly with holes) using a minimum number of small pieces, where a small piece is a connected sub-polygon contained in an axis-aligned unit square. For covering, we seek to write $P$ as a union of small pieces, and in partitioning, we furthermore require the pieces to be pairwise interior-disjoint. We show that these problems are in fact equivalent: Optimum covers and partitions have the same number of pieces.
  For covering, a natural local search algorithm repeatedly attempts to replace $k$ pieces from a candidate cover with $k-1$ pieces. In two dimensions and for sufficiently large $k$, we show that when no such swap is possible, the cover is a $1+O(1/\sqrt k)$-approximation, hence obtaining the first PTAS for the problem. Prior to our work, the only known algorithm was a $13$-approximation that only works for polygons without holes [Abrahamsen and Rasmussen, SODA 2025]. In contrast, in the three dimensional version of the problem, for a polyhedron $P$ of complexity $n$, we show that it is NP-hard to approximate an optimal cover or partition to within a factor that is logarithmic in $n$, even if $P$ is simple, i.e., has genus $0$ and no holes.

</details>


### [121] [Dynamic Light Spanners in Doubling Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23490)
*Sujoy Bhore,Jonathan Conroy,Arnold Filtser*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在常数倍维度量空间中维护动态点集轻量级(1+ε)-spanner的高效算法，更新时间为poly(log Φ)


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏在动态点集（支持插入删除）上维护轻量级spanner的高效算法，即使在低维欧氏空间中也没有已知解决方案

Method: 针对常数倍维度量空间中的动态点集，设计了一种维护(1+ε)-spanner的算法，保持总权重在最小生成树权重的常数倍内

Result: 实现了每个更新操作（插入或删除）在poly(log Φ)时间内完成，其中Φ是点集的纵横比，这是首个高效的动态轻量级spanner维护算法

Conclusion: 该工作填补了动态轻量级spanner维护算法的空白，为常数倍维度量空间中的动态点集提供了首个高效解决方案

Abstract: A $t$-spanner of a point set $X$ in a metric space $(\mathcal{X}, δ)$ is a graph $G$ with vertex set $P$ such that, for any pair of points $u,v \in X$, the distance between $u$ and $v$ in $G$ is at most $t$ times $δ(u,v)$. We study the problem of maintaining a spanner for a dynamic point set $X$ -- that is, when $X$ undergoes a sequence of insertions and deletions -- in a metric space of constant doubling dimension. For any constant $\varepsilon>0$, we maintain a $(1+\varepsilon)$-spanner of $P$ whose total weight remains within a constant factor of the weight of the minimum spanning tree of $X$. Each update (insertion or deletion) can be performed in $\operatorname{poly}(\log Φ)$ time, where $Φ$ denotes the aspect ratio of $X$. Prior to our work, no efficient dynamic algorithm for maintaining a light-weight spanner was known even for point sets in low-dimensional Euclidean space.

</details>
